# List 5

## Unit 1

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>compound</b></td><td>/kəmˈpaʊnd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>混合，合成；使恶化</td><td>compound a medicine<br>配制药剂</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The pharmacist compounded an ointment from three simple ingredients.  药剂师用三种简单原料配制出一种药膏。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 admixture</td><td>/ædˈmɪkstʃər/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>混合物，掺和料</td><td>an admixture of clay<br>掺入的黏土</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The concrete contains an admixture that slows the drying process.  这种混凝土含有一种延缓干燥的掺和料。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 alloy</td><td>/ˈæˌlɔɪ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>合金；使（品质）降低，掺杂</td><td>an alloy of copper and tin<br>铜锡合金</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Bronze is an alloy of copper and tin used since ancient times.  青铜是自古就使用的铜锡合金。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 amalgam</td><td>/əˈmælɡəm/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>混合物；汞合金</td><td>an amalgam of styles<br>多种风格的混合</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her music is an amalgam of jazz and traditional folk song.  她的音乐是爵士与传统民歌的混合。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 amalgamation</td><td>/əˌmælɡəˈmeɪʃən/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>合并，混合</td><td>the amalgamation of firms<br>公司的合并</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The amalgamation of the two unions created a powerful bloc.  两个工会的合并形成了一个强大的集团。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cocktail</td><td>/ˈkɑːkˌteɪl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>混合物；鸡尾酒</td><td>a cocktail of drugs<br>多种药物的混合</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Patients receive a cocktail of three drugs each morning.  病人每天早上服用三种药物的混合剂。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 combination</td><td>/ˌkɑːmbəˈneɪʃən/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>结合，组合，混合</td><td>a winning combination<br>成功的组合</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Success came from a combination of luck and careful planning.  成功来自运气与周密计划的结合。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 composite</td><td>/kəmˈpɑːzət/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>合成的；合成物</td><td>a composite picture<br>合成的图像</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The artist built a composite portrait from four separate photographs.  艺术家用四张照片合成了一幅肖像。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fusion</td><td>/ˈfjuːʒən/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>融合，结合；熔合</td><td>a fusion of cultures<br>文化的融合</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their cooking is a fusion of Korean and Peruvian traditions.  他们的烹饪是韩国与秘鲁传统的融合。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 intermixture</del></td><td>/ˌɪntərˈmɪkstʃər/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>混合，混合物</td><td>an intermixture of peoples<br>各族群的混居</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The port city grew from an intermixture of many trading peoples.  这座港城由许多贸易族群混居发展而来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 meld</td><td>/ˈmeld/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>融合，合并</td><td>meld into one<br>融为一体</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The choir's voices melded into a single warm sound.  合唱团的声音融合成一种温暖的声响。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mix</td><td>/ˈmɪks/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>混合，掺和</td><td>mix the ingredients<br>把配料混合</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Mix the flour and water until the dough becomes smooth.  把面粉和水混合，直到面团变得光滑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 conflation</td><td>/kənˈfleɪʃən/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>合并，混同</td><td>a conflation of two ideas<br>两种观念的混同</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The essay suffers from a conflation of law and morality.  这篇文章的问题在于把法律与道德混为一谈。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 synthesis</td><td>/ˈsɪnθəsɪs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>合成，综合</td><td>a synthesis of ideas<br>各种思想的综合</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His theory is a synthesis of ideas drawn from several disciplines.  他的理论是多个学科思想的综合。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 amalgamated</td><td>/əˈmælɡəˌmeɪtɪd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>合并的，混合的</td><td>an amalgamated union<br>合并后的工会</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The amalgamated company now employs six thousand people.  合并后的公司现有六千名员工。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 chain</td><td>/ˈtʃeɪn/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>连接，用链系住；链条</td><td>chain the parts together<br>把各部分连起来</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He chained his bicycle to the railing outside the shop.  他把自行车锁在店外的栏杆上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 conjugate</td><td>/ˈkɑːndʒəɡeɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词、形容词</td><td>结合，配对；成对的</td><td>conjugate two molecules<br>使两个分子结合</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Researchers conjugate the drug to an antibody for precise delivery.  研究者把药物与抗体结合以实现精准投递。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 couple</td><td>/ˈkʌpəl/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>连接，结合；一对</td><td>couple the two carriages<br>连接两节车厢</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Workers coupled the new carriage to the waiting train.  工人把新车厢挂到等候的列车上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hook</td><td>/ˈhʊk/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>钩住，连接；钩子</td><td>hook the trailer on<br>把拖车挂上</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She hooked the two cables together before starting the engine.  她把两根电缆连接好才启动发动机。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 interconnect</td><td>/ˌɪntərkəˈnekt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>互相连接</td><td>interconnect the networks<br>使各网络互连</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Bridges interconnect the islands so the ferry is no longer needed.  桥梁把各岛连接起来，渡船已无必要。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 interlink</td><td>/ˌɪntərˈlɪŋk/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>使连锁，互相连接</td><td>interlink the systems<br>使各系统相连</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two railways interlink at a single mountain station.  两条铁路在一个山间车站相连。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 join</td><td>/ˈdʒɔɪn/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>连接，接合；加入</td><td>join the two pieces<br>把两块接起来</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Join the ends of the rope with a firm knot.  用牢固的结把绳子两端接起来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 link</td><td>/ˈlɪŋk/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>联系，纽带；连接</td><td>a close link<br>紧密的联系</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Researchers found a close link between sleep and memory.  研究者发现睡眠与记忆之间存在紧密联系。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 yoke</td><td>/joʊk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>使结合，联结；给…上轭</td><td>yoke two oxen together<br>把两头牛套在一起</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The farmer yoked two oxen together to pull the heavy plow.  农夫把两头牛套在一起拉那沉重的犁。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 aggravate</td><td>/ˈæɡrəveɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>加重，恶化；激怒</td><td>aggravate the situation<br>使局势恶化</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Running on the injury will only aggravate it.  带着伤跑步只会让伤势加重。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 complicate</td><td>/ˈkɑːmplɪkeɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使复杂化</td><td>complicate matters further<br>使事情更复杂</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 New evidence complicated the investigation and delayed the final report.  新证据使调查复杂化，并推迟了最终报告。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 exacerbate</td><td>/ɪɡˈzæsərbeɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使加剧，使恶化</td><td>exacerbate the crisis<br>使危机加剧</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Cutting the budget would only exacerbate the housing shortage.  削减预算只会使住房短缺进一步恶化。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 intensify</td><td>/ɪnˈtensɪfaɪ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>加强，激化</td><td>intensify the pressure<br>加大压力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The rain intensified just as we reached the summit.  我们刚到山顶，雨就更大了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 worsen</td><td>/ˈwɜːrsən/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>使变坏，恶化</td><td>worsen over time<br>随时间恶化</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her cough worsened after a week outdoors in the cold.  在寒冷的户外待了一周后，她的咳嗽加重了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 noncompound</del></td><td>/ˌnɑnˈkɑmpaʊnd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>非复合的，单一成分的</td><td>a noncompound substance<br>非复合物质</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Salt is a compound, but a noncompound element like gold stands alone.  盐是化合物，而像金这样的非复合元素独立存在。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 unhitch</del></td><td>/ənˈhɪtʃ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>解开，卸下（挂钩的东西）</td><td>unhitch the trailer<br>把拖车解下来</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The farmer stopped to unhitch the horse from the loaded cart.  农夫停下来把马从装满货的车上卸下来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 unyoke</del></td><td>/ʌnˈjoʊk/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>解开轭；使分离，解脱</td><td>unyoke the oxen<br>给牛解轭</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 At dusk the workers unyoked the oxen and led them to water.  黄昏时工人给牛解开轭，牵它们去饮水。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 allay</td><td>/əˈleɪ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>减轻，平息</td><td>allay public fears<br>平息公众恐慌</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The statement was meant to allay concerns about safety.  这份声明意在减轻人们对安全的担忧。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 alleviate</td><td>/əˈliːvieɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>缓和，减轻</td><td>alleviate the pain<br>减轻疼痛</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A warm bath helped alleviate the soreness in his back.  热水澡帮他缓解了背部的酸痛。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 assuage</td><td>/əˈsweɪdʒ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>缓和，减轻；平息</td><td>assuage his fears<br>减轻他的恐惧</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The mayor's speech did little to assuage public anger.  市长的演讲几乎没能平息公众的怒火。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 mitigate</td><td>/ˈmɪtɪɡeɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>减轻，使缓和</td><td>mitigate the damage<br>减轻损害</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Planting trees can mitigate the effects of summer heat.  种树可以减轻夏季高温的影响。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 mollify</td><td>/ˈmɑːlɪfaɪ/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>平息，抚慰，缓和</td><td>mollify an angry customer<br>安抚愤怒的顾客</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A sincere apology from the manager mollified the furious client.  经理诚挚的道歉安抚了这位愤怒的客户。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 palliate</del></td><td>/ˈpælieɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>减轻，缓和；掩饰</td><td>palliate the symptoms<br>缓解症状</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new drug can only palliate the pain, not cure the disease.  这种新药只能减轻疼痛，无法治愈疾病。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 relieve</td><td>/rɪˈliːv/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>减轻，缓解；换班替换</td><td>relieve the pressure<br>缓解压力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A short walk relieved the tension in her shoulders.  短暂散步缓解了她肩膀的紧张。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 soothe</td><td>/suːð/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>安慰，抚慰；减轻</td><td>soothe a crying baby<br>安抚哭闹的婴儿</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Soft music helped soothe the restless patient through the long night.  轻柔的音乐帮助安抚了焦躁的病人度过漫长的夜晚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>compress</b></td><td>/kəmˈpres/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>压缩，压紧</td><td>compress the file<br>压缩文件</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 You should compress the file before sending it by email.  用邮件发送之前你应该压缩这个文件。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 capsule</td><td>/ˈkæpsəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>胶囊；压缩的，简要的</td><td>a capsule summary<br>简要概述</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The editor asked for a capsule version of the long report.  编辑要一份长篇报告的浓缩版本。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 collapse</td><td>/kəˈlæps/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>失败</td><td>the collapse of talks<br>谈判的破裂</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The collapse of the talks surprised nobody in the capital.  谈判的破裂在首都没让任何人感到意外。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 compact</td><td>/ˈkɑːmpækt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>契约，协议；紧凑的</td><td>sign a compact<br>签订协议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two nations signed a compact to protect the shared river.  两国签订协议保护共有的河流。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 condense</td><td>/kənˈdens/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>压缩，缩短；使凝结</td><td>condense the article<br>压缩这篇文章</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She managed to condense three chapters into a single clear page.  她把三章内容压缩成清晰的一页。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 constrict</td><td>/kənsˈtrɪkt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>收缩，压缩；限制</td><td>constrict blood vessels<br>使血管收缩</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Cold air constricts the blood vessels near the skin's surface.  冷空气使皮肤表层的血管收缩。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 constringe</del></td><td>/kənˈstrɪndʒ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使紧缩</td><td>constringe the tissue<br>使组织紧缩</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The cold compress constringed the swollen tissue around the wound.  冷敷使伤口周围肿胀的组织收缩了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 contract</td><td>/ˈkɑːnˌtrækt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>契约，合同；收缩；感染疾病</td><td>sign a contract<br>签订合同</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Both parties signed the contract without reading the final clause carefully.  双方都没有仔细读最后一条就签了合同。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 shrink</td><td>/ʃrɪŋk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>（在数量或价值方面）降低，减小</td><td>shrink in value<br>价值缩水</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The value of the old coins began to shrink after the scandal.  丑闻之后，那些旧币的价值开始缩水。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 telescope</td><td>/ˈteləˌskoʊp/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>望远镜；压缩，缩短</td><td>telescope the schedule<br>压缩日程</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The director telescoped the novel's events into a two-hour film.  导演把小说的情节压缩成两小时的电影。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 increase in volume</td><td>/ˈɪnkris ɪn ˈvɑljum/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>体积增大</td><td>a sharp increase in volume<br>体积急剧增大</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Freezing water shows a surprising increase in volume inside the pipe.  水结冰时在管道内体积会明显增大。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 decompress</td><td>/ˌdikəmˈprɛs/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>解压缩；减压</td><td>decompress the archive<br>解压压缩包</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 You must decompress the archive before the program can read it.  必须先解压压缩包，程序才能读取。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 expand</td><td>/ɪkˈspænd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>扩大，扩张；详述</td><td>expand the business<br>扩大业务</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company plans to expand into three new markets.  公司计划扩张到三个新市场。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 balloon</td><td>/bəˈluːn/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>迅速增加</td><td>costs have ballooned<br>成本迅速膨胀</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Repair costs ballooned from two thousand to twenty thousand dollars.  维修费用从两千美元猛增到两万美元。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>compromise</b></td><td>/ˈkɑːmprəmaɪz/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>妥协，折中；危害</td><td>reach a compromise<br>达成妥协</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After long debate the committee reached a workable compromise.  经过长时间辩论，委员会达成了可行的妥协。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 accommodation</td><td>/əˌkɑːməˈdeɪʃən/</td><td>★★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>迁就，通融；住处</td><td>reach an accommodation<br>达成通融安排</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two sides finally reached an accommodation over water rights.  双方最终就水权问题达成了通融。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 negotiation</td><td>/nɪˌɡoʊʃiˈeɪʃən/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>谈判，协商</td><td>enter into negotiation<br>开始谈判</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Months of quiet negotiation produced a treaty both nations accepted.  数月的低调谈判促成了两国都接受的条约。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hazard</td><td>/ˈhæzərd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>危险，危害；使遭危险</td><td>a fire hazard<br>火灾隐患</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Loose wiring in the basement is a serious fire hazard.  地下室松脱的电线是严重的火灾隐患。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 imperil</td><td>/ˌɪmˈperəl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使处于危险，危及</td><td>imperil public safety<br>危及公共安全</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Cutting the inspections would imperil the safety of every passenger.  削减检查会危及每位乘客的安全。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 jeopardize</td><td>/ˈdʒepərˌdaɪz/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>危及，损害</td><td>jeopardize the deal<br>危及这笔交易</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 One careless remark could jeopardize years of patient diplomacy.  一句不慎的话可能危及多年耐心的外交努力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 menace</td><td>/ˈmenəs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>威胁，使处于危险</td><td>menace world peace<br>威胁世界和平</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Rising sea levels menace coastal villages that have stood for hundreds of years.  海平面上升威胁着屹立数百年的沿海村庄。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 peril</td><td>/ˈperəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>危险，风险</td><td>at your peril<br>冒着危险</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old bridge put every crossing traveler in real peril.  那座旧桥让每个通行的旅人都身处险境。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 venture</td><td>/ˈventʃər/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>冒险；冒险事业</td><td>a risky venture<br>一项冒险的事业</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He ventured his whole savings on an untested new product.  他把全部积蓄冒险投在一款未经检验的新产品上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>compunction</b></td><td>/kəmˈpʌŋkʃn/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>内疚，良心不安</td><td>without compunction<br>毫无内疚地</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She fired the loyal staff without the slightest compunction.  她毫无内疚地解雇了那些忠诚的员工。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 anxiety</td><td>/æŋˈzaɪəti/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>焦虑，不安；担忧</td><td>a source of anxiety<br>焦虑的根源</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Waiting for the test results caused her weeks of anxiety.  等待检查结果让她焦虑了好几周。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 misgiving</td><td>/ˌmɪsˈɡɪvɪŋ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>担忧，疑虑</td><td>have serious misgivings<br>深感疑虑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She had serious misgivings about signing the contract without a lawyer present.  她对在没有律师在场的情况下签合同深感疑虑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 scruple</td><td>/ˈskruːpl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>（良心上的）不安</td><td>have no scruples<br>毫无顾忌</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He felt no scruple about reading the private letters of his brother.  他对偷看弟弟的私人信件毫无良心不安。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 absence of misgiving</td><td>/ˈæbsəns ʌv mɪsˈɡɪvɪŋ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>毫无疑虑，坦然无忧</td><td>a striking absence of misgiving<br>出奇地毫无疑虑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His total absence of misgiving about the plan worried the older advisers.  他对该计划毫无疑虑，这让年长顾问们担心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>concatenate</b></td><td>/kənˈkætɪneɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>连接，串联</td><td>concatenate two strings<br>连接两个字符串</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The program concatenates two strings into a single long line.  该程序把两个字符串连接成一行长文本。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 catenate</del></td><td>/ˈkætəneɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>连成链状，串联</td><td>catenate the units<br>把各单元串联起来</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The chemist catenated the small molecules into one long chain.  化学家把小分子串联成一条长链。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 chain</td><td>/ˈtʃeɪn/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>连接，用链系住；链条</td><td>chain the parts together<br>把各部分连起来</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He chained his bicycle to the railing outside the shop.  他把自行车锁在店外的栏杆上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 couple</td><td>/ˈkʌpəl/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>连接，结合；一对</td><td>couple the two carriages<br>连接两节车厢</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Workers coupled the new carriage to the waiting train.  工人把新车厢挂到等候的列车上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hook</td><td>/ˈhʊk/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>钩住，连接；钩子</td><td>hook the trailer on<br>把拖车挂上</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She hooked the two cables together before starting the engine.  她把两根电缆连接好才启动发动机。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 interconnect</td><td>/ˌɪntərkəˈnekt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>互相连接</td><td>interconnect the networks<br>使各网络互连</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Bridges interconnect the islands so the ferry is no longer needed.  桥梁把各岛连接起来，渡船已无必要。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 interlink</td><td>/ˌɪntərˈlɪŋk/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>使连锁，互相连接</td><td>interlink the systems<br>使各系统相连</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two railways interlink at a single mountain station.  两条铁路在一个山间车站相连。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 join</td><td>/ˈdʒɔɪn/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>连接，接合；加入</td><td>join the two pieces<br>把两块接起来</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Join the ends of the rope with a firm knot.  用牢固的结把绳子两端接起来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 link</td><td>/ˈlɪŋk/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>联系，纽带；连接</td><td>a close link<br>紧密的联系</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Researchers found a close link between sleep and memory.  研究者发现睡眠与记忆之间存在紧密联系。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 yoke</td><td>/joʊk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>使结合，联结；给…上轭</td><td>yoke two oxen together<br>把两头牛套在一起</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The farmer yoked two oxen together to pull the heavy plow.  农夫把两头牛套在一起拉那沉重的犁。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 disconnect</td><td>/dɪskəˈnekt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>断开，使分离</td><td>disconnect the power<br>切断电源</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Please disconnect the printer before you open its back panel.  打开后盖前请先断开打印机的连接。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 disjoin</del></td><td>/dɪsˈdʒɔɪn/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>使分开，使分离</td><td>disjoin the two parts<br>把两部分分开</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The engineer had to disjoin the pipes to replace a valve.  工程师得把管子分开才能更换阀门。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 disjoint</td><td>/dɪsˈdʒɔɪnt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>动词、形容词</td><td>使脱节；不连贯的</td><td>disjoint the sections<br>使各部分脱节</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Careless editing disjointed the argument and confused every reader.  草率的编辑使论证脱节，让每位读者都感到困惑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 dissever</del></td><td>/dɪˈsevər/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>分开，切断</td><td>dissever the two halves<br>把两半分开</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The war dissevered families that had lived together for generations.  战争把世代同住的家庭分割开来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 disunite</del></td><td>/ˌdɪsjuˈnaɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>使分裂，使不团结</td><td>disunite the party<br>使党派分裂</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The tax proposal disunited a coalition that had held for years.  这项税收提案使维持多年的联盟分裂了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 separate</td><td>/ˈsepəreɪt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>动词、形容词</td><td>分开，分离</td><td>separate the yolks<br>把蛋黄分离出来</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Please separate the recyclable waste from the ordinary rubbish.  请把可回收垃圾与普通垃圾分开。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unchain</td><td>/ʌnˈtʃeɪn/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>解开锁链，释放</td><td>unchain the dog<br>把狗解开链子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The keeper unchained the old elephant and let it wander freely.  饲养员解开老象的链子，让它自由走动。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 uncouple</td><td>/ənˈkʌpəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>解开连接，使分离</td><td>uncouple the carriages<br>把车厢脱开</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Workers uncoupled the rear carriages before the train left the yard.  火车离开车场前工人把后面几节车厢脱开。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 unhitch</del></td><td>/ənˈhɪtʃ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>解开，卸下（挂钩的东西）</td><td>unhitch the trailer<br>把拖车解下来</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The farmer stopped to unhitch the horse from the loaded cart.  农夫停下来把马从装满货的车上卸下来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 unyoke</del></td><td>/ʌnˈjoʊk/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>解开轭；使分离，解脱</td><td>unyoke the oxen<br>给牛解轭</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 At dusk the workers unyoked the oxen and led them to water.  黄昏时工人给牛解开轭，牵它们去饮水。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>concoct</b></td><td>/kənˈkɑːkt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>捏造，编造</td><td>concoct a story<br>编造一个故事</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The prisoner concocted an elaborate story to win a lighter sentence.  囚犯编造了一个精心的故事以求减轻刑罚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 invent</td><td>/ˌɪnˈvent/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>发明；编造</td><td>invent an excuse<br>编造借口</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He invented a clumsy excuse for missing the morning meeting.  他为缺席早会编了个拙劣的借口。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 construct</td><td>/kənˈstrʌkt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>建造，建立；创造，想出</td><td>construct a theory<br>构建一套理论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The lawyer constructed a careful argument from a handful of ordinary documents.  律师用少量普通文件构建出一套严密的论证。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 devise</td><td>/dɪˈvaɪz/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>想出，设计，策划</td><td>devise a plan<br>想出一个计划</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The team devised a clever method for testing the old samples.  团队想出了检验旧样本的巧妙方法。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fabricate</td><td>/ˈfæbrɪkeɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>捏造</td><td>fabricate evidence<br>伪造证据</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The witness had fabricated evidence to protect his own brother.  那名证人捏造证据来保护自己的兄弟。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 make up</td><td>/meɪk ˈʌp/</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>构成，组成；编造</td><td>make up the whole<br>构成整体</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Women make up more than half of the entering class.  女生占新生班级的一半以上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 manufacture</td><td>/ˌmænjəˈfæktʃər/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>制造；编造，虚构</td><td>manufacture an alibi<br>编造不在场证明</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The witness had manufactured an alibi that fell apart quickly.  证人编造的不在场证明很快就站不住脚了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>conceal</b></td><td>/kənˈsiːl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>隐藏，隐瞒</td><td>conceal the truth<br>隐瞒真相</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The witness tried to conceal the truth from the police.  证人试图向警方隐瞒真相。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cache</td><td>/kæʃ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>囤货，藏货</td><td>a cache of weapons<br>一批藏匿的武器</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Police discovered a cache of weapons beneath the barn floor.  警方在谷仓地板下发现了一批藏匿的武器。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 secrete</td><td>/sɪˈkriːt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>隐藏；分泌</td><td>secrete the documents<br>把文件藏起来</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The driver had secreted the documents beneath the front seat.  司机把文件藏在了前排座椅下面。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 disguise</td><td>/dɪsˈɡaɪz/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>伪装，掩饰；伪装</td><td>disguise his intentions<br>掩饰他的意图</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He disguised his disappointment behind a polite and steady smile.  他用礼貌平静的微笑掩饰了失望。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mask</td><td>/ˈmæsk/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>掩盖，掩饰；面具</td><td>mask his anger<br>掩饰他的怒气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Loud music masked the sound of the approaching helicopter.  响亮的音乐掩盖了直升机靠近的声音。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 occult</td><td>/əˈkʌlt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>难以理解的</td><td>occult knowledge<br>玄奥的知识</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The manuscript is filled with occult knowledge that scholars still cannot decipher.  这份手稿满是学者至今无法解读的玄奥知识。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 ensconce</del></td><td>/ɪnˈskɑːns/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>安置，安顿；隐藏</td><td>ensconce oneself in<br>把自己安置在</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She ensconced herself in the armchair nearest the fire and opened her book.  她把自己安置在离火最近的扶手椅里，打开了书。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 display</td><td>/dɪsˈpleɪ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>展示，陈列；显露</td><td>display the paintings<br>展出这些画作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The museum displays its rarest coins in a locked glass case.  博物馆把最珍稀的钱币陈列在上锁的玻璃柜里。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 exhibit</td><td>/ɪɡˈzɪbɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>展出；显示，表现出</td><td>exhibit great courage<br>表现出极大勇气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Young children often exhibit no fear of strangers at all.  幼童常常对陌生人完全不表现出畏惧。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>concede</b></td><td>/kənˈsiːd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>承认</td><td>concede the point<br>承认这一点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She finally conceded that her opponent had made a fair point.  她最终承认对手说得有道理。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 acknowledge</td><td>/ækˈnɑːlɪdʒ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>承认；对…表示感谢</td><td>acknowledge the mistake<br>承认错误</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The mayor finally acknowledged that the report contained serious errors.  市长最终承认那份报告有严重错误。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 grant</td><td>/ˈɡrænt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>承认；授予，同意给予</td><td>grant that he is right<br>承认他是对的</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 I grant that the plan has some obvious weaknesses.  我承认这个计划有些明显的弱点。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 confess</td><td>/kənˈfes/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>承认，坦白，供认</td><td>confess to the crime<br>供认罪行</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After hours of questioning he confessed to taking the money.  盘问数小时后他承认拿了那笔钱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 refuse to grant</td><td>/rɪˈfjuz tə ɡrænt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>拒绝给予，不肯准许</td><td>refuse to grant permission<br>拒绝给予许可</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The board chose to refuse to grant the extra funding.  董事会决定拒绝给予额外资金。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 deny</td><td>/dɪˈnaɪ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>否认；拒绝给予</td><td>flatly deny the charge<br>断然否认指控</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 I do not deny that the plan carries some very real risks.  我不否认这个计划确实带有一些实实在在的风险。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 aggression</td><td>/əˈɡreʃən/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>侵略，侵犯；攻击性</td><td>an act of aggression<br>侵略行为</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The border raid was widely condemned as naked aggression.  这次边境突袭被广泛谴责为赤裸裸的侵略。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>concentrate</b></td><td>/ˈkɑːnsntreɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>浓缩；聚集</td><td>concentrate the solution<br>浓缩溶液</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Boil the syrup slowly in order to concentrate the solution.  慢慢熬煮糖浆以便浓缩溶液。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 condense</td><td>/kənˈdens/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>压缩，缩短；使凝结</td><td>condense the article<br>压缩这篇文章</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She managed to condense three chapters into a single clear page.  她把三章内容压缩成清晰的一页。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 accumulate</td><td>/əˈkjuːmjəleɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>不及物动词、及物动词</td><td>逐渐增长，积累</td><td>accumulate wealth<br>积累财富</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Dust had accumulated on the shelves during our absence.  我们离开期间架子上积了灰。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 amass</td><td>/əˈmæs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>积累，聚集（大量）</td><td>amass a fortune<br>积累财富</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Over thirty years she amassed an astonishing collection of maps.  三十多年间她积累了数量惊人的地图收藏。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 assemble</td><td>/əˈsembəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>聚集，集合；组装</td><td>assemble a crowd<br>聚集起人群</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Villagers assembled in the square to hear the announcement.  村民聚集在广场上听那则公告。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 collect</td><td>/kəˈlekt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>收集，聚集，收取</td><td>collect rainwater<br>收集雨水</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Dust collects quickly on shelves in this dry climate.  在这种干燥气候里灰尘很快就积在架子上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 congregate</td><td>/ˈkɑːŋɡrəˌɡeɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>聚集，集合</td><td>congregate in the hall<br>聚集在大厅里</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Students congregate near the fountain between morning classes.  上午课间学生们聚集在喷泉附近。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 garner</td><td>/ˈɡɑːrnər/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>获得，积累</td><td>garner support<br>获得支持</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The proposal garnered support from both major parties.  该提案获得了两大党的支持。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 dilute</td><td>/daɪˈluːt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词、形容词</td><td>稀释，冲淡；经稀释的</td><td>dilute the acid<br>稀释这种酸</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Please dilute the concentrated juice with three parts water before serving it.  上桌前请用三倍的水稀释这种浓缩果汁。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 water down</td><td>/ˌwɔːtər ˈdaʊn/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>掺水稀释；削弱</td><td>water down a proposal<br>弱化一项提案</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Lawmakers watered down the bill to win more votes.  议员们削弱了这项法案的力度以争取更多选票。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 dispel</td><td>/dɪˈspel/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>驱散，消除（疑虑）</td><td>dispel the rumors<br>消除谣言</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Clear figures from the auditor dispelled most of the doubts.  审计师提供的清晰数据消除了大部分疑虑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 disperse</td><td>/dɪˈspɜːrs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>分散，散开，驱散</td><td>disperse the crowd<br>驱散人群</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Police asked the protesters to disperse before nightfall.  警方要求抗议者在天黑前散开。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 dissipate</td><td>/ˈdɪsɪpeɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词、不及物动词</td><td>驱散，消散；浪费</td><td>dissipate the fog<br>驱散雾气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The morning mist dissipated as soon as the sun rose.  太阳一升起，晨雾就消散了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 scatter</td><td>/ˈskætər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>散开，四散；撒播</td><td>scatter the seeds<br>把种子撒开</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The birds scattered the moment the gardener opened the gate.  园丁一开门，鸟就四散飞走了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 break up</td><td>/breɪk ʌp/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>分开，分裂；解散</td><td>break up the group<br>拆散这个团体</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Police arrived quickly to break up the noisy crowd.  警察迅速赶到驱散嘈杂的人群。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 disband</td><td>/dɪsˈbænd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>解散，遣散</td><td>disband the committee<br>解散委员会</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The band disbanded after their final concert last year.  乐队在去年最后一场演唱会后解散。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>concerted</b></td><td>/kənˈsɜːrtɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>共同完成的</td><td>a concerted effort<br>共同的努力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A concerted effort by volunteers cleaned the river in a week.  志愿者的共同努力一周内清理了这条河。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 collaborative</td><td>/kəˈlæbəˌreɪtɪv/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>合作的，协作的</td><td>a collaborative project<br>一个合作项目</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The paper was the product of a long collaborative study.  这篇论文是一项长期合作研究的成果。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 combined</td><td>/kəmˈbaɪnd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>联合的，共同的，合计的</td><td>combined efforts<br>共同的努力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their combined savings were still not enough for the house.  他们的存款合起来仍不够买房。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cooperative</td><td>/koʊˈɑːpərətɪv/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>合作的，配合的；合作社</td><td>a cooperative attitude<br>配合的态度</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The witness was polite and unusually cooperative with investigators.  这名证人礼貌，而且异常配合调查人员。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 united</td><td>/juˈnaɪtɪd/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>联合的，团结一致的</td><td>a united front<br>统一战线</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The villages stayed united against the plan to divert the river.  各村庄团结一致反对改道河流的计划。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 separate</td><td>/ˈsepəreɪt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>动词、形容词</td><td>分开，分离</td><td>separate the yolks<br>把蛋黄分离出来</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Please separate the recyclable waste from the ordinary rubbish.  请把可回收垃圾与普通垃圾分开。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 2

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>conciliate</b></td><td>/kənˈsɪlieɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>安抚，平息；调解</td><td>conciliate angry customers<br>安抚愤怒的顾客</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The manager tried to conciliate the angry customers with refunds.  经理试图用退款来安抚愤怒的顾客。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 appease</td><td>/əˈpiːz/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>安抚，平息；满足</td><td>appease the angry crowd<br>安抚愤怒的人群</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company offered refunds to appease its angry customers.  公司提供退款以安抚愤怒的顾客。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pacify</td><td>/ˈpæsɪfaɪ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使平静，安慰</td><td>pacify a crying baby<br>安抚哭闹的婴儿</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The nurse sang softly to pacify the frightened child before the injection.  打针前，护士轻声唱歌安抚受惊的孩子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 assuage</td><td>/əˈsweɪdʒ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>缓和，减轻；平息</td><td>assuage his fears<br>减轻他的恐惧</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The mayor's speech did little to assuage public anger.  市长的演讲几乎没能平息公众的怒火。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mollify</td><td>/ˈmɑːlɪfaɪ/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>平息，抚慰，缓和</td><td>mollify an angry customer<br>安抚愤怒的顾客</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A sincere apology from the manager mollified the furious client.  经理诚挚的道歉安抚了这位愤怒的客户。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 placate</td><td>/ˈpleɪkeɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>（通过让步以）平息抚慰</td><td>placate the critics<br>平息批评者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company offered full refunds to placate its angriest customers.  公司提供全额退款以平息最愤怒的客户。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 propitiate</td><td>/prəˈpɪʃieɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>抚慰，劝解</td><td>propitiate the angry gods<br>抚慰愤怒的神灵</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Villagers offered rice and flowers to propitiate the mountain spirits.  村民献上米和花来抚慰山神。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 calm</td><td>/ˈkɑːm/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词、动词、名词</td><td>平静的；使镇静</td><td>calm down quickly<br>很快平静下来</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She spoke in a calm voice until the children settled.  她用平静的声音说话，直到孩子们安静下来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 annoy</td><td>/əˈnɔɪ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使恼怒，不断烦扰</td><td>annoy the neighbors<br>不断打扰邻居</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The barking dog began to annoy every neighbor on the quiet street.  那只不停犬吠的狗开始烦扰这条安静街上的每位邻居。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 enrage</td><td>/enˈreɪdʒ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使愤怒，激怒</td><td>enrage the audience<br>激怒观众</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His flippant apology only enraged the families further.  他轻率的道歉只让那些家庭更加愤怒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 vex</td><td>/veks/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使烦恼，使恼怒</td><td>vex the teacher<br>使老师恼怒</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Constant interruptions vexed the teacher throughout the long afternoon lesson.  不断的打扰让老师在漫长的下午课上十分恼怒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 nettle</td><td>/ˈnetl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>惹怒</td><td>nettled by criticism<br>被批评惹怒</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She was clearly nettled by the reporter's rude question.  记者无礼的提问显然惹恼了她。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 rile</td><td>/raɪl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>刺激，惹怒</td><td>rile up the crowd<br>激怒人群</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Nothing riles him more than being interrupted in the middle of a sentence.  没有什么比话说到一半被打断更让他恼火。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 incense</td><td>/ɪnˈsens/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>激怒</td><td>incense the public<br>激怒公众</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The proposal is certain to incense feminists across the country.  这项提案肯定会激怒全国的女权主义者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 inflame</td><td>/ɪnˈfleɪm/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使加剧</td><td>inflame public anger<br>使公众愤怒加剧</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The mayor's careless comments only inflamed public anger over the housing shortage.  市长轻率的言论只是加剧了公众对住房短缺的愤怒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 exasperate</td><td>/ɪɡˈzæspəreɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使恼怒，激怒</td><td>exasperate his parents<br>使他的父母恼火</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His constant lateness exasperated even the most patient teachers.  他总是迟到，连最有耐心的老师都被激怒了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 infuriate</td><td>/ɪnˈfjʊrieɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>激怒</td><td>infuriate the critics<br>激怒批评者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The decision to cut pensions infuriated thousands of workers across the country.  削减养老金的决定激怒了全国成千上万的工人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 discompose</del></td><td>/ˌdɪskəmˈpoʊz/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使不安；使混乱</td><td>discompose the audience<br>让观众不安</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Nothing seemed to discompose the veteran pilot, not even engine failure.  似乎没什么能让这位老飞行员心慌，连引擎故障也不行。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 disturb</td><td>/dɪˈstɜːrb/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>打扰，扰乱；使不安</td><td>disturb the peace<br>扰乱安宁</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Please do not disturb the birds while they are nesting.  鸟类筑巢时请不要打扰它们。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 perturb</td><td>/pərˈtɜːrb/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使不安，使烦扰</td><td>perturb the balance<br>扰乱平衡</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The sudden change of plan perturbed everyone on the team.  计划的突然改变让全队的人都心神不宁。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 upset</td><td>/əpˈset/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、形容词</td><td>使心烦，使不安；打乱</td><td>upset the schedule<br>打乱日程</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The news of the closure upset the staff more than management expected.  停业的消息比管理层预想的更让员工不安。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>commonplace</b></td><td>/ˈkɑːmənpleɪs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>平凡的；老生常谈</td><td>a commonplace observation<br>老生常谈的看法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Video calls have become commonplace in ordinary office life.  视频通话在日常办公中已很平常。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 banal</td><td>/bəˈnɑːl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>非原创的，陈腐的</td><td>banal remarks<br>陈腐的言论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The film's ending was so banal that the audience laughed.  这部电影的结尾陈腐得让观众笑了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 cliché</del></td><td>/kliːˈʃeɪ/</td><td>☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>陈词滥调，老套的说法</td><td>an overused cliché<br>用滥的陈词</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Saying that time heals everything has become a tired cliché.  说时间治愈一切已成为老套的陈词滥调。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stale</td><td>/steɪl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>陈旧乏味的</td><td>stale jokes<br>老掉牙的笑话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His stale jokes made the audience shift restlessly in their seats.  他老掉牙的笑话让观众在座位上不安地挪动。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hackneyed</td><td>/ˈhæknid/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>陈腐的，缺乏创新的</td><td>a hackneyed phrase<br>陈腐的说法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The speech relied on hackneyed images of struggle and triumph.  这篇演讲依赖于陈腐的奋斗与胜利意象。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 moth-eaten</td><td>/ˈmɔːθ ˌiːtən/</td><td>★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>虫蛀的；陈旧过时的</td><td>a moth-eaten coat<br>被虫蛀的外套</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He arrived in a moth-eaten sweater from his college days.  他穿着大学时代那件被虫蛀的毛衣来了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 musty</td><td>/ˈmʌsti/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>发霉的，霉味的；陈腐的</td><td>a musty smell<br>一股霉味</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The cellar was dark, damp, and full of musty old books.  地窖阴暗潮湿，堆满带霉味的旧书。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 shopworn</td><td>/ˈʃɑːpwɔːrn/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>陈腐的，老一套的</td><td>shopworn arguments<br>陈腐的论点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The candidate repeated shopworn arguments that convinced almost nobody in the hall.  那位候选人重复着陈腐的论点，几乎没能说服场内任何人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stereotyped</td><td>/ˈsteriətaɪpt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>老套的，刻板的</td><td>stereotyped characters<br>刻板的人物</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The novel is full of stereotyped characters and predictable plots.  这部小说充满刻板人物和可预测的情节。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 threadbare</td><td>/ˈθredber/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>陈腐的；破旧的</td><td>a threadbare excuse<br>老掉牙的借口</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He offered the same threadbare excuse for arriving late again.  他又用同一个老掉牙的借口解释迟到。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 timeworn</td><td>/ˈtaɪmˌwɔːrn/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>陈旧的，老套的</td><td>a timeworn saying<br>老掉牙的谚语</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He comforted us with timeworn advice about patience and hard work.  他用关于耐心与勤奋的老套建议安慰我们。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 trite</td><td>/traɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>陈腐的，陈词滥调的</td><td>a trite observation<br>陈腐的见解</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The speech relied on trite phrases that moved nobody in the hall.  那篇演讲满是陈腐的套话，没能感动会场里的任何人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 fresh</td><td>/ˈfreʃ/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>新颖的；新鲜的</td><td>a fresh approach<br>新颖的方法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The team needs a fresh approach to this old problem.  团队需要用新颖的方法处理这个老问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 new</td><td>/ˈnuː/</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>新的，最近出现的</td><td>a brand new car<br>一辆全新的汽车</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They moved into a new apartment near the river.  他们搬进了河边一套新公寓。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 novel</td><td>/ˈnɑːvl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>新奇的</td><td>a novel approach<br>新奇的方法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her novel approach to the old problem surprised even the senior researchers.  她对这个老问题的新奇方法连资深研究员都感到意外。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 original</td><td>/əˈrɪdʒənl/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>创新的；最初的，最早的</td><td>an original idea<br>有创意的想法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her proposal was genuinely original, unlike the tired ideas we usually hear.  她的提案确实新颖，不像我们常听到的那些陈旧想法。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 unhackneyed</del></td><td>/ʌnˈhæknid/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不陈腐的，新颖的</td><td>an unhackneyed phrase<br>一个新颖的说法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her essay pleased the judges with its fresh, unhackneyed images.  她的文章以新鲜不俗的意象取悦了评委。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>concord</b></td><td>/ˈkɑːŋkɔːrd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>一致，和睦</td><td>live in concord<br>和睦相处</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The treaty brought decades of concord between the two states.  该条约给两国带来数十年的和睦。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 comity</td><td>/ˈkɑːmɪti/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>友好，礼让；和睦</td><td>the comity of nations<br>国家间的礼让</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The treaty was signed in a spirit of genuine comity.  条约是在真诚友好的气氛中签署的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 compatibility</td><td>/kəmˌpætəˈbɪləti/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>和睦相处；兼容性</td><td>check for compatibility<br>检查兼容性</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their compatibility as roommates surprised everyone who knew them.  他们作为室友相处融洽，令认识他们的人都惊讶。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 peace</td><td>/ˈpiːs/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>和平，和睦；平静</td><td>live in peace<br>和睦相处</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two villages have lived in peace for over a century.  这两个村庄和睦相处已逾一个世纪。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 conflict</td><td>/ˈkɑːnflɪkt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>冲突，矛盾；抵触</td><td>a conflict of interest<br>利益冲突</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The judge withdrew because of a possible conflict of interest.  法官因可能存在利益冲突而回避。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 discord</td><td>/ˈdɪskɔːrd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>意见不一致，不和谐</td><td>sow discord<br>挑起不和</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A single careless remark sowed discord among the previously friendly neighbors.  一句不慎的话在原本和睦的邻居间挑起了不和。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 dissension</td><td>/dɪˈsenʃn/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>意见分歧，不和</td><td>internal dissension<br>内部分歧</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Internal dissension over funding split the small research group into two camps.  关于经费的内部分歧把这个小研究组分成了两派。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 variance</td><td>/ˈveriəns/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>不一致，不和谐</td><td>at variance with<br>与…不一致</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His account was clearly at variance with the official police report.  他的说法明显与警方的官方报告不一致。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>concur</b></td><td>/kənˈkɜːr/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>同意，一致；同时发生</td><td>concur with the judge<br>同意法官的意见</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Two other judges concurred with the opinion she had written.  另外两位法官同意她撰写的意见。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 agree</td><td>/əˈɡriː/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>同意，赞同；一致</td><td>agree to the terms<br>同意这些条款</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Both sides finally agreed to the new safety rules.  双方最终同意了新的安全规定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 coincide</td><td>/ˌkoʊɪnˈsaɪd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>同时发生；相符，一致</td><td>coincide with the holiday<br>与假日同时发生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The festival coincides with the first full moon of autumn.  这个节日与秋天第一次满月同时到来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 coexist</td><td>/ˌkoʊəɡˈzɪst/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>共存，同时存在</td><td>coexist peacefully<br>和平共存</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Two rival species coexist along the narrow river bank.  两个竞争物种在狭窄的河岸上共存。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 synchronize</td><td>/ˈsɪŋkrəˌnaɪz/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>同步，使同时发生</td><td>synchronize the clocks<br>使时钟同步</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The team synchronized their watches before entering the tunnel.  小队进隧道前把手表调成同步。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 co-occur</td><td>/ˌkoʊəˈkɜr/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>同时发生，共同出现</td><td>frequently co-occur<br>经常同时出现</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 These two symptoms co-occur in most untreated patients.  这两种症状在多数未治疗的病人身上同时出现。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 band together</td><td>/bænd təˈɡɛðər/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>联合起来，团结一致</td><td>band together against<br>联合起来对抗</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Small shops banded together to fight the new tax.  小店铺联合起来抵制新税。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 collaborate</td><td>/kəˈlæbəˌreɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>合作，协作</td><td>collaborate on a book<br>合作写一本书</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Two labs collaborated closely to finish the vaccine trial.  两个实验室紧密合作完成了疫苗试验。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 concert</td><td>/ˈkɑːnsərt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>协力，一致；音乐会</td><td>act in concert<br>协同行动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The agencies acted in concert to trace the missing funds.  各机构协同行动追查失踪的资金。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 conjoin</td><td>/kənˈdʒɔɪn/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>结合，连接</td><td>conjoin the two parts<br>把两部分接合</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The bridge conjoins the island and the mainland neatly.  这座桥把小岛与大陆巧妙相连。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 league</td><td>/ˈliːɡ/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>联盟，同盟；结盟</td><td>in league with<br>与…结盟</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The merchants formed a league to set fair shipping rates.  商人们结成联盟以定出公道的运费。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 team up</td><td>/tim ˈʌp/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>组队合作，联手</td><td>team up with rivals<br>与对手联手</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two authors teamed up to write a children's history.  两位作者联手写了一部儿童历史读物。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unite</td><td>/juˈnaɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>联合，团结</td><td>unite against a threat<br>联合抵御威胁</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The crisis united the villagers as nothing else had.  这场危机以前所未有的方式团结了村民。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 differ</td><td>/ˈdɪfər/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>不同；意见不一致</td><td>differ in opinion<br>意见不同</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Experts differ widely on how the disease should be treated.  专家们对该疾病的治疗方式看法大相径庭。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 disagree</td><td>/dɪsəˈɡriː/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>不同意，有分歧</td><td>disagree with the plan<br>不同意这个计划</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Two experts openly disagreed about the cause of the accident.  两位专家对事故原因公开表示分歧。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>condescending</b></td><td>/ˌkɑːndɪˈsendɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>摆出高人一等姿态的，居高临下的</td><td>a condescending tone<br>高人一等的语气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She hated his condescending tone whenever she asked a simple technical question.  每次她问简单的技术问题，都讨厌他那种居高临下的语气。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 patronizing</td><td>/ˈpeɪtrəˌnaɪzɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>摆出高人一等姿态的</td><td>a patronizing tone<br>居高临下的语气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She disliked his patronizing tone whenever she asked a question.  每次她提问，他那居高临下的语气都让她反感。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del><b>condign</b></del></td><td>/kənˈdaɪn/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>应得的，恰当的</td><td>condign punishment<br>应得的惩罚</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The court imposed condign punishment on the corrupt inspector.  法庭对腐败的检查员施以应得的惩罚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 due</td><td>/ˈduː/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>应得的，应有的；到期的</td><td>give due credit<br>给予应得的赞誉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The engineers never received the credit that was rightly due.  工程师们从未得到他们应得的赞誉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 deserved</td><td>/dɪˈzɜːrvd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>应得的，理所应当的</td><td>a well deserved rest<br>应得的休息</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After the long tour she took a well deserved break.  长途巡演后她得到了应有的休息。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 merited</td><td>/ˈmerɪtɪd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>应得的，理所当然的</td><td>merited praise<br>应得的赞扬</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The award was merited praise for thirty years of quiet, patient teaching.  这个奖项是对三十年默默耐心教学的应得赞誉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 Justified</td><td>/ˈdʒʌstəˌfaɪd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有正当理由的，合理的</td><td>fully justified anger<br>完全合理的愤怒</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her complaint was justified, and the store refunded the money.  她的投诉有理，商店退了钱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 warranted</td><td>/ˈwɔːrəntɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有正当理由的，应得的</td><td>fully warranted criticism<br>完全有理的批评</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Given the delays, their anger seemed entirely warranted.  考虑到这些延误，他们的愤怒似乎完全合理。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 undeserved</td><td>/ˌʌndɪˈzɜːrvd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不应得的，冤枉的</td><td>undeserved blame<br>不应受的责难</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The quiet clerk suffered years of undeserved blame.  那位沉默的职员多年来承受着不该受的责难。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 undue</td><td>/ʌnˈduː/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>过度的，过分的；不适当的</td><td>undue influence<br>不当的影响</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The judge ruled that the will had been signed under undue influence.  法官裁定这份遗嘱是在不当影响下签署的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unmerited</td><td>/ʌnˈmɛrɪtɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不应得的，无理的</td><td>unmerited reward<br>不应得的奖赏</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He accepted the unmerited honor with visible embarrassment.  他明显尴尬地接受了这份不该得的荣誉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>condole</b></td><td>/kənˈdoʊl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>表达同情</td><td>condole with the widow<br>向寡妇表示慰问</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Neighbors came all week to condole with the grieving widow.  邻居们整周都来慰问那位悲伤的寡妇。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 compassionate</td><td>/kəmˈpæʃənət/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有同情心的，慈悲的</td><td>compassionate care<br>充满关怀的照护</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A compassionate nurse sat with the frightened child all night.  一位有同情心的护士整夜陪着那个受惊的孩子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sympathize with</td><td>/ˈsɪmpəθaɪz wɪð/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>同情，对…表示慰问</td><td>sympathize with the victims<br>同情受害者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Everyone in the office sympathized with her sudden loss.  办公室里每个人都同情她突遭的丧亲之痛。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 yearn over</td><td>/jɜrn ˈoʊvər/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>为…心生怜惜，深切怜爱</td><td>yearn over a lost child<br>为失去的孩子伤怀</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old woman yearned over the son she had not seen in years.  老妇人为多年未见的儿子牵挂心疼。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>condone</b></td><td>/kənˈdoʊn/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>宽恕；忽视</td><td>condone the violence<br>宽恕这种暴力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The school will never condone violence between its students.  学校绝不会宽恕学生之间的暴力行为。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 disregard</td><td>/ˌdɪsrɪˈɡɑːrd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>漠视，不关注；缺乏兴趣，缺乏关心</td><td>total disregard for safety<br>完全不顾安全</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their total disregard for safety rules eventually caused a serious accident.  他们完全不顾安全规定，最终造成了一起严重事故。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ignore</td><td>/ˌɪɡˈnɔːr/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>忽视，不理</td><td>ignore the warning<br>忽视警告</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The captain ignored the warning and sailed into the storm.  船长忽视了警告，驶入了风暴之中。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 overlook</td><td>/ˈoʊvərˌlʊk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>忽视；宽恕；俯视</td><td>overlook a minor error<br>宽容小错</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The teacher chose to overlook the boy's late arrival.  老师决定不追究那男孩的迟到。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 remit</td><td>/rɪˈmɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>减轻，免除；汇款</td><td>remit a penalty<br>免除罚款</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The judge agreed to remit part of the heavy fine.  法官同意减免部分高额罚款。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 shrug off</td><td>/ʃrʌɡ ˈɔf/</td><td>★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>对…不予理会，一笑置之</td><td>shrug off the criticism<br>对批评一笑置之</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The coach shrugged off questions about his uncertain future.  教练对有关他前途未定的问题不予理会。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 gloss over</td><td>/ɡlɔs ˈoʊvər/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>掩饰，含糊带过</td><td>gloss over the problems<br>对问题轻描淡写</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The report glossed over the losses of the previous year.  报告对前一年的亏损轻描淡写。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 denounce</td><td>/dɪˈnaʊns/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>公开指责</td><td>denounce corruption<br>公开指责腐败</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Local leaders publicly denounced the corruption exposed by the newspaper.  当地领导人公开谴责报纸曝光的腐败行为。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>conducive</b></td><td>/kənˈduːsɪv/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有益的，有促进作用的</td><td>conducive to learning<br>有助于学习</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A quiet room with good light is conducive to learning.  安静明亮的房间有助于学习。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 facilitative</td><td>/fəˈsɪlɪteɪtɪv/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有促进作用的，起助益的</td><td>a facilitative role<br>促进性的作用</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Good lighting plays a facilitative role in careful reading.  良好的照明对仔细阅读起促进作用。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 useful</td><td>/ˈjuːsfəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有用的，有益的</td><td>a useful tool<br>一件有用的工具</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 This old dictionary is still useful for checking rare words.  这本旧词典查生僻词仍然很有用。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unhelpful</td><td>/ənˈhelpfəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无益的，帮不上忙的</td><td>an unhelpful reply<br>毫无帮助的答复</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The clerk gave a vague and thoroughly unhelpful answer.  职员给出一个含糊而毫无帮助的答复。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 useless</td><td>/ˈjuːsləs/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无用的，无效的</td><td>practically useless<br>几乎毫无用处</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Without a charger the expensive phone was completely useless.  没有充电器，这部贵手机完全没用。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>cantankerous</b></td><td>/kænˈtæŋkərəs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>脾气坏的，爱争吵的</td><td>a cantankerous neighbor<br>一个爱吵架的邻居</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their cantankerous landlord argued about every small repair.  他们那位爱争吵的房东为每项小修理都要吵。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 acid</td><td>/ˈæsəd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>尖刻的；酸的；酸</td><td>an acid remark<br>一句尖刻的话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her acid comment about his writing silenced the whole table.  她对他文章的尖刻评论让全桌人都沉默了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ill-tempered</td><td>/ˌɪl ˈtɛmpərd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>脾气坏的，易怒的</td><td>an ill-tempered remark<br>一句脾气暴躁的话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His ill-tempered remark silenced everyone at the dinner table.  他脾气暴躁的话让餐桌上所有人都沉默了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dyspeptic</td><td>/dɪsˈpeptɪk/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>脾气坏的</td><td>a dyspeptic old man<br>一个脾气坏的老头</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A dyspeptic old man shouted at the children for stepping on his lawn.  一个脾气坏的老头因孩子踩了他的草坪而大声呵斥。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ill-natured</td><td>/ˌɪl ˈneɪtʃərd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>脾气坏的，凶恶的</td><td>an ill-natured remark<br>恶毒的言论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His ill-natured comments about her work upset everyone at the meeting.  他对她工作的恶意评论让会上每个人都很不快。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 surly</td><td>/ˈsɜːrli/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>脾气不好的</td><td>a surly waiter<br>一个态度恶劣的侍者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A surly waiter took our order without once looking up.  一个态度恶劣的侍者点单时始终没抬头。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 amiable</td><td>/ˈeɪmiəbl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>好脾气的，友好易相处的</td><td>an amiable manner<br>和气友善的举止</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Our amiable guide answered the same question a dozen times.  我们和气的导游把同一个问题回答了十几遍。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 affable</td><td>/ˈæfəbl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>和蔼的，温和的</td><td>an affable host<br>和蔼的主人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Our affable neighbor greets everyone on the street by name.  我们和蔼的邻居在街上会叫出每个人的名字打招呼。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 good-humored</td><td>/ˌɡʊd ˈhjumərd/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>脾气好的，和气的</td><td>a good-humored laugh<br>和气的笑声</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He stayed good-humored even after losing the final match.  即使输掉决赛，他仍然保持好脾气。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 good-natured</td><td>/ˌɡʊd ˈneɪtʃərd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>脾气好的，和善的</td><td>good-natured teasing<br>善意的调侃</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He accepted the joke with a good-natured shrug.  他和善地耸耸肩接受了这个玩笑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 good-tempered</td><td>/ˌɡʊd ˈtɛmpərd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>性情温和的，不易发怒的</td><td>a good-tempered child<br>脾气温和的孩子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old dog was good-tempered even when children pulled its ears.  即使孩子们拉它的耳朵，这只老狗也很温顺。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 3

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>confine</b></td><td>/kənˈfaɪn/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>限制</td><td>confine the discussion<br>限定讨论范围</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Please confine the discussion to the budget for this year.  请把讨论限定在今年的预算上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 limit</td><td>/ˈlɪmət/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>界限，限度；限制</td><td>exceed the speed limit<br>超过限速</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was fined for exceeding the speed limit downtown.  他因在市区超速而被罚款。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 circumscribe</td><td>/ˈsɜːrkəmskraɪb/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>限制，约束；画界线</td><td>circumscribe his power<br>限制他的权力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new law sharply circumscribed the mayor's emergency powers.  新法律大幅限制了市长的紧急权力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 restrict</td><td>/rɪˈstrɪkt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>限制，约束</td><td>restrict access to<br>限制进入</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 New rules restrict the number of visitors allowed each day.  新规定限制了每天允许进入的参观者人数。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 exceed</td><td>/ɪkˈsiːd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>超过，超出</td><td>exceed the speed limit<br>超速</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Costs must not exceed the budget agreed by the board.  成本不得超过董事会批准的预算。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>confluence</b></td><td>/ˈkɑːnfluəns/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>汇合处；汇集，交汇</td><td>the confluence of rivers<br>河流的汇合处</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The city grew up at the confluence of two great rivers.  这座城市在两条大河的汇合处发展起来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 conjunction</td><td>/kənˈdʒʌŋkʃən/</td><td>★★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>结合，同时发生；连词</td><td>in conjunction with<br>与…一起</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The drug works best in conjunction with a strict diet.  这种药与严格饮食配合使用效果最好。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 divergence</td><td>/daɪˈvɜːrdʒəns/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>分歧，分离，背离</td><td>a sharp divergence of views<br>观点的明显分歧</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A wide divergence of opinion delayed the final vote.  意见的巨大分歧推迟了最终投票。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>confront</b></td><td>/kənˈfrʌnt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>直接对抗，直面</td><td>confront the problem<br>直面问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Sooner or later you must confront the problem directly.  你早晚必须直面这个问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 affront</td><td>/əˈfrʌnt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>侮辱，冒犯</td><td>an affront to dignity<br>对尊严的侮辱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His careless remark was an affront to everyone in the room.  他随口的一句话侮辱了房间里的每个人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 brazen</td><td>/ˈbreɪzn/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词、动词</td><td>蛮横大胆的，厚颜无耻的；大胆自信地去面对或从事</td><td>a brazen lie<br>无耻的谎言</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She told a brazen lie without even lowering her eyes.  她说了个无耻的谎言，连眼睛都没垂下。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 encounter</td><td>/ɪnˈkaʊntər/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>遭遇，遇到；相遇</td><td>encounter difficulties<br>遇到困难</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Travelers may encounter sudden storms high on the pass.  旅行者在高处的山口可能遇上突来的风暴。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 face</td><td>/ˈfeɪs/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>面对，正视；脸，面</td><td>face the truth<br>正视事实</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Sooner or later she must face the truth about the debt.  她迟早得正视这笔债务的真相。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 meet</td><td>/miːt/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>合适的</td><td>a meet response<br>合适的回应</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A public apology seemed the only meet response to such a serious blunder.  公开道歉似乎是对如此严重失误唯一合适的回应。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 dodge</td><td>/dɑːdʒ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>躲避</td><td>dodge the question<br>回避问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The minister managed to dodge the question three times in one interview.  部长在一次采访中三次成功回避了这个问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 duck</td><td>/dʌk/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>躲避，回避；迅速低头</td><td>duck the question<br>回避这个问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The minister ducked every question about the budget.  部长回避了所有关于预算的问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 parry</td><td>/ˈpæri/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>躲避，回避（问题）</td><td>parry the question<br>回避这个问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The senator tried to parry every question about his campaign funds.  那位参议员试图回避每一个关于竞选资金的问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 shirk</td><td>/ʃɜːrk/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>逃避，规避</td><td>shirk responsibility<br>逃避责任</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 No one on this team is allowed to shirk hard work.  这个团队里没人可以逃避苦活。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 sidestep</td><td>/ˈsaɪdstep/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>回避，避开（问题）</td><td>sidestep the issue<br>回避这个问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The candidate neatly sidestepped the issue of rising unemployment.  这位候选人巧妙地回避了失业率上升的问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>confound</b></td><td>/kənˈfaʊnd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使困惑；证明为假，证伪</td><td>confound the critics<br>让批评者困惑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The late victory confounded the critics who had predicted failure.  最后的胜利让预言失败的批评者困惑不已。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 baffle</td><td>/ˈbæfl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使疑惑</td><td>baffle the experts<br>使专家困惑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The strange symptoms continued to baffle the visiting experts.  这些奇怪的症状一直使来访的专家疑惑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bewilder</td><td>/bɪˈwɪldər/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使迷惑，使不知所措</td><td>bewilder the newcomers<br>使新来者困惑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The maze of corridors bewildered every visitor on the first day.  迷宫般的走廊让每位来访者第一天都不知所措。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 confuse</td><td>/kənˈfjuːz/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使困惑；混淆</td><td>confuse the issue<br>混淆问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The vague instructions confused nearly everyone in the training class.  含糊的说明让培训班几乎所有人都困惑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 muddle</td><td>/ˈmʌdəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>使混乱，弄糟；混乱</td><td>muddle the facts<br>把事实弄乱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Too many footnotes muddled the argument of his short essay.  过多脚注把他短文的论证弄乱了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 perplex</td><td>/pərˈpleks/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使困惑，使费解</td><td>perplex the researchers<br>使研究者困惑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The strange results perplexed the researchers for several long months.  奇怪的结果让研究者困惑了好几个月。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 puzzle</td><td>/ˈpʌzəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>使困惑；谜题</td><td>puzzle the doctors<br>使医生们困惑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her sudden recovery puzzled the doctors who had treated her.  她的突然康复让治疗过她的医生们感到困惑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 belie</td><td>/bɪˈlaɪ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>与…相对立；与…相矛盾</td><td>belie his claims<br>与他的说法矛盾</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company's own records belie his claims about the accident.  公司自己的记录与他关于事故的说法相矛盾。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 debunk</td><td>/ˌdiːˈbʌŋk/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>揭穿…的真面目</td><td>debunk the myth<br>揭穿这个神话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A single careful experiment debunked the theory that had misled everyone.  一次严谨的实验揭穿了那个误导所有人的理论。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 falsify</td><td>/ˈfɔːlsəˌfaɪ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>篡改，伪造；证明为假</td><td>falsify the records<br>篡改记录</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 An accountant was accused of falsifying the company's records.  一名会计被指控篡改公司账目。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 disprove</td><td>/ˌdɪsˈpruːv/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>反驳，证明为假</td><td>disprove the theory<br>驳倒这一理论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Magellan's voyage finally disproved the belief in a flat earth.  麦哲伦的航行最终驳倒了地球是平的这一信念。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rebut</td><td>/rɪˈbʌt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>反驳，驳斥</td><td>rebut the accusation<br>反驳这项指控</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She rebutted every charge with documents and dated photographs.  她用文件和标注日期的照片反驳了每项指控。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 refute</td><td>/rɪˈfjuːt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>驳倒，反驳</td><td>refute the claim<br>驳斥这一说法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The scientist used new data to refute the claim about rising temperatures.  这位科学家用新数据反驳了关于气温上升的说法。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 clarify</td><td>/ˈklerɪfaɪ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>阐明，澄清</td><td>clarify the rules<br>澄清规则</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The dean clarified the rules after weeks of confused complaints.  在数周混乱的投诉后，院长澄清了规则。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 confirm</td><td>/kənˈfɜːrm/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>证实，确认，进一步确定</td><td>confirm the findings<br>证实这些发现</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Later tests confirmed what the first team had suspected.  后来的检测证实了第一个团队的猜测。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 validate</td><td>/ˈvælədeɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>证实，确认；使有效</td><td>validate the results<br>验证这些结果</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Independent labs validated the results within three months.  独立实验室在三个月内验证了这些结果。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 verify</td><td>/ˈverɪfaɪ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>证实，核实</td><td>verify the data<br>核实这些数据</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Two independent labs will verify the results before publication.  两家独立实验室将在发表前核实这些结果。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>congeal</b></td><td>/kənˈdʒiːl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>凝固，固化</td><td>congeal into a mass<br>凝结成块</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The gravy congealed as soon as the plate cooled.  盘子一凉，肉汁就凝住了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 coagulate</td><td>/koʊˈæɡjuleɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>凝结，凝固</td><td>coagulate into clots<br>凝结成块</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Blood from the small cut coagulated within a few minutes.  小伤口流出的血几分钟内就凝结了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 solidify</td><td>/səˈlɪdəˌfaɪ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>使凝固；使稳固</td><td>solidify into a mass<br>凝固成一块</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The melted wax solidified quickly in the cold room.  融化的蜡在冷房间里很快凝固了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 indurate</del></td><td>/ˈɪndjʊreɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使变硬；使冷酷无情</td><td>indurate the heart<br>使心变得冷硬</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Years of betrayal had indurated his heart against every plea for sympathy.  多年的背叛使他的心变得冷硬，对任何求情都无动于衷。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 clot</td><td>/klɑːt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>密集的一群；凝结</td><td>a blood clot<br>血块</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A blood clot in his leg required immediate medical attention.  他腿部的血块需要立即接受治疗。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 melt</td><td>/ˈmelt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>融化，熔化</td><td>melt in the sun<br>在阳光下融化</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The ice cream melted before we reached the beach.  我们还没到海滩冰淇淋就化了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 liquefy</td><td>/ˈlɪkwəˌfaɪ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>使液化，变成液体</td><td>liquefy the mixture<br>使混合物液化</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Heat will liquefy the butter in about two minutes.  加热约两分钟就能把黄油化成液体。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>congenial</b></td><td>/kənˈdʒiːniəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>和善的，友好的</td><td>a congenial host<br>一位和善的主人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Our congenial host made every guest feel completely at home.  我们和善的主人让每位客人都感到自在。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 agreeable</td><td>/əˈɡriːəbl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>令人愉悦的；相一致的</td><td>an agreeable companion<br>一个讨人喜欢的同伴</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She found him an agreeable companion on the long train journey.  她觉得他是长途火车旅程中一位讨人喜欢的同伴。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 amicable</td><td>/ˈæmɪkəbl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>友善的，和睦的</td><td>an amicable settlement<br>友好的和解</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The dispute ended in an amicable agreement between neighbors.  这场纠纷以邻里间的友好协议告终。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 compatible</td><td>/kəmˈpætəbl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>一致的，能共存的</td><td>compatible with the system<br>与该系统兼容</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new driver is compatible with both older printers.  新驱动与两款旧打印机都兼容。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unanimous</td><td>/juˈnænɪməs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>一致同意的</td><td>a unanimous decision<br>一致通过的决定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The jury reached a unanimous verdict after only two hours of discussion.  陪审团仅讨论两小时便达成了一致的裁决。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 discordant</td><td>/dɪˈskɔːrdənt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不一致的，不和谐的</td><td>discordant opinions<br>不一致的意见</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Discordant voices from the back ruined the quiet ceremony.  后面不和谐的声音破坏了安静的仪式。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 incompatible</td><td>/ˌɪnkəmˈpætəbəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不相容的，矛盾的</td><td>incompatible goals<br>互不相容的目标</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Rapid growth and strict conservation seemed incompatible goals here.  在这里快速发展与严格保护似乎是矛盾的目标。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>congruent</b></td><td>/ˈkɑːŋɡruənt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>和谐一致的；全等的</td><td>congruent with the facts<br>与事实相符</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His account is congruent with the facts we already know.  他的说法与我们已知的事实相符。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 consonant</td><td>/ˈkɑːnsənənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>和谐一致的</td><td>consonant with tradition<br>与传统相符</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His modest lifestyle is consonant with the values he preaches.  他朴素的生活方式与他宣扬的价值观相符。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 compatible</td><td>/kəmˈpætəbl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>一致的，能共存的</td><td>compatible with the system<br>与该系统兼容</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new driver is compatible with both older printers.  新驱动与两款旧打印机都兼容。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 consistent</td><td>/kənˈsɪstənt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>一致的；始终如一的</td><td>consistent with the evidence<br>与证据相符</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His account is consistent with what the neighbors reported.  他的说法与邻居们所述一致。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 harmonious</td><td>/hɑːrˈmoʊniəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>和谐的，融洽的；悦耳的</td><td>a harmonious relationship<br>和谐的关系</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two neighbors maintained a harmonious relationship for over thirty years.  这两位邻居三十多年来保持着融洽的关系。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 accordant</del></td><td>/əˈkɔrdənt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>一致的，符合的</td><td>accordant with the law<br>与法律相符</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The ruling was accordant with decisions made in earlier cases.  该裁决与早先案件的判决一致。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 identical</td><td>/aɪˈdentɪkəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>完全相同的，同一的</td><td>virtually identical results<br>几乎完全相同的结果</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two samples produced identical readings on every machine.  两个样本在每台机器上都得出相同读数。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 exact</td><td>/ɪɡˈzækt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、形容词</td><td>强索，征收；精确的</td><td>exact a heavy tax<br>征收重税</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The conquerors exacted tribute from every village in the valley.  征服者向谷中每个村庄强征贡赋。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 disagreeable</td><td>/ˌdɪsəˈɡriːəbəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>令人不快的；脾气坏的</td><td>a disagreeable task<br>令人不快的任务</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Cleaning the drains was a thoroughly disagreeable job.  清理下水道是件极其讨人厌的活儿。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 disparate</td><td>/ˈdɪspərət/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>迥然不同的，全然不同的</td><td>disparate viewpoints<br>迥然不同的观点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The committee somehow reconciled disparate viewpoints into a single workable proposal.  委员会竟把迥然不同的观点整合成一份可行的提案。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
