<tr><td><b>conjecture</b></td><td>/kənˈdʒektʃər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>推测，猜测；（没有依据地）认为</td><td>pure conjecture<br>纯属推测</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Without records, any date for the painting is pure conjecture.  没有记录，这幅画的年代纯属推测。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 speculation</td><td>/ˌspekjəˈleɪʃən/</td><td>★★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>推测，猜测；投机</td><td>idle speculation<br>无根据的猜测</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Without data the report was little more than speculation.  没有数据，这份报告不过是猜测。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 supposition</td><td>/ˌsʌpəˈzɪʃn/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>猜想，推测</td><td>based on supposition<br>基于猜测</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their conclusion rests on supposition rather than on hard evidence.  他们的结论基于猜测，而非确凿证据。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 surmise</td><td>/sərˈmaɪz/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>（根据不足的）推测，揣测</td><td>a mere surmise<br>只是一种揣测</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Without documents, his account is a mere surmise about what happened.  没有文件，他的说法只是对当时情形的揣测。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 theory</td><td>/ˈθɪri/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>理论；推测，看法</td><td>in theory<br>理论上</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 One popular theory holds that the fire began outdoors.  一种流行的说法认为火是从室外烧起的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 assume</td><td>/əˈsuːm/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>就任，承担（职权）；假定</td><td>assume office<br>就职上任</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She will assume office as director next Monday.  她将于下周一就任主任。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 imagine</td><td>/ˌɪˈmædʒən/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>想象；猜想，认为</td><td>hard to imagine<br>难以想象</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 I imagine the meeting will run past six o'clock.  我猜会议会开到六点以后。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 presume</td><td>/prɪˈzuːm/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>推测，假定；擅自做</td><td>presume innocence<br>假定无罪</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We must presume the letter was lost in the post.  我们只能假定那封信在邮寄中丢失了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 speculate</td><td>/ˈspekjuleɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>推测，揣测</td><td>speculate about the cause<br>推测原因</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Scientists can only speculate about the cause of the sudden extinction.  科学家只能推测这次突然灭绝的原因。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 suppose</td><td>/səˈpoʊz/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>猜想，假定，认为</td><td>suppose for a moment<br>姑且假定一下</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Suppose the train is late; how will you reach the airport?  假设火车晚点，你怎么去机场？</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 fact</td><td>/ˈfækt/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>事实，实情</td><td>a matter of fact<br>事实问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Whether the letter existed is a question of fact, not opinion.  那封信是否存在是事实问题，不是意见问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>connive</b></td><td>/kəˈnaɪv/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>共谋；纵容，默许坏事</td><td>connive at fraud<br>对欺诈行为暗中纵容</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Two officers connived at the fraud for several years.  两名官员多年来对这起欺诈暗中纵容。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 conspire</td><td>/kənˈspaɪər/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>合谋，密谋</td><td>conspire to overthrow<br>密谋推翻</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Several generals conspired to seize power while the president was abroad.  几名将军趁总统出国时密谋夺权。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 intrigue</td><td>/ˈɪntriːɡ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>阴谋；激起…的兴趣</td><td>political intrigue<br>政治阴谋</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Court records reveal decades of political intrigue behind the sudden fall of the minister.  法庭记录揭示了这位大臣突然倒台背后数十年的政治阴谋。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 plot</td><td>/ˈplɑːt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>密谋，策划；情节</td><td>plot against the king<br>密谋反对国王</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Several officers plotted secretly to seize the northern ports.  几名军官暗中策划夺取北方港口。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 collude</td><td>/kəˈluːd/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>串通，共谋（做坏事）</td><td>collude with rivals<br>与对手串通</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Two suppliers colluded with rivals to keep prices artificially high.  两家供应商与对手串通，人为维持高价。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 machinate</del></td><td>/ˈmækəˌneɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>阴谋策划，图谋</td><td>machinate against a rival<br>图谋对付对手</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The ministers machinated for months to remove the young king.  大臣们策划了数月要废掉年轻的国王。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 put up</td><td>/pʊt ˈʌp/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>共同策划；提供；张贴</td><td>put up a scheme<br>合谋策划一个计划</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two brothers put up the whole story to fool their teacher.  两兄弟合伙编造了整个故事来骗老师。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>connoisseur</b></td><td>/ˌkɑːnəˈsɜːr/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>鉴赏家（尤指艺术领域）；专家</td><td>a connoisseur of fine wine<br>美酒鉴赏家</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He is a connoisseur of fine wine and rare books.  他是美酒和珍本书的鉴赏家。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 aesthete</td><td>/ˈesθit/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>唯美主义者，审美家</td><td>a self-styled aesthete<br>自称的审美家</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The aesthete spent hours arranging flowers in a plain vase.  那位审美家花几个钟头在素瓶里插花。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 cognoscente</del></td><td>/ˌkɑnjəˈʃɛnti/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>鉴赏家，行家</td><td>a wine cognoscente<br>一位品酒行家</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Only a cognoscente would notice the flaw in that varnish.  只有行家才会注意到那层漆的瑕疵。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 adept</td><td>/əˈdept/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>内行的，熟练的；行家</td><td>adept at negotiation<br>擅长谈判</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She is remarkably adept at calming angry customers.  她极擅长安抚愤怒的顾客。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 artist</td><td>/ˈɑːrtəst/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>艺术家；能手，行家</td><td>a true artist<br>真正的行家</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old baker was an artist with sugar and butter.  那位老面包师是驾驭糖与黄油的能手。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 authority</td><td>/əˈθɔːrəti/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>（某领域的）权威人士；管辖权，控制</td><td>an authority on ancient coins<br>古钱币方面的权威</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She is a recognized authority on ancient Greek coins.  她是古希腊钱币方面公认的权威人士。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 maestro</td><td>/ˈmaɪstroʊ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>大师（尤指音乐指挥家）</td><td>the aging maestro<br>年迈的大师</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The maestro raised his baton and the hall fell silent.  大师举起指挥棒，全场随之安静。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 master</td><td>/ˈmæstər/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>大师，名家；主人</td><td>a master of disguise<br>伪装大师</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Only a master of the craft could carve such fine details.  只有行家高手才能雕出这般精细的细节。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 maven</td><td>/ˈmeɪvn/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>专家</td><td>a marketing maven<br>营销专家</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The magazine hired a marketing maven to explain why the campaign had failed.  杂志请来一位营销专家解释这次宣传为何失败。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 proficient</td><td>/prəˈfɪʃnt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>熟练的，精通的</td><td>proficient in Spanish<br>精通西班牙语</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After two years abroad she became proficient in Spanish and Portuguese.  在国外待了两年后，她精通了西班牙语和葡萄牙语。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 virtuoso</td><td>/ˌvɜːrtʃuˈoʊsoʊ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>技艺大师，演奏名家；技艺精湛的</td><td>a violin virtuoso<br>小提琴演奏大师</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The violin virtuoso played the difficult passage without a single mistake.  那位小提琴大师毫无差错地演奏了这段难曲。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 amateur</td><td>/ˈæməˌtɜːr/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>业余爱好者；外行的</td><td>a talented amateur<br>有天赋的业余者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The comet was first spotted by an amateur astronomer.  这颗彗星最先由一位业余天文学家发现。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 inexpert</td><td>/ˌɪnˈekspɜːrt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不熟练的，外行的</td><td>inexpert hands<br>不熟练的手法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 To inexpert eyes the two paintings looked exactly the same.  在外行眼里，这两幅画看起来完全一样。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 nonexpert</del></td><td>/ˌnɑnˈɛkspɜrt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>非专家，外行（的）</td><td>a nonexpert reader<br>非专业的读者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The guide explains the disease clearly for nonexpert readers.  这本指南为非专业读者清楚地解释了该疾病。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 4

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>conscientious</b></td><td>/ˌkɑːnʃiˈenʃəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>仔细的，一丝不苟的；有良心的，正直的</td><td>a conscientious worker<br>一位认真的员工</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A conscientious editor checks every fact before the article goes to print.  一丝不苟的编辑会在文章付印前核实每一个事实。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 careful</td><td>/ˈkerfəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>谨慎的，仔细的</td><td>careful consideration<br>慎重考虑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Be careful when you cross this busy street at night.  夜里穿过这条繁忙的街道时要小心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 exact</td><td>/ɪɡˈzækt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、形容词</td><td>强索，征收；精确的</td><td>exact a heavy tax<br>征收重税</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The conquerors exacted tribute from every village in the valley.  征服者向谷中每个村庄强征贡赋。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 heedful</del></td><td>/ˈhiːdfəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>留心的，注意的</td><td>heedful of the warning<br>留意警告</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Heedful of the forecast, the climbers turned back early.  留意到天气预报，登山者提早返回了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 meticulous</td><td>/məˈtɪkjələs/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>一丝不苟的，极为细致的</td><td>meticulous attention to detail<br>对细节极为在意</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The restorer's meticulous attention to detail saved the fragile manuscript from further damage.  修复师一丝不苟地关注细节，使这份脆弱的手稿免于进一步损坏。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 painstaking</td><td>/ˈpeɪnzteɪkɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>煞费苦心的，一丝不苟的</td><td>painstaking research<br>煞费苦心的研究</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Years of painstaking research went into this single short article.  这篇短文背后是多年一丝不苟的研究。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 scrupulous</td><td>/ˈskruːpjələs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>正直的；一丝不苟的</td><td>scrupulous honesty<br>一丝不苟的诚实</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her scrupulous honesty made her the obvious choice to handle the accounts.  她一丝不苟的诚实使她成为管账的当然人选。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 conscionable</del></td><td>/ˈkɑnʃənəbəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>合乎良心的，正当的</td><td>a conscionable agreement<br>一份公道的协议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 No conscionable judge would enforce so one-sided a contract.  有良心的法官不会强制执行如此偏袒一方的合同。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ethical</td><td>/ˈeθɪkəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>道德的，合乎道义的</td><td>ethical standards<br>道德标准</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Reporters must weigh ethical limits before publishing private letters.  记者发表私人信件前必须权衡道德界限。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 honest</td><td>/ˈɑːnəst/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>诚实的，正直的</td><td>an honest answer<br>一个诚实的回答</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Give me an honest answer, even if it disappoints me.  给我一个诚实的答复，即使会让我失望。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 moral</td><td>/ˈmɔːrəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>道德的；道德准则</td><td>a moral obligation<br>道义上的义务</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They felt a moral obligation to shelter the refugees.  他们觉得有道义上的义务收留难民。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 upright</td><td>/ˈʌpraɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>正直的，诚实的；竖直的</td><td>an upright citizen<br>一位正直的公民</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Neighbors described him as an upright and reliable man.  邻居们说他是个正直可靠的人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 principled</td><td>/ˈprɪnsəpəld/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有原则的，有道德的</td><td>a principled stand<br>有原则的立场</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her principled refusal cost her a promising position.  她基于原则的拒绝让她失去了一个有前途的职位。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 careless</td><td>/ˈkerləs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>粗心的，疏忽的</td><td>a careless mistake<br>粗心的错误</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 One careless remark ruined months of patient negotiation.  一句不慎的话毁了数月的耐心谈判。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 remiss</td><td>/rɪˈmɪs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>疏忽的，不留心的</td><td>be remiss in one's duties<br>疏于职守</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 I would be remiss if I did not thank the volunteers.  如果我不感谢志愿者，那就是我的疏忽。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unscrupulous</td><td>/ənsˈkruːpjələs/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不道德的，无原则的</td><td>an unscrupulous dealer<br>一个不择手段的商人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Unscrupulous lenders charged the farmers impossible rates of interest.  不择手段的放贷者向农民收取高得离谱的利息。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>consensus</b></td><td>/kənˈsensəs/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>一致同意</td><td>reach a consensus<br>达成一致意见</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The board finally reached a consensus after four hours of debate.  董事会经过四小时辩论终于达成了一致。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 accord</td><td>/əˈkɔːrd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>一致，符合；授予</td><td>in accord with<br>与…一致</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His actions were in accord with the policy he announced.  他的行动与他宣布的政策一致。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 assent</td><td>/əˈsent/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>同意，赞同</td><td>assent to the proposal<br>赞同该提议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The committee assented to the proposal without further debate.  委员会未经进一步辩论就同意了该提案。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 agreement</td><td>/əˈɡriːmənt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>一致，同意；协议</td><td>come to an agreement<br>达成协议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two firms signed an agreement to share research costs.  两家公司签署协议分担研究费用。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 harmony</td><td>/ˈhɑːrməni/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>和谐，融洽</td><td>live in harmony with<br>与…和谐共处</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two communities have lived in harmony for generations.  这两个社区世代和睦相处。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unanimity</td><td>/ˌjuːnəˈnɪməti/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>全体一致，一致同意</td><td>rare unanimity<br>难得的一致</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 There was surprising unanimity among the usually divided members.  在平时意见分歧的成员中出现了惊人的一致。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unison</td><td>/ˈjuːnəsən/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>一致；齐声，同时</td><td>in unison<br>齐声地</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The children answered in unison when the teacher asked.  老师提问时孩子们齐声回答。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 disagreement</td><td>/dɪsəˈɡriːmənt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>分歧，不一致，争执</td><td>a sharp disagreement<br>激烈的分歧</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A long disagreement over funding stalled the whole project.  关于资金的长期分歧使整个项目停滞。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>consequence</b></td><td>/ˈkɑːnsəkwens/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>结果；重要性，价值</td><td>of little consequence<br>无关紧要</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The full consequences of the new policy will not be clear for years.  这项新政策的全部后果多年内都不会明朗。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 aftermath</td><td>/ˈæftərˌmæθ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>后果，余波，影响</td><td>in the aftermath of<br>在…的余波中</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Aid arrived slowly in the aftermath of the flood.  洪水过后援助迟迟才到。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 effect</td><td>/ɪˈfekt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>影响，效果；实现</td><td>have a lasting effect<br>产生持久影响</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new rule had little effect on daily traffic.  新规定对日常交通几乎没有影响。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 outcome</td><td>/ˈaʊtˌkʌm/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>结果，结局</td><td>the likely outcome<br>可能的结果</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Nobody could predict the outcome of such a close election.  没人能预测这样势均力敌的选举结果。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 result</td><td>/rɪˈzʌlt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>结果；导致，产生</td><td>as a result of<br>由于…的结果</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The delay resulted from a simple error in the schedule.  这次延误源于日程上的一个小错误。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 moment</td><td>/ˈmoʊmənt/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>重要性，重大；瞬间</td><td>of great moment<br>极为重要</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The treaty was an event of real moment for both nations.  这项条约对两国都是意义重大的事件。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 magnitude</td><td>/ˈmæɡnɪtuːd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>重要，重大</td><td>of the first magnitude<br>头等重要</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Few people grasped the magnitude of the coming economic crisis.  很少有人明白即将到来的经济危机的严重程度。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 weight</td><td>/ˈweɪt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>重要性，影响力；重量</td><td>carry great weight<br>极有影响力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her opinion carries considerable weight with the committee.  她的意见在委员会里很有分量。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 source</td><td>/ˈsɔːrs/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>来源，源头，出处</td><td>the source of the problem<br>问题的根源</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Engineers traced the leak to its source under the road.  工程师把漏水追查到路面下的源头。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 origin</td><td>/ˈɔːrədʒən/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>起源，由来，出身</td><td>the origin of the custom<br>这一习俗的起源</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Scholars still argue about the origin of the strange name.  学者们仍在争论这个奇怪名字的来源。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 cause</td><td>/ˈkɔːz/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>原因；导致，引起</td><td>the root cause<br>根本原因</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Heavy rain caused the small stream to flood the road.  大雨使这条小溪淹了公路。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 antecedent</td><td>/ˌæntəˈsiːdnt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>先前的；前事，前情</td><td>antecedent events<br>先前发生的事件</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Historians study the antecedent causes of the revolution.  历史学家研究这场革命的先前起因。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 triviality</td><td>/ˌtrɪviˈæləti/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>琐事；微不足道</td><td>argue over trivialities<br>为琐事争吵</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The long meeting sank into arguments about pure trivialities.  漫长的会议陷入了对纯粹琐事的争论。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>conservative</b></td><td>/kənˈsɜːrvətɪv/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>守旧的，不愿改变的；不招摇的，低调的</td><td>a conservative estimate<br>保守的估计</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her parents were too conservative to accept such an unconventional career choice.  她的父母太守旧，无法接受这样不合常规的职业选择。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hidebound</td><td>/ˈhaɪdbaʊnd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>死板的，极度保守的</td><td>hidebound traditions<br>僵化的传统</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The hidebound innkeeper flatly refused to install internet for guests.  这位死板的旅店老板断然拒绝为客人装网络。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 reactionary</td><td>/riˈækʃəneri/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>反对变革的，极保守的</td><td>reactionary policies<br>反对变革的政策</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics called the new rules reactionary and openly unfair.  批评者称新规定保守倒退且明显不公。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 die-hard</td><td>/ˈdaɪ hɑrd/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>顽固不化的；死硬派</td><td>a die-hard supporter<br>一个死忠支持者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Only a few die-hard fans stayed until the final whistle.  只有几个死忠球迷留到终场哨响。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 muted</td><td>/ˈmjuːtɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>低调的，柔和的；压低的</td><td>a muted response<br>反应平淡</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The announcement drew a surprisingly muted response from investors.  这项公告得到投资者出奇平淡的反应。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 repressed</td><td>/riˈprest/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>受压抑的，克制的</td><td>repressed anger<br>被压抑的怒气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Years of repressed anger finally showed in his shaking voice.  多年压抑的怒气终于在他颤抖的声音里显露。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 low-key</td><td>/ˌloʊ ˈki/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>低调的，不张扬的</td><td>a low-key ceremony<br>一场低调的仪式</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They chose a low-key wedding with only twelve guests.  他们选了只有十二位客人的低调婚礼。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 understated</td><td>/ˈʌndərˌsteɪtɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>含蓄的，不张扬的</td><td>understated elegance<br>含蓄的优雅</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her understated gray suit suited the solemn occasion perfectly.  她那身低调的灰西装非常适合这个庄重的场合。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unpretentious</td><td>/ˌʌnprɪˈtenʃəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不炫耀的，朴实低调的</td><td>an unpretentious restaurant<br>朴实的小餐馆</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They live in a small, unpretentious house near the river.  他们住在河边一间朴实的小房子里。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 radical</td><td>/ˈrædəkəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>激进的；根本的；激进者</td><td>radical reform<br>激进的改革</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The council rejected any radical change to the old charter.  议会否决了对旧章程的任何激进改动。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 aggressive</td><td>/əˈɡresɪv/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>好斗的；积极进取的，强有力的</td><td>an aggressive marketing campaign<br>强势的营销活动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The dog became aggressive whenever strangers approached the gate.  只要有陌生人靠近大门，这只狗就变得有攻击性。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 flamboyant</td><td>/flæmˈbɔɪənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>艳丽夺目的，炫耀的，充满装饰的</td><td>a flamboyant style<br>浮夸炫目的风格</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The singer wore a flamboyant coat covered in mirrors and feathers.  歌手穿着一件缀满镜片和羽毛的艳丽夺目的外套。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ostentatious</td><td>/ˌɑːstenˈteɪʃəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>炫耀卖弄的</td><td>ostentatious display of wealth<br>炫富的排场</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His ostentatious diamond watch drew stares from everyone in the modest room.  他炫耀的钻石表引来了朴素房间里所有人的注视。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 splashy</td><td>/ˈsplæʃi/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>引人注目的，招眼的</td><td>a splashy advertisement<br>一则招眼的广告</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The magazine ran a splashy cover story about the singer.  杂志刊出一篇关于那位歌手的招眼封面报道。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>conservatory</b></td><td>/kənˈsɜːrvətɔːri/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>温室；艺术学院</td><td>study at a conservatory<br>在艺术学院学习</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He studied piano at a famous conservatory before joining the city orchestra.  他在一所著名的艺术学院学钢琴，之后加入了市交响乐团。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 greenhouse</td><td>/ˈɡriːnˌhaʊs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>温室，暖房</td><td>grow in a greenhouse<br>在温室里种植</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 These orchids grow only in a heated greenhouse.  这些兰花只能在加温的温室里生长。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hothouse</td><td>/ˈhɑːtˌhaʊs/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>温室；温室般的环境</td><td>hothouse flowers<br>温室里的花</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The school became a hothouse for young mathematical talent.  这所学校成了培养年轻数学人才的温床。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del><b>consilience</b></del></td><td>/kənˈsɪliəns/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>融合</td><td>the consilience of evidence<br>多方证据的融合</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The theory gains strength from the consilience of findings across several unrelated fields.  这一理论因多个不相关领域研究结果的融合而更有力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>considerable</b></td><td>/kənˈsɪdərəbl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>（数量上）可观（而值得注意）的</td><td>a considerable sum<br>一笔可观的钱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The renovation required a considerable amount of money and nearly two years of work.  这次翻修耗费了可观的资金和近两年的工期。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 extensive</td><td>/ɪkˈstensɪv/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>广泛的，大量的，广阔的</td><td>extensive research<br>广泛的研究</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The library holds an extensive collection of local newspapers.  这家图书馆藏有大量的地方报纸。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 substantial</td><td>/səbˈstænʃl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>大量的，可观的；实质的</td><td>a substantial increase<br>大幅的增长</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company reported a substantial increase in profits this quarter.  公司报告本季度利润大幅增长。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 large-scale</td><td>/ˈlɑːrdʒˌskeɪl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>大规模的，大范围的</td><td>large-scale production<br>大规模生产</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Large-scale planting began after the spring rains stopped.  春雨停后开始了大规模种植。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 important</td><td>/ˌɪmˈpɔːrtənt/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>重要的，重大的</td><td>an important decision<br>一个重要的决定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Sleep is as important as diet for a young athlete.  对年轻运动员来说，睡眠和饮食一样重要。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 significant</td><td>/səɡˈnɪfɪkənt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>重要的；显著的</td><td>a significant difference<br>显著的差别</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Researchers found a significant drop in the pollution levels.  研究者发现污染水平显著下降。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 consequential</td><td>/ˌkɑːnsəˈkwenʃəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>重要的；随之而来的</td><td>a consequential ruling<br>一项重要的裁决</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 That quiet vote proved the most consequential act of the session.  那次不起眼的投票成了本届会议最重要的举动。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 momentous</td><td>/moʊˈmentəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>重大的，重要的</td><td>a momentous decision<br>一个重大的决定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The board reached a momentous decision that changed the company's direction entirely.  董事会做出了一个重大决定，彻底改变了公司的方向。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 weighty</td><td>/ˈweɪti/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>重要的，重大的；沉重的</td><td>weighty matters<br>重大的事务</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The elders debated weighty questions of land and water.  长者们讨论着土地与水源的重大问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 trivial</td><td>/ˈtrɪviəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无足轻重的，不重要的</td><td>a trivial detail<br>无关紧要的细节</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The editor refused to argue over such a trivial difference in wording.  编辑不愿为措辞上如此无足轻重的差别争论。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>console</b></td><td>/kənˈsoʊl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>安慰，慰藉</td><td>console a grieving friend<br>安慰悲伤的朋友</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Nothing anyone said could console her that evening.  那天晚上无论谁说什么都无法安慰她。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 comfort</td><td>/ˈkʌmfərt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>舒适；安慰</td><td>live in comfort<br>过舒适的生活</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old chair gave him a great deal of comfort.  那把旧椅子给他带来极大的舒适。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 solace</td><td>/ˈsɑːləs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>安慰</td><td>find solace in music<br>在音乐中找到安慰</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She found solace in music during the long months of her illness.  在漫长的病中，她在音乐里找到安慰。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 soothe</td><td>/suːð/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>安慰，抚慰；减轻</td><td>soothe a crying baby<br>安抚哭闹的婴儿</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Soft music helped soothe the restless patient through the long night.  轻柔的音乐帮助安抚了焦躁的病人度过漫长的夜晚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 calm</td><td>/ˈkɑːm/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词、动词、名词</td><td>平静的；使镇静</td><td>calm down quickly<br>很快平静下来</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She spoke in a calm voice until the children settled.  她用平静的声音说话，直到孩子们安静下来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 distress</td><td>/dɪsˈtres/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>痛苦，忧伤；使痛苦</td><td>cause great distress<br>造成极大痛苦</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The sudden news distressed everyone in the small office.  突来的消息让小办公室里的每个人都很难受。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 torment</td><td>/tɔːrˈment/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>折磨，使痛苦；痛苦</td><td>torment the prisoners<br>折磨囚犯</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Guilt tormented him for years after the accident.  事故之后，负罪感折磨了他多年。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 torture</td><td>/ˈtɔːrtʃər/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>折磨，拷打；酷刑</td><td>under torture<br>在酷刑之下</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The long wait for results was pure torture for her.  漫长等待结果对她来说简直是折磨。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>consolidate</b></td><td>/kənˈsɑːlɪdeɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>巩固，加强；合并</td><td>consolidate their power<br>巩固他们的权力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new king moved quickly to consolidate his hold over the northern provinces.  新国王迅速行动，巩固他对北方各省的控制。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 enhance</td><td>/ɪnˈhæns/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>提高，增强，提升</td><td>enhance the flavor<br>提升风味</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A little salt greatly enhances the sweetness of the fruit.  少许盐能大大提升水果的甜味。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 strengthen</td><td>/ˈstreŋθən/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词、不及物动词</td><td>加强，增强</td><td>strengthen the economy<br>增强经济</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Regular exercise will strengthen your heart and lungs.  规律锻炼会增强你的心肺功能。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fortify</td><td>/ˈfɔːrtɪfaɪ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>加固；增强，强化</td><td>fortify a city wall<br>加固城墙</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The milk is fortified with extra vitamin D.  这种牛奶额外强化添加了维生素D。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 reinforce</td><td>/ˌriːɪnˈfɔːrs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>加强，强化</td><td>reinforce the message<br>强化这一信息</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Repeated praise reinforced the child's interest in reading.  反复的表扬强化了孩子对阅读的兴趣。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 abate</td><td>/əˈbeɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>减轻，减弱；减少</td><td>the storm gradually abated<br>风暴逐渐减弱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The fever finally abated after two days of medication.  服药两天后，烧终于退了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 attenuate</td><td>/əˈtenjueɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>减弱，衰减；变细</td><td>attenuate the signal strength<br>减弱信号强度</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Thick concrete walls attenuate the radio signal inside the building.  厚厚的混凝土墙削弱了建筑内部的无线电信号。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 undermine</td><td>/ˈʌndərˌmaɪn/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>削弱，暗中破坏</td><td>undermine confidence<br>削弱信心</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Constant rumors undermined confidence in the young manager.  不断的谣言削弱了人们对这位年轻经理的信心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 weaken</td><td>/ˈwiːkən/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>削弱，使变弱，变衰弱</td><td>weaken the structure<br>使结构变弱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Repeated flooding weakened the foundations of the stone bridge.  反复的洪水削弱了石桥的地基。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>consonant</b></td><td>/ˈkɑːnsənənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>和谐一致的</td><td>consonant with tradition<br>与传统相符</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His modest lifestyle is consonant with the values he preaches.  他朴素的生活方式与他宣扬的价值观相符。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 harmonious</td><td>/hɑːrˈmoʊniəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>和谐的，融洽的；悦耳的</td><td>a harmonious relationship<br>和谐的关系</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two neighbors maintained a harmonious relationship for over thirty years.  这两位邻居三十多年来保持着融洽的关系。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 compatible</td><td>/kəmˈpætəbl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>一致的，能共存的</td><td>compatible with the system<br>与该系统兼容</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new driver is compatible with both older printers.  新驱动与两款旧打印机都兼容。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 congenial</td><td>/kənˈdʒiːniəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>和善的，友好的</td><td>a congenial host<br>一位和善的主人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Our congenial host made every guest feel completely at home.  我们和善的主人让每位客人都感到自在。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 consistent</td><td>/kənˈsɪstənt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>一致的；始终如一的</td><td>consistent with the evidence<br>与证据相符</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His account is consistent with what the neighbors reported.  他的说法与邻居们所述一致。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 congruous</td><td>/ˈkɑŋɡruəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>一致的，适合的，协调的</td><td>congruous with the setting<br>与环境协调</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The plain wooden benches were congruous with the simple chapel.  素木长凳与这座朴素的礼拜堂很协调。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 correspondent</td><td>/ˌkɔːrəˈspɑːndənt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>相符的，一致的；记者</td><td>correspondent to the facts<br>与事实相符</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His testimony was correspondent to the notes taken that night.  他的证词与那晚记下的笔记相符。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 conflicting</td><td>/kənˈflɪktɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>冲突的，矛盾的</td><td>conflicting accounts<br>相互矛盾的说法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Witnesses gave conflicting accounts of the driver's speed.  证人对司机车速的说法相互矛盾。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 dissonant</td><td>/ˈdɪsənənt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不和谐的，不一致的</td><td>dissonant voices<br>不和谐的声音</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A dissonant chord broke the calm mood of the piece.  一个不和谐的和弦打破了乐曲平静的气氛。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 discrepant</td><td>/dɪˈskrɛpənt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有差异的，不一致的</td><td>discrepant figures<br>互不相符的数字</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two audits produced discrepant figures for the same quarter.  两次审计对同一季度得出不一致的数字。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 inconsistent</td><td>/ˌɪnkənˈsɪstənt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不一致的，矛盾的</td><td>inconsistent with the facts<br>与事实不符</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His testimony was inconsistent with the facts established by the police.  他的证词与警方查明的事实不符。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 5

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>conspicuous</b></td><td>/kənˈspɪkjuəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>显而易见的；吸引人的</td><td>conspicuous consumption<br>炫耀性消费</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His bright red jacket made him conspicuous in the gray winter crowd.  他鲜红的夹克让他在灰暗的冬日人群中格外显眼。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 apparent</td><td>/əˈperənt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>明显的；表面上的</td><td>for no apparent reason<br>没有明显原因</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It soon became apparent that the machine had never worked.  很快就明显看出这台机器从未运转过。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 clear</td><td>/ˈklɪr/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>明确的，确凿的；清楚的</td><td>a clear majority<br>明显的多数</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The party won by a clear majority of votes.  该党以明显多数票获胜。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 distinct</td><td>/dɪˈstɪŋkt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>清晰的，明显的</td><td>a distinct impression<br>清晰的印象</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 There is a distinct difference between the two dialects.  这两种方言之间有明显差别。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 evident</td><td>/ˈevɪdənt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>明显的，显然的</td><td>clearly evident<br>显而易见</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her talent for music was evident from a very early age.  她的音乐才华从很小就显而易见。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 manifest</td><td>/ˈmænɪfest/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词、动词</td><td>显然的，明显易懂的；显现，显露</td><td>manifest injustice<br>明显的不公</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His discomfort at the question was manifest to everyone seated around the table.  他对这个问题的不适让围坐桌边的每个人都看得明明白白。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 plain</td><td>/ˈpleɪn/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词、副词</td><td>完全的，十足的；朴素的，清楚的</td><td>plain stupid<br>简直愚蠢</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Ignoring the warning was just plain stupid.  忽视警告纯粹是愚蠢。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 patent</td><td>/ˈpeɪtnt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>显而易见的，明显的；专利</td><td>a patent lie<br>显而易见的谎言</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The excuse was a patent lie and everyone knew it.  这个借口是显而易见的谎言，人人都知道。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 noticeable</td><td>/ˈnoʊtəsəbəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>显而易见的，明显的</td><td>a noticeable change<br>一个明显的变化</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 There was a noticeable pause before he answered the question.  他回答问题前有一个明显的停顿。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 hidden</td><td>/ˈhɪdən/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>隐藏的，隐蔽的</td><td>hidden costs<br>隐藏的费用</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The contract contained hidden fees no one had mentioned.  合同里含有没人提过的隐藏费用。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 concealed</td><td>/kənˈsiːld/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>隐藏的，暗藏的</td><td>a concealed entrance<br>一个隐蔽的入口</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A concealed door behind the shelves led to the cellar.  书架后一扇隐蔽的门通向地窖。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>conspire</b></td><td>/kənˈspaɪər/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>合谋，密谋</td><td>conspire to overthrow<br>密谋推翻</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Several generals conspired to seize power while the president was abroad.  几名将军趁总统出国时密谋夺权。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 plot</td><td>/ˈplɑːt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>密谋，策划；情节</td><td>plot against the king<br>密谋反对国王</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Several officers plotted secretly to seize the northern ports.  几名军官暗中策划夺取北方港口。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 contrive</td><td>/kənˈtraɪv/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>谋划，设法做到；发明</td><td>contrive a meeting<br>设法安排一次会面</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He contrived to leave the party without anyone noticing.  他设法离开了聚会，没被任何人察觉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 connive</td><td>/kəˈnaɪv/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>共谋；纵容，默许坏事</td><td>connive at fraud<br>对欺诈行为暗中纵容</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Two officers connived at the fraud for several years.  两名官员多年来对这起欺诈暗中纵容。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 cogitate</del></td><td>/ˈkɑːdʒɪˌteɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>认真思考，仔细考虑</td><td>cogitate on the problem<br>仔细思考这个问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He sat by the window and cogitated on his next move.  他坐在窗边，仔细思量下一步该怎么走。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 intrigue</td><td>/ˈɪntriːɡ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>阴谋；激起…的兴趣</td><td>political intrigue<br>政治阴谋</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Court records reveal decades of political intrigue behind the sudden fall of the minister.  法庭记录揭示了这位大臣突然倒台背后数十年的政治阴谋。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 put up</td><td>/pʊt ˈʌp/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>共同策划；提供；张贴</td><td>put up a scheme<br>合谋策划一个计划</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two brothers put up the whole story to fool their teacher.  两兄弟合伙编造了整个故事来骗老师。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>constitute</b></td><td>/ˈkɑːnstətuːt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>指派，设立；构成</td><td>constitute a committee<br>设立委员会</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The board was properly constituted under the new rules.  依据新规则，董事会得以正式组成。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 nominate</td><td>/ˈnɑːmɪneɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>提名，推荐；任命</td><td>nominate a candidate<br>提名一位候选人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The staff nominated her for the annual teaching award.  同事们提名她角逐年度教学奖。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 designate</td><td>/ˈdezɪɡneɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>任命；命名</td><td>designate an heir<br>指定继承人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The council designated the whole valley a protected natural area.  议会将整个山谷指定为自然保护区。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 authorize</td><td>/ˈɔːθəˌraɪz/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>授权，批准，委托</td><td>authorize the payment<br>批准这笔付款</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Only the director can authorize spending above that limit.  只有主管能批准超出该限额的开支。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 compose</td><td>/kəmˈpoʊz/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>使镇定；组成；创作</td><td>compose oneself<br>让自己镇定下来</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He took a breath to compose himself before speaking.  他深吸一口气让自己镇定下来才开口。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 comprise</td><td>/kəmˈpraɪz/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>包含，由…组成</td><td>comprise three parts<br>由三部分组成</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The collection comprises nine paintings and a dozen sketches.  这批收藏包括九幅画和十几张素描。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 form</td><td>/ˈfɔːrm/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>构成，形成；形式</td><td>form a committee<br>组成一个委员会</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Six neighbors formed a committee to repair the shared path.  六位邻居组成委员会修理共用的小路。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 discharge</td><td>/dɪsˈtʃɑːrdʒ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>释放；解雇</td><td>discharge the prisoner<br>释放囚犯</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The court discharged the prisoner immediately after the new evidence appeared.  新证据出现后，法庭立即释放了那名囚犯。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 dismiss</td><td>/dɪˈsmɪs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>解散，打发走；解雇；不予考虑</td><td>dismiss the class<br>宣布下课</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The teacher dismissed the class ten minutes ahead of time.  老师提前十分钟宣布下课。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>constrain</b></td><td>/kənˈstreɪn/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>限制</td><td>constrain his freedom<br>限制他的自由</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Strict rules constrain his freedom to travel outside the country.  严格的规定限制了他出国旅行的自由。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bridle</td><td>/ˈbraɪdl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>限制</td><td>bridle his anger<br>克制他的怒气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She bridled her temper and answered the rude question politely.  她克制住脾气，礼貌地回答了那个无礼的问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 check</td><td>/tʃek/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使突然停止，阻止；检查，核对</td><td>check the spread of disease<br>阻止疾病蔓延</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Strict quarantine measures checked the spread of the disease.  严格的隔离措施阻止了疾病的蔓延。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 confine</td><td>/kənˈfaɪn/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>限制</td><td>confine the discussion<br>限定讨论范围</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Please confine the discussion to the budget for this year.  请把讨论限定在今年的预算上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 imprison</td><td>/ˌɪmˈprɪzən/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>关押，监禁，禁闭</td><td>imprison the rebels<br>监禁那些叛乱者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The poet was imprisoned for three years without any trial.  这位诗人未经审判被监禁了三年。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 restrain</td><td>/rɪˈstreɪn/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>抑制，约束，制止</td><td>restrain his temper<br>克制他的脾气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She could hardly restrain her laughter during the speech.  演讲时她几乎忍不住笑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 trammel</td><td>/ˈtræməl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>束缚，限制</td><td>the trammels of custom<br>习俗的束缚</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Old rules trammel the creativity of young designers here.  旧规矩束缚了这里年轻设计师的创造力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 release</td><td>/riˈliːs/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>释放，解除束缚；发布</td><td>release the prisoners<br>释放囚犯</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The court ordered officials to release him from custody at once.  法庭命令官员立即将他从拘留中释放。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del><b>constringe</b></del></td><td>/kənˈstrɪndʒ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使紧缩</td><td>constringe the tissue<br>使组织紧缩</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The cold compress constringed the swollen tissue around the wound.  冷敷使伤口周围肿胀的组织收缩了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 capsule</td><td>/ˈkæpsəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>胶囊；压缩的，简要的</td><td>a capsule summary<br>简要概述</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The editor asked for a capsule version of the long report.  编辑要一份长篇报告的浓缩版本。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 constrict</td><td>/kənsˈtrɪkt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>收缩，压缩；限制</td><td>constrict blood vessels<br>使血管收缩</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Cold air constricts the blood vessels near the skin's surface.  冷空气使皮肤表层的血管收缩。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 narrow</td><td>/ˈnæroʊ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词、动词</td><td>狭窄的；狭隘的</td><td>a narrow outlook<br>狭隘的眼界</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His narrow reading habits left him ignorant of modern poetry.  他狭窄的阅读习惯使他对现代诗一无所知。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 compress</td><td>/kəmˈpres/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>压缩，压紧</td><td>compress the file<br>压缩文件</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 You should compress the file before sending it by email.  用邮件发送之前你应该压缩这个文件。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 squeeze</td><td>/ˈskwiːz/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>挤压，压缩；紧握</td><td>squeeze a lemon<br>挤柠檬</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She squeezed the tube until the last bit of paste came out.  她挤压软管直到最后一点膏体出来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 telescope</td><td>/ˈteləˌskoʊp/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>望远镜；压缩，缩短</td><td>telescope the schedule<br>压缩日程</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The director telescoped the novel's events into a two-hour film.  导演把小说的情节压缩成两小时的电影。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 broaden</td><td>/ˈbrɔːdən/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>使变宽，扩大</td><td>broaden your horizons<br>开阔你的眼界</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Travel abroad can broaden a young student's view of the world.  出国旅行能开阔年轻学生的世界观。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 expand</td><td>/ɪkˈspænd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>扩大，扩张；详述</td><td>expand the business<br>扩大业务</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company plans to expand into three new markets.  公司计划扩张到三个新市场。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 outstretch</del></td><td>/aʊtsˈtretʃ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>伸展，展开；超过</td><td>outstretch both arms<br>伸开双臂</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The eagle outstretched its wings and glided over the valley.  雄鹰展开双翼，滑过山谷。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>construct</b></td><td>/kənˈstrʌkt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>建造，建立；创造，想出</td><td>construct a theory<br>构建一套理论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The lawyer constructed a careful argument from a handful of ordinary documents.  律师用少量普通文件构建出一套严密的论证。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 assemble</td><td>/əˈsembəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>聚集，集合；组装</td><td>assemble a crowd<br>聚集起人群</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Villagers assembled in the square to hear the announcement.  村民聚集在广场上听那则公告。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 build</td><td>/ˈbɪld/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>建造，建立；体格</td><td>build a bridge<br>建造一座桥</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They plan to build a new library on the empty lot downtown.  他们计划在市中心的空地上建一座新图书馆。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fabricate</td><td>/ˈfæbrɪkeɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>捏造</td><td>fabricate evidence<br>伪造证据</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The witness had fabricated evidence to protect his own brother.  那名证人捏造证据来保护自己的兄弟。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 make</td><td>/ˈmeɪk/</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>制造，做成；使成为</td><td>make a wooden chair<br>做一把木椅</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 My grandfather could make a wooden chair with only simple tools.  我祖父只用简单工具就能做出一把木椅。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 produce</td><td>/prəˈduːs/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>生产，制造；产品</td><td>produce steel locally<br>本地生产钢材</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The factory can produce ten thousand units of steel every month.  这家工厂每月能生产一万单位钢材。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rear</td><td>/ˈrɪr/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>养育，培养；竖立，后部</td><td>rear a large family<br>养育一大家子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She had to rear four children alone after her husband died.  丈夫去世后她只能独自养育四个孩子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 set up</td><td>/sɛt ˈʌp/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>建立，创办；安装，搭建</td><td>set up a company<br>创办一家公司</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They set up a small company in the garage two years ago.  两年前他们在车库里创办了一家小公司。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 contrive</td><td>/kənˈtraɪv/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>谋划，设法做到；发明</td><td>contrive a meeting<br>设法安排一次会面</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He contrived to leave the party without anyone noticing.  他设法离开了聚会，没被任何人察觉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 devise</td><td>/dɪˈvaɪz/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>想出，设计，策划</td><td>devise a plan<br>想出一个计划</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The team devised a clever method for testing the old samples.  团队想出了检验旧样本的巧妙方法。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 excogitate</del></td><td>/ɛksˈkɑdʒəteɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>苦心想出，仔细谋划</td><td>excogitate a new plan<br>苦心构思新方案</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The engineers excogitated a clever solution after weeks of failure.  几周失败后，工程师们苦心想出了一个巧妙的方案。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 vamp up</td><td>/væmp ˈʌp/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>拼凑而成；修补翻新</td><td>vamp up an old story<br>拼凑翻新旧故事</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The lazy writer simply vamped up an old story for the magazine.  这个懒作家只是把旧故事拼凑翻新给杂志。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 demolish</td><td>/dɪˈmɑːlɪʃ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>拆除，摧毁；驳倒</td><td>demolish an old building<br>拆除旧楼</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The city decided to demolish the unsafe old building last spring.  去年春天市政府决定拆除这栋危旧建筑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 destroy</td><td>/dɪsˈtrɔɪ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>毁灭，破坏</td><td>destroy the evidence<br>销毁证据</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The fire destroyed nearly every document stored in the basement.  大火毁掉了地下室里几乎所有文件。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 raze</td><td>/reɪz/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>彻底摧毁，拆为平地</td><td>raze a village<br>将村庄彻底摧毁</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The invading army razed the village and burned every field around it.  入侵军队将村庄彻底摧毁，并烧掉了周围所有田地。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>consummate</b></td><td>/ˈkɑːnsəmət/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>专业的，有造诣的；无纰漏的，完美的</td><td>a consummate professional<br>极为专业的行家</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She handled the hostile interview with the ease of a consummate diplomat.  她像老练的外交家一样从容应对了这场充满敌意的采访。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 accomplished</td><td>/əˈkɑːmplɪʃt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>技艺精湛的，有造诣的</td><td>an accomplished pianist<br>技艺精湛的钢琴家</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She is an accomplished pianist who has toured three continents.  她是位技艺精湛的钢琴家，已在三大洲巡演。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 finished</td><td>/ˈfɪnɪʃt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>技艺完美的；完成的</td><td>a finished performance<br>炉火纯青的演出</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The young dancer already gives a remarkably finished performance.  这位年轻舞者的表演已经相当炉火纯青。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 virtuosic</td><td>/ˌvɜrtʃuˈɑsɪk/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>技艺超凡的，大师级的</td><td>virtuosic violin playing<br>超凡的小提琴演奏</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics praised his virtuosic violin playing throughout the long concerto.  评论家称赞他整首长协奏曲中超凡的小提琴演奏。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 versed</td><td>/vɜːrst/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>熟知的；精通的</td><td>well versed in history<br>精通历史</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He is well versed in both classical and modern poetry.  他对古典诗歌和现代诗歌都很精通。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 veteran</td><td>/ˈvetərən/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>老兵；有丰富经验的人</td><td>a veteran journalist<br>资深记者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A veteran journalist, she has covered four wars and six elections.  作为资深记者，她报道过四场战争和六次选举。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 flawless</td><td>/ˈflɔːləs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无瑕的，完美无缺的</td><td>a flawless diamond<br>毫无瑕疵的钻石</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her flawless pronunciation surprised everyone in the language class.  她毫无瑕疵的发音令语言课上所有人惊讶。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 impeccable</td><td>/ɪmˈpekəbl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无瑕的，无可挑剔的</td><td>impeccable manners<br>无可挑剔的举止</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her impeccable manners impressed even the most demanding guests.  她无可挑剔的举止让最挑剔的客人都印象深刻。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 perfect</td><td>/ˈpɜːrfɪkt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词、及物动词</td><td>完美的；使完善</td><td>a perfect score<br>满分成绩</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He spent years trying to perfect his technique on the cello.  他花了数年时间钻研以完善自己的大提琴技巧。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 amateur</td><td>/ˈæməˌtɜːr/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>业余爱好者；外行的</td><td>a talented amateur<br>有天赋的业余者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The comet was first spotted by an amateur astronomer.  这颗彗星最先由一位业余天文学家发现。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 defective</td><td>/dɪˈfektɪv/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有缺陷的，有瑕疵的</td><td>a defective product<br>有瑕疵的产品</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company recalled thousands of defective batteries after the accident.  事故后公司召回了数千个有缺陷的电池。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>contagious</b></td><td>/kənˈteɪdʒəs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>传染的；（情绪等）唤起共鸣的</td><td>a contagious disease<br>传染性疾病</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His laughter was so contagious that the whole room was soon giggling.  他的笑声太能唤起共鸣，整个房间很快都笑了起来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 infectious</td><td>/ˌɪnˈfekʃəs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>传染性的；有感染力的</td><td>infectious laughter<br>有感染力的笑声</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her infectious laughter soon had the whole room smiling.  她那富有感染力的笑声很快让全屋人都笑了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pestilent</td><td>/ˈpɛstələnt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>引起瘟疫的；有害的</td><td>pestilent swamp air<br>招致瘟疫的沼气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Settlers blamed the pestilent swamp air for the summer fevers.  移民把夏季的热病归咎于招致瘟疫的沼气。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 transmissible</td><td>/trænsˈmɪsəbəl/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>可传播的，可传染的</td><td>a transmissible virus<br>可传播的病毒</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Researchers warned that the virus is highly transmissible through the air.  研究者警告该病毒极易通过空气传播。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 epidemic</td><td>/ˌepəˈdemɪk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>流行病；流行的</td><td>control the epidemic<br>控制流行病</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Health officials worked around the clock to control the epidemic.  卫生官员昼夜不停地工作以控制这场流行病。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 catching</td><td>/ˈkætʃɪŋ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>传染的；有感染力的</td><td>the flu is catching<br>流感会传染</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Stay home, because that kind of flu is very catching.  待在家里吧，那种流感很容易传染。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 spreading</td><td>/ˈspredɪŋ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>蔓延的，扩散的</td><td>a spreading infection<br>蔓延的感染</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Doctors struggled to stop the spreading infection in the ward.  医生们努力阻止病房里蔓延的感染。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 incommunicable</del></td><td>/ˌɪnkəˈmjunɪkəbəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不可传染的；难以言表的</td><td>an incommunicable disease<br>不会传染的疾病</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The doctor assured us the condition was entirely incommunicable.  医生向我们保证这种病完全不会传染。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>contaminate</b></td><td>/kənˈtæmɪneɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>污染，感染</td><td>contaminate the water supply<br>污染供水</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Leaking chemicals contaminated the soil around the factory.  泄漏的化学品污染了工厂周围的土壤。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pollute</td><td>/pəˈluːt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>污染，弄脏</td><td>pollute the environment<br>污染环境</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Factories along the river have polluted the water for decades.  沿河的工厂几十年来一直在污染水体。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 defile</td><td>/dɪˈfaɪl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>玷污，污损；亵渎</td><td>defile a sacred site<br>亵渎圣地</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Vandals defiled the ancient temple with paint and broken glass.  破坏者用油漆和碎玻璃亵渎了那座古庙。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 taint</td><td>/teɪnt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>使（品质）污损</td><td>taint someone's reputation<br>损害某人名誉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The scandal tainted his reputation for the rest of his career.  这场丑闻在他余下的职业生涯中都损害着他的名声。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 infect</td><td>/ˌɪnˈfekt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>感染，传染；影响</td><td>infect the water supply<br>污染供水</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Careless handling can easily infect the wound with bacteria.  处理不当很容易使伤口感染细菌。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 purify</td><td>/ˈpjʊrəˌfaɪ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>净化，使纯净</td><td>purify the water<br>净化水质</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 These tablets purify water so it becomes safe to drink.  这些药片能净化水，使其可安全饮用。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 sanitize</td><td>/ˈsænɪˌtaɪz/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>消毒，净化；删改</td><td>sanitize the surfaces<br>给台面消毒</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Staff must sanitize every table before the next guests arrive.  员工必须在下批客人到来前给每张桌子消毒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>content</b></td><td>/ˈkɑːntent/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>内容，内涵；使满足</td><td>the content of the letter<br>信的内容</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The teacher praised the style but questioned the content of my essay.  老师赞赏我文章的文风，却质疑其内容。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 satisfy</td><td>/ˈsætɪsfaɪ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使满意；使确信</td><td>satisfy the skeptics<br>让怀疑者信服</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The extra evidence satisfied the skeptics who had doubted his story.  额外的证据让怀疑他说法的人信服了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 gratify</td><td>/ˈɡrætɪfaɪ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使满足，使高兴</td><td>gratify a desire<br>满足某种欲望</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It gratified her to see her students succeed.  看到学生成功让她很欣慰。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rejoice</td><td>/rɪˈdʒɔɪs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>欣喜，喜悦</td><td>rejoice at the news<br>为消息而欣喜</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The whole village rejoiced at the news of the safe return.  听到平安归来的消息，全村都欣喜不已。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 intention</td><td>/ˌɪnˈtentʃən/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>意图，目的</td><td>with good intention<br>出于好意</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He announced his intention to resign at the end of the year.  他宣布了年底辞职的意图。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 purport</td><td>/pərˈpɔːrt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>声称，打算</td><td>purport to be an expert<br>自称是专家</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Several websites purport to offer free medical advice from real doctors.  有几个网站声称提供真医生的免费医疗建议。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sense</td><td>/ˈsens/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>意思，含义；感觉</td><td>in a broad sense<br>广义上讲</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 In a broad sense, every artist borrows from earlier traditions.  广义上讲，每位艺术家都借鉴前人的传统。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 significance</td><td>/səɡˈnɪfɪkəns/</td><td>★★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>意义，含义；重要性</td><td>of great significance<br>意义重大</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Scholars still debate the significance of these ancient carvings.  学者们仍在争论这些古代雕刻的含义。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 discontent</td><td>/ˌdɪskənˈtent/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>不满，不满意</td><td>widespread discontent<br>普遍的不满</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Rising prices caused growing discontent among the workers.  物价上涨引起工人越来越大的不满。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 6

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>contentious</b></td><td>/kənˈtenʃəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>引起争论的；好争论的，好战的</td><td>a contentious issue<br>有争议的议题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Land ownership remains the most contentious issue in the peace negotiations.  土地所有权仍是和谈中最有争议的议题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 controversial</td><td>/ˌkɑːntrəˈvɜːrʃəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有争议的，引起争论的</td><td>a controversial decision<br>有争议的决定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The judge's controversial ruling was debated for months afterward.  法官那项有争议的裁决之后被讨论了数月。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 disputatious</del></td><td>/ˌdɪspjuˈteɪʃəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>好争辩的，爱吵架的</td><td>a disputatious committee<br>好争辩的委员会</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The disputatious committee argued for hours without reaching any decision.  这个好争辩的委员会争了几小时也没有决定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 polemical</td><td>/pəˈleməkəl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>争论的，好辩的</td><td>a polemical essay<br>论战性的文章</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His polemical essay on education provoked replies from many teachers.  他那篇关于教育的论战文章引来许多教师回应。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 belligerent</td><td>/bəˈlɪdʒərənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>好斗的，好战的</td><td>a belligerent tone<br>好斗的口气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He answered the question in a belligerent tone that shocked us.  他用一种令我们震惊的好斗口气回答了这个问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bellicose</td><td>/ˈbeləˌkoʊs/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>好战的，挑起争斗的</td><td>bellicose rhetoric<br>好战的言辞</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The general's bellicose rhetoric alarmed the neighboring countries.  这位将军的好战言辞令邻国警觉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 combative</td><td>/kəmˈbætɪv/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>好斗的，好争辩的</td><td>a combative tone<br>好斗的语气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He answered every question in a combative tone.  他用好斗的语气回答每一个问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 truculent</td><td>/ˈtrʌkjələnt/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>好战的，好斗的；粗野的</td><td>a truculent response<br>咄咄逼人的回应</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The truculent teenager argued with every teacher who tried to correct him.  这个好斗的少年跟每位试图纠正他的老师都吵。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 litigious</td><td>/lɪˈtɪdʒəs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>好诉讼的；爱争吵的</td><td>a litigious society<br>好打官司的社会</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 In such a litigious society, doctors buy expensive insurance.  在这样一个好打官司的社会里，医生都购买昂贵保险。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pugnacious</td><td>/pʌɡˈneɪʃəs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>好斗的，爱争吵的</td><td>a pugnacious debater<br>好争辩的辩手</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The pugnacious little dog barked at every passing car.  这只好斗的小狗对每辆过路的车都叫。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 scrappy</td><td>/ˈskræpi/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>好斗的，好吵架的</td><td>a scrappy little dog<br>爱打架的小狗</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A scrappy little dog guarded the yard and challenged every visitor.  一只爱打架的小狗看守院子，向每位来客叫阵。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 dovish</td><td>/ˈdʌvɪʃ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>鸽派的，爱好和平的</td><td>a dovish policy<br>鸽派政策</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The bank adopted a dovish policy and kept interest rates unusually low.  央行采取鸽派政策，把利率维持在异常低的水平。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 peace-loving</td><td>/ˈpis ˌlʌvɪŋ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>爱好和平的</td><td>a peace-loving nation<br>爱好和平的国家</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They describe themselves as a peace-loving nation with no territorial claims.  他们自称是没有领土要求的爱好和平的国家。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>contiguous</b></td><td>/kənˈtɪɡjuəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>邻接的，毗连的</td><td>contiguous states<br>相邻的各州</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The farm consists of three contiguous fields separated only by low hedges.  这座农场由三块仅以矮树篱相隔的毗连田地组成。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 adjacent</td><td>/əˈdʒeɪsənt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>邻近的，毗连的</td><td>adjacent rooms<br>相邻的房间</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The hotel gave us two adjacent rooms on the fifth floor.  酒店给了我们五楼两间相邻的房间。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 abutting</td><td>/əˈbʌtɪŋ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>毗邻的，接界的</td><td>the abutting property<br>毗邻的地产</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A tall fence separates our garden from the abutting property.  一道高栅栏把我们的花园与毗邻的地产隔开。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 neighboring</td><td>/ˈneɪbərɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>邻近的，附近的</td><td>a neighboring village<br>邻村</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Farmers from the neighboring village came to help with the harvest.  邻村的农民赶来帮着收割。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 Juxtaposed</td><td>/ˌdʒʌkstəˈpoʊzd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>并置的，紧挨排列的</td><td>juxtaposed images<br>并置的图像</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The exhibition showed juxtaposed images of the city before and after the war.  展览展示了并置的战前战后城市图像。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 verging</td><td>/ˈvɜːrdʒɪŋ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>接近的，濒临的</td><td>verging on the border<br>紧靠边界</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their farmland lies at the park's eastern boundary, verging on protected forest.  他们的农田位于公园东界，紧邻受保护的森林。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 apart</td><td>/əˈpɑːrt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>副词</td><td>分开地，相隔地</td><td>drift apart over time<br>随时间渐渐疏远</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two brothers grew apart after their father's death.  父亲去世后两兄弟渐渐疏远了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 separate</td><td>/ˈsepəreɪt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>动词、形容词</td><td>分开，分离</td><td>separate the yolks<br>把蛋黄分离出来</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Please separate the recyclable waste from the ordinary rubbish.  请把可回收垃圾与普通垃圾分开。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>contort</b></td><td>/kənˈtɔːrt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>扭曲</td><td>contort his face<br>把脸扭曲</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Pain contorted his face as the doctor examined the swollen ankle.  医生检查肿起的脚踝时，疼痛使他面容扭曲。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 deform</td><td>/ˌdiːˈfɔːrm/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使变形，使畸形</td><td>deform the metal frame<br>使金属框变形</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Heat can deform the plastic casing within a few minutes.  高温几分钟内就能使塑料外壳变形。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 distort</td><td>/dɪˈstɔːrt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>扭曲，歪曲</td><td>distort the facts<br>歪曲事实</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Editing the tape that way would distort the facts of the interview.  那样剪辑录音会歪曲采访的事实。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 warp</td><td>/wɔːrp/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>曲解</td><td>warp the truth<br>曲解真相</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A careless English translation had warped the author's original meaning.  一个粗心的英文译本曲解了作者的原意。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 misshape</del></td><td>/ˌmɪsˈʃeɪp/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使变形，使畸形</td><td>misshape the soft clay<br>把软陶弄变形</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Careless packing had misshaped every hat in the box.  粗心的包装把箱里每顶帽子都弄变形了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>contract</b></td><td>/ˈkɑːnˌtrækt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>契约，合同；收缩；感染疾病</td><td>sign a contract<br>签订合同</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Both parties signed the contract without reading the final clause carefully.  双方都没有仔细读最后一条就签了合同。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 agreement</td><td>/əˈɡriːmənt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>一致，同意；协议</td><td>come to an agreement<br>达成协议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two firms signed an agreement to share research costs.  两家公司签署协议分担研究费用。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 compact</td><td>/ˈkɑːmpækt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>契约，协议；紧凑的</td><td>sign a compact<br>签订协议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two nations signed a compact to protect the shared river.  两国签订协议保护共有的河流。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 convention</td><td>/kənˈvenʃn/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>常规，习俗；大会，集会</td><td>an annual convention<br>年度大会</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Thousands of teachers attended the annual convention held in the capital.  数千名教师参加了在首都举行的年度大会。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 treaty</td><td>/ˈtriːti/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>条约，协定</td><td>sign a peace treaty<br>签署和平条约</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two nations finally signed a peace treaty after years of war.  两国经过多年战争终于签署了和平条约。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 compress</td><td>/kəmˈpres/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>压缩，压紧</td><td>compress the file<br>压缩文件</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 You should compress the file before sending it by email.  用邮件发送之前你应该压缩这个文件。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 condense</td><td>/kənˈdens/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>压缩，缩短；使凝结</td><td>condense the article<br>压缩这篇文章</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She managed to condense three chapters into a single clear page.  她把三章内容压缩成清晰的一页。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 concentrate</td><td>/ˈkɑːnsntreɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>浓缩；聚集</td><td>concentrate the solution<br>浓缩溶液</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Boil the syrup slowly in order to concentrate the solution.  慢慢熬煮糖浆以便浓缩溶液。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 catch</td><td>/ˈkætʃ/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>染上（疾病）；抓住</td><td>catch a cold<br>感冒</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 You will catch a cold if you go out with wet hair.  你要是湿着头发出门就会感冒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 get</td><td>/ˈɡet/</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>患上（疾病）；获得</td><td>get the flu<br>得流感</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Half the office got the flu during that freezing week.  那个严寒的星期里办公室一半人得了流感。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sicken</td><td>/ˈsɪkən/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>生病，患病；使厌恶</td><td>sicken and die<br>患病死去</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Many of the sheep sickened after drinking from the polluted stream.  许多羊喝了污染的溪水后患病。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 expand</td><td>/ɪkˈspænd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>扩大，扩张；详述</td><td>expand the business<br>扩大业务</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company plans to expand into three new markets.  公司计划扩张到三个新市场。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 heal</td><td>/ˈhiːl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>治愈，痊愈</td><td>heal a wound<br>治愈伤口</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The deep cut took nearly a month to heal completely.  那道深伤口花了近一个月才完全愈合。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>contravene</b></td><td>/ˌkɑːntrəˈviːn/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>违反，反对</td><td>contravene the rules<br>违反规定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Building a factory here would contravene several local environmental regulations.  在此建厂会违反数条当地环保法规。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 breach</td><td>/briːtʃ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>违背</td><td>breach the contract<br>违约</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company breached the contract by delivering the goods late.  这家公司延迟交货，违反了合同。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 infringe</td><td>/ɪnˈfrɪndʒ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>违反；侵犯（权利）</td><td>infringe on his rights<br>侵犯他的权利</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new rule infringes on the privacy of every employee.  新规定侵犯了每位员工的隐私。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 transgress</td><td>/trænzˈɡres/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>违背；犯错</td><td>transgress the rules<br>违反规则</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Any player who transgresses the rules will be removed from the tournament.  任何违反规则的选手都将被逐出这场比赛。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fracture</td><td>/ˈfræktʃər/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>破裂</td><td>a hairline fracture<br>一道细微裂缝</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The scan revealed a hairline fracture in his left wrist.  扫描显示他左手腕有一道细微裂缝。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 deny</td><td>/dɪˈnaɪ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>否认；拒绝给予</td><td>flatly deny the charge<br>断然否认指控</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 I do not deny that the plan carries some very real risks.  我不否认这个计划确实带有一些实实在在的风险。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 contradict</td><td>/ˌkɑːntrəˈdɪkt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>否认，反驳；与…相矛盾</td><td>contradict the evidence<br>与证据相矛盾</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The witness's second statement flatly contradicted everything she had said earlier.  证人的第二份陈述与她此前所说完全矛盾。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 gainsay</td><td>/ˌɡeɪnˈseɪ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>否认</td><td>gainsay the evidence<br>否认证据</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 No one could gainsay the evidence the inspector had collected.  没有人能否认检查员收集到的证据。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 reject</td><td>/rɪˈdʒekt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>拒绝，驳回；废品</td><td>reject the proposal<br>否决提案</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The committee voted to reject the proposal without further discussion.  委员会投票否决了该提案，不再讨论。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 uphold</td><td>/ʌpˈhoʊld/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>支持，赞成；维持（原判）</td><td>uphold a decision<br>维持原判决</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The appeals court chose to uphold the original ruling in full.  上诉法院决定完全维持原判。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 support</td><td>/səˈpɔːrt/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>支持，支撑</td><td>support a proposal<br>支持一项提议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Most members voted to support the new proposal.  大多数成员投票支持这项新提案。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 buttress</td><td>/ˈbʌtrɪs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>支持，加强（论点）</td><td>buttress an argument<br>为论点提供支撑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Fresh evidence buttressed the prosecutor's case against the two suspects.  新证据支持了检察官针对两名嫌疑人的指控。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>contrite</b></td><td>/kənˈtraɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>（因为有罪孽或过错而感到）后悔悲痛的</td><td>a contrite apology<br>悔痛的道歉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He looked genuinely contrite as he apologized for breaking the window.  他为打破窗户道歉时显得真心悔痛。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 compunctious</del></td><td>/kəmˈpʌŋkʃəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>内疚的，懊悔的</td><td>a compunctious glance<br>内疚的一瞥</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He gave her a compunctious glance and quietly returned the money.  他内疚地看了她一眼，悄悄把钱还了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 regrettable</td><td>/rɪˈɡretəbəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>令人遗憾的，可惜的</td><td>a regrettable mistake<br>令人遗憾的错误</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The manager called the delay a regrettable but unavoidable mistake.  经理称这次延误是令人遗憾但无法避免的错误。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 remorseful</td><td>/rɪˈmɔːrsfəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>悔恨的，自责的</td><td>look deeply remorseful<br>显得深深悔恨</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The young thief seemed genuinely remorseful in front of the judge.  这个年轻小偷在法官面前显得真心悔恨。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 apologetic</td><td>/əˌpɑːləˈdʒetɪk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>道歉的，愧疚的</td><td>an apologetic smile<br>带着歉意的微笑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She sent an apologetic note explaining why she had missed dinner.  她送来一张致歉的便条，解释为何缺席晚餐。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 penitent</td><td>/ˈpenɪtənt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>悔过的</td><td>a penitent sinner<br>悔过的罪人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The penitent thief returned the money and apologized to the shopkeeper.  那个悔过的小偷退还了钱，并向店主道歉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 repentant</td><td>/rɪˈpentənt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>悔过的</td><td>sound repentant<br>听来悔过</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The thief looked genuinely repentant as he returned the money to its owner.  那名小偷把钱还给主人时显得真心悔过。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rueful</td><td>/ˈruːfəl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>悔恨的，懊悔的</td><td>a rueful smile<br>懊悔的苦笑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He admitted his error with a rueful smile and a shrug.  他带着懊悔的苦笑和一个耸肩承认了错误。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 impenitent</del></td><td>/ɪmˈpenɪtənt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不悔悟的，死不认错的</td><td>an impenitent offender<br>毫无悔意的罪犯</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The thief remained impenitent even after hearing the victims speak.  即便听了受害者的陈述，窃贼仍毫无悔意。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unrepentant</td><td>/ˌʌnrɪˈpentənt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不悔悟的，毫无悔意的</td><td>remain unrepentant<br>仍毫无悔意</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Even after the verdict he remained completely unrepentant about the fraud.  即使判决之后，他对这场欺诈仍毫无悔意。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del><b>contumacious</b></del></td><td>/ˌkɑːntuˈmeɪʃəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不服从的，倔强的</td><td>a contumacious witness<br>不服从的证人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The contumacious witness was fined for defying the judge.  那名抗命的证人因违抗法官而被罚款。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 balky</td><td>/ˈbɔːlki/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>执拗的，不肯前进的</td><td>a balky engine<br>不听使唤的引擎</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The balky engine refused to start on the coldest morning.  在最冷的那个早晨，这台不听使唤的引擎怎么也发动不起来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 defiant</td><td>/dɪˈfaɪənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>公然违抗的，挑战的</td><td>a defiant attitude<br>挑战的态度</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The defiant student refused to apologize to his teacher.  这个公然抗拒的学生拒绝向老师道歉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 insubordinate</td><td>/ɪnsəˈbɔːrdənət/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不服从的，抗命的</td><td>insubordinate behavior<br>抗命的行为</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The officer was discharged for insubordinate conduct at sea.  这名军官因在海上抗命行为被开除。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 intractable</td><td>/ˌɪnˈtræktəbəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>难对付的，棘手的</td><td>an intractable problem<br>棘手的问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Poverty remains an intractable problem in many rural regions.  贫困在许多农村地区仍是棘手的问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 obstreperous</td><td>/əbˈstrepərəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不听话的，任性的</td><td>an obstreperous child<br>不听话的孩子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 An obstreperous child kept kicking the seat in front of him.  一个吵闹不驯的孩子一直踢着前排的座椅。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rebellious</td><td>/rɪˈbeljəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>反叛的，难管束的</td><td>a rebellious teenager<br>叛逆的少年</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her rebellious streak showed early, long before high school.  她的叛逆性格早早就显露，远在高中之前。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 recalcitrant</td><td>/rɪˈkælsɪtrənt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>顽抗的，不顺从的</td><td>a recalcitrant employee<br>不听劝的员工</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A recalcitrant employee refused every instruction his new supervisor gave him.  一名顽抗的员工拒绝了新主管给他的每一条指示。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 refractory</td><td>/rɪˈfræktəri/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>倔强的，不顺从的</td><td>a refractory pupil<br>不顺从的学生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 One refractory pupil disrupted the lesson for the entire class.  一个不顺从的学生扰乱了全班的课。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 restive</td><td>/ˈrestɪv/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>焦躁不安的；不驯服的</td><td>a restive crowd<br>焦躁不安的人群</td></tr>
