<tr><td colspan="7">例 The audience became restive when the concert started forty minutes late.  音乐会迟了四十分钟才开始，观众变得焦躁不安。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ungovernable</td><td>/ʌnˈɡʌvərnəbl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>难以控制的，无法管束的</td><td>an ungovernable temper<br>无法控制的脾气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The crowd became ungovernable once the gates were opened.  大门一开，人群就变得无法控制。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unruly</td><td>/ʌnˈruːli/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>难驾驭的，不守规矩的</td><td>an unruly crowd<br>不守规矩的人群</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Two teachers struggled to calm the unruly class.  两位老师费力地安抚那个不守规矩的班级。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 untoward</td><td>/ʌnˈtɔːrd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不幸的；难以驯服的，难以驾驭的</td><td>an untoward incident<br>意外的不幸事件</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Nothing untoward happened during the long flight across the ocean.  在这趟跨洋长途飞行中没有发生任何不幸的意外。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 wayward</td><td>/ˈweɪwərd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>任性的，不服管束的</td><td>a wayward son<br>不服管束的儿子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their wayward son ignored every rule his patient parents set.  他们不服管束的儿子无视父母耐心定下的每条规矩。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 willful</td><td>/ˈwɪlfl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>固执的，倔强的</td><td>willful disobedience<br>故意的违抗</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His willful refusal to ask for help delayed the repair by weeks.  他固执地拒绝求助，使维修推迟了好几周。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 obedient</td><td>/əˈbiːdiənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>服从的，顺从的</td><td>an obedient child<br>听话的孩子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The dog sat quietly, obedient to every hand signal.  那只狗安静地坐着，服从每个手势。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 docile</td><td>/ˈdɑːsəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>温顺的，听话的</td><td>a docile animal<br>温顺的动物</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old horse was docile enough for beginners to ride.  这匹老马很温顺，初学者也能骑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 ruly</del></td><td>/ˈruli/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>守规矩的，服管的</td><td>a ruly classroom<br>守规矩的教室</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new teacher was surprised to find such a ruly classroom.  新老师惊讶地发现这个教室如此守规矩。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>conundrum</b></td><td>/kəˈnʌndrəm/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>难解的问题，谜</td><td>face a difficult conundrum<br>面对棘手难题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 How to feed the city without more farmland is a real conundrum.  如何在不增加农田的情况下养活这座城市是个真正的难题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 enigma</td><td>/ɪˈnɪɡmə/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>难以理解或解释的事物，谜</td><td>remain an enigma<br>仍是个谜</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His sudden disappearance that autumn remains an enigma to local historians.  他那年秋天的突然失踪对当地史学家来说仍是个谜。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mystery</td><td>/ˈmɪstəri/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>谜，神秘事物</td><td>solve the mystery<br>解开谜团</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Detectives worked for years before they solved the mystery.  侦探们工作数年才解开这个谜团。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 puzzlement</td><td>/ˈpʌzəlmənt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>困惑，迷惑</td><td>a look of puzzlement<br>困惑的表情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A look of puzzlement crossed her face when she read the note.  读那张便条时，她脸上掠过困惑的表情。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 riddle</td><td>/ˈrɪdəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>谜语，难题</td><td>answer a riddle<br>解答谜语</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old man asked a riddle that nobody in the village could answer.  老人问了一个村里没人能答的谜语。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 secret</td><td>/ˈsiːkrət/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>秘密的，隐秘的；秘密</td><td>keep a secret<br>保守秘密</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They held secret meetings in the basement for several months.  他们在地下室秘密开会数月。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>convalesce</b></td><td>/ˌkɑːnvəˈles/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>渐渐康复，渐愈</td><td>convalesce at home<br>在家中养病康复</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She spent three quiet weeks convalescing at home after the surgery.  手术后她在家静养康复了三周。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 heal</td><td>/ˈhiːl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>治愈，痊愈</td><td>heal a wound<br>治愈伤口</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The deep cut took nearly a month to heal completely.  那道深伤口花了近一个月才完全愈合。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 recover</td><td>/rɪˈkʌvər/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>康复；恢复，找回</td><td>recover from illness<br>病后康复</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It took him two months to recover from the serious operation.  他花了两个月才从这场大手术中康复。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 recuperate</td><td>/rɪˈkuːpəreɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>康复，恢复（健康或体力）</td><td>recuperate from an illness<br>病后康复</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He spent two weeks at the coast recuperating from surgery.  他在海边待了两周，从手术中康复。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 recoup</td><td>/rɪˈkuːp/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>恢复，复原；补偿</td><td>recoup his strength<br>恢复体力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After the long march the soldiers rested to recoup their strength.  长途行军后，士兵们休息以恢复体力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 snap back</td><td>/snæp ˈbæk/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>迅速恢复，反弹</td><td>snap back quickly<br>迅速恢复</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Young athletes usually snap back quickly after a minor injury.  年轻运动员在轻伤后通常恢复得很快。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 aggravate</td><td>/ˈæɡrəveɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>加重，恶化；激怒</td><td>aggravate the situation<br>使局势恶化</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Running on the injury will only aggravate it.  带着伤跑步只会让伤势加重。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 deteriorate</td><td>/dɪˈtɪriəreɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>恶化，变坏</td><td>deteriorate rapidly<br>迅速恶化</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her health deteriorated sharply during the cold winter.  在寒冷的冬天里她的健康急剧恶化。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 intensify</td><td>/ɪnˈtensɪfaɪ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>加强，激化</td><td>intensify the pressure<br>加大压力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The rain intensified just as we reached the summit.  我们刚到山顶，雨就更大了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>cloak</b></td><td>/kloʊk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>遮掩，隐藏</td><td>cloak the truth<br>掩盖真相</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Officials cloaked the truth behind pages of confusing statistics.  官员用一堆令人困惑的统计数字掩盖真相。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 belie</td><td>/bɪˈlaɪ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>与…相对立；与…相矛盾</td><td>belie his claims<br>与他的说法矛盾</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company's own records belie his claims about the accident.  公司自己的记录与他关于事故的说法相矛盾。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 camouflage</td><td>/ˈkæməflɑːʒ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>伪装，掩饰；伪装物</td><td>military camouflage<br>军事伪装</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The soldiers used branches and mud as camouflage in the forest.  士兵们用树枝和泥浆在森林里作伪装。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 conceal</td><td>/kənˈsiːl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>隐藏，隐瞒</td><td>conceal the truth<br>隐瞒真相</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The witness tried to conceal the truth from the police.  证人试图向警方隐瞒真相。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cover</td><td>/ˈkʌvər/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>掩盖，遮盖；覆盖物</td><td>cover up the truth<br>掩盖真相</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They tried to cover up the truth with a vague statement.  他们试图用含糊的声明掩盖真相。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 curtain</td><td>/ˈkɜːrtən/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>遮蔽，掩盖；窗帘</td><td>curtain off the room<br>用帘子隔开房间</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Heavy drapes curtained the windows and hid the room from view.  厚重的帷幔遮住窗户，把房间藏了起来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 disguise</td><td>/dɪsˈɡaɪz/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>伪装，掩饰；伪装</td><td>disguise his intentions<br>掩饰他的意图</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He disguised his disappointment behind a polite and steady smile.  他用礼貌平静的微笑掩饰了失望。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mask</td><td>/ˈmæsk/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>掩盖，掩饰；面具</td><td>mask his anger<br>掩饰他的怒气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Loud music masked the sound of the approaching helicopter.  响亮的音乐掩盖了直升机靠近的声音。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 obscure</td><td>/əbˈskjʊr/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>含义模糊的；平凡的，不知名的</td><td>an obscure poet<br>不知名的诗人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He remained an obscure poet until a critic rediscovered his work decades later.  他一直是个不知名的诗人，直到几十年后一位批评家重新发现他的作品。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 occult</td><td>/əˈkʌlt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>难以理解的</td><td>occult knowledge<br>玄奥的知识</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The manuscript is filled with occult knowledge that scholars still cannot decipher.  这份手稿满是学者至今无法解读的玄奥知识。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 screen</td><td>/skriːn/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>掩护物，屏障；掩护，遮蔽</td><td>a screen of trees<br>一道树木屏障</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A thick screen of trees hid the old house from the road.  一道浓密的树木屏障把老屋从路上遮住了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sham</td><td>/ʃæm/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词、形容词</td><td>掩饰，假装；虚假的</td><td>a sham marriage<br>假婚姻</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The couple admitted their marriage was a sham arranged for the visa.  这对夫妻承认他们的婚姻是为签证安排的假婚姻。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 shroud</td><td>/ʃraʊd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>将…从视线中隔离，遮蔽</td><td>shrouded in mist<br>笼罩在雾中</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Thick mist shrouded the harbor and hid the ships from view.  浓雾笼罩港口，把船只从视线中遮住。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 suppress</td><td>/səˈpres/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>用暴力终止，镇压；封锁，删除</td><td>suppress a rebellion<br>镇压叛乱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The regime used the army to suppress a rebellion in the capital.  该政权动用军队镇压首都的叛乱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 veil</td><td>/ˈveɪl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>遮掩，掩饰；面纱</td><td>veil his true motives<br>掩饰真实动机</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Polite words could not veil his true motives for long.  客气话没能长久掩饰他的真实动机。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 debunk</td><td>/ˌdiːˈbʌŋk/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>揭穿…的真面目</td><td>debunk the myth<br>揭穿这个神话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A single careful experiment debunked the theory that had misled everyone.  一次严谨的实验揭穿了那个误导所有人的理论。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 disclose</td><td>/dɪsˈkloʊz/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>揭露，公开，透露</td><td>disclose the details<br>公开细节</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company refused to disclose the details of the settlement.  公司拒绝公开和解协议的细节。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 display</td><td>/dɪsˈpleɪ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>展示，陈列；显露</td><td>display the paintings<br>展出这些画作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The museum displays its rarest coins in a locked glass case.  博物馆把最珍稀的钱币陈列在上锁的玻璃柜里。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 divulge</td><td>/daɪˈvʌldʒ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>泄漏（秘密）</td><td>divulge a secret<br>泄露秘密</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 No employee may divulge a secret learned during the merger negotiations.  任何员工都不得泄露在并购谈判中得知的秘密。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 expose</td><td>/ɪkˈspoʊz/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>揭露；使暴露</td><td>expose the fraud<br>揭露欺诈行为</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Two journalists exposed the fraud after months of quiet investigation.  两名记者经过数月暗中调查揭露了这起欺诈。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 reveal</td><td>/rɪˈviːl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>揭示，透露</td><td>reveal the details<br>透露细节</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company refused to reveal the details of the settlement.  公司拒绝透露和解的细节。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 show</td><td>/ˈʃoʊ/</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>显示，展现；展览</td><td>show your feelings<br>表露感情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He rarely shows his feelings, even to close friends.  他很少表露感情，连对好友也如此。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 uncloak</del></td><td>/ʌnˈkloʊk/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>揭露，揭开真相</td><td>uncloak a conspiracy<br>揭露阴谋</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The journalist worked for months to uncloak the corruption scheme.  这位记者花了数月揭露这个腐败阴谋。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 uncover</td><td>/ənˈkʌvər/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>揭开，发现，揭露</td><td>uncover the evidence<br>发现证据</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Inspectors uncovered evidence of fraud in the accounting records.  检查人员在账目中发现了欺诈的证据。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unmask</td><td>/ʌnˈmæsk/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>揭露，揭穿真面目</td><td>unmask the impostor<br>揭穿那个冒充者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The journalist unmasked the company's secret polluting practices.  记者揭露了这家公司秘密排污的做法。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unveil</td><td>/ənˈveɪl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>揭幕，公布，揭示</td><td>unveil a new model<br>发布新款</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company will unveil a new model at the spring show.  公司将在春季展会上发布新款。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 7

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>convention</b></td><td>/kənˈvenʃn/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>常规，习俗；大会，集会</td><td>an annual convention<br>年度大会</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Thousands of teachers attended the annual convention held in the capital.  数千名教师参加了在首都举行的年度大会。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 custom</td><td>/ˈkʌstəm/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>习俗，惯例</td><td>a local custom<br>当地习俗</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Removing your shoes indoors is an old custom in that country.  在那个国家，进屋脱鞋是古老的习俗。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ritual</td><td>/ˈrɪtʃuəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>仪式，例行程序</td><td>a morning ritual<br>晨间例行仪式</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Brewing coffee slowly is his cherished morning ritual.  慢慢煮咖啡是他珍视的晨间仪式。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 manner</td><td>/ˈmænər/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>方式；举止，礼貌</td><td>in a polite manner<br>以礼貌的方式</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She answered every question in a calm and polite manner.  她以平静而礼貌的方式回答了每个问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 assembly</td><td>/əˈsembli/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>集会，议会；装配</td><td>a general assembly<br>全体大会</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The whole school gathered in the hall for the morning assembly.  全校师生在礼堂集合参加早会。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 congress</td><td>/ˈkɑːŋɡrəs/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>代表大会；国会</td><td>an international congress<br>国际大会</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Scientists from forty countries attended the international congress in Vienna.  来自四十国的科学家参加了维也纳的国际大会。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 council</td><td>/ˈkaʊnsəl/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>委员会，理事会</td><td>the city council<br>市议会</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The city council voted to build a new park near the river.  市议会投票决定在河边建一座新公园。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 gathering</td><td>/ˈɡæðərɪŋ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>聚会，集会</td><td>a family gathering<br>家庭聚会</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Every autumn they hold a small family gathering in the orchard.  每年秋天他们在果园里举行小型家庭聚会。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 deviation</td><td>/ˌdiːviˈeɪʃən/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>偏离，背离</td><td>a deviation from the norm<br>对常规的偏离</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Any deviation from the standard procedure must be reported immediately.  任何对标准流程的偏离都必须立即上报。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>converge</b></td><td>/kənˈvɜːrdʒ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>汇集，交汇于一点</td><td>converge on the square<br>汇集到广场</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 All the main roads converge on the square in the center.  所有主干道都在中心广场交汇。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 meet</td><td>/miːt/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>合适的</td><td>a meet response<br>合适的回应</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A public apology seemed the only meet response to such a serious blunder.  公开道歉似乎是对如此严重失误唯一合适的回应。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 focus</td><td>/ˈfoʊkəs/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>聚集，集中；焦点</td><td>focus attention on<br>把注意力集中于</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The lens focuses sunlight into a single bright point.  透镜把阳光聚集成一个明亮的点。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 concentrate</td><td>/ˈkɑːnsntreɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>浓缩；聚集</td><td>concentrate the solution<br>浓缩溶液</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Boil the syrup slowly in order to concentrate the solution.  慢慢熬煮糖浆以便浓缩溶液。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 diverge</td><td>/daɪˈvɜːrdʒ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>（使）分叉，散开；分歧</td><td>diverge from the path<br>偏离原有路径</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two trails diverge just beyond the old wooden bridge.  两条小径在那座老木桥之后就分岔开来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 deviate</td><td>/ˈdiːviˌeɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>偏离，背离</td><td>deviate from the plan<br>偏离计划</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The pilot had to deviate from the planned route because of storms.  因风暴，飞行员不得不偏离既定航线。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 digress</td><td>/daɪˈɡres/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>脱离主题</td><td>digress from the topic<br>偏离主题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The professor tended to digress from the topic whenever a student asked about history.  一有学生问到历史，教授就爱偏离主题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>conversant</b></td><td>/kənˈvɜːrsnt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>熟悉的，通晓的</td><td>conversant with the law<br>通晓法律</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She is fully conversant with the new safety regulations.  她非常熟悉新的安全条例。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 acquaint</td><td>/əˈkweɪnt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使熟悉，使了解</td><td>acquaint yourself with the rules<br>熟悉这些规则</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 New employees must acquaint themselves with the safety procedures first.  新员工必须先熟悉安全流程。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 aware</td><td>/əˈwer/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>知道的，意识到的</td><td>be aware of the danger<br>意识到危险</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She was fully aware that the deadline had already passed.  她完全知道截止日期已经过了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 familiar</td><td>/fəˈmɪljər/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>熟悉的，通晓的</td><td>familiar with the subject<br>熟悉这门学科</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her voice sounded strangely familiar over the phone.  她的声音在电话里听起来奇怪地熟悉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 informed</td><td>/ɪnˈfɔːrmd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>见识广的，掌握信息的</td><td>make an informed decision<br>做出明智的决定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Voters need accurate data to make an informed choice.  选民需要准确的数据才能做出明智的选择。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 versed</td><td>/vɜːrst/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>熟知的；精通的</td><td>well versed in history<br>精通历史</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He is well versed in both classical and modern poetry.  他对古典诗歌和现代诗歌都很精通。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ignorant</td><td>/ˈɪɡnərənt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无知的，不知道的</td><td>ignorant of the facts<br>不了解事实</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They were completely ignorant of the danger ahead.  他们对前方的危险毫不知情。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unfamiliar</td><td>/ˌʌnfəˈmɪljər/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不熟悉的，陌生的</td><td>an unfamiliar face<br>一张陌生的脸</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The street names here were all unfamiliar to me.  这里的街名对我来说都很陌生。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>convert</b></td><td>/kənˈvɜːrt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>改变，转化</td><td>convert heat into energy<br>把热转化为能量</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Engineers converted the abandoned warehouse into a bright public library.  工程师们把废弃的仓库改造成了明亮的公共图书馆。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 alter</td><td>/ˈɔːltər/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>改变，修改</td><td>alter the design<br>修改设计</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The architect had to alter the design after the budget was cut.  预算削减后建筑师不得不修改设计。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 change</td><td>/ˈtʃeɪndʒ/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>改变，变化；零钱</td><td>change your mind<br>改变主意</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Nothing anyone said could change his mind about leaving.  谁说什么都无法改变他离开的主意。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 transform</td><td>/trænsˈfɔːrm/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>彻底改变，转变</td><td>transform the neighborhood<br>彻底改变街区</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new subway line transformed the quiet neighborhood within five years.  新地铁线五年内彻底改变了这个安静的街区。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 transfigure</td><td>/trænsˈfɪɡjər/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使改变外观</td><td>transfigure her face<br>使她面容改观</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The rising sunlight transfigured the plain valley into a field of gold.  初升的阳光把平凡的山谷变成一片金色的原野。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>consort</b></td><td>/kənˈsɔːrt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>结交</td><td>consort with criminals<br>与罪犯结交</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 No respectable official would consort with smugglers so openly in public.  没有正派的官员会如此公开地与走私者结交。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 company</td><td>/ˈkʌmpəni/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>陪伴，同伴；公司</td><td>keep bad company<br>结交坏朋友</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His parents worried that he was keeping bad company at school.  他父母担心他在学校结交坏朋友。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 consociate</del></td><td>/kənˈsoʊʃieɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>结交，联合</td><td>consociate with rivals<br>与对手联合</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The small firms consociated to bid for the huge contract.  这些小公司联合起来竞标这份大合同。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 associate</td><td>/əˈsoʊʃieɪt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>联合，结交；联想</td><td>associate with the group<br>与该团体结交</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He refused to associate himself with such a risky plan.  他拒绝与这样冒险的计划有牵连。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hook up</td><td>/hʊk ˈʌp/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>结识交往；连接</td><td>hook up with friends<br>跟朋友碰面结伴</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We hooked up with two other travelers on the mountain trail.  我们在山道上与另外两名旅行者结伴而行。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>cagey</b></td><td>/ˈkeɪdʒi/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不乐意说话的</td><td>cagey about the details<br>对细节含糊其辞</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The manager was cagey about the reasons for her sudden resignation.  经理对她突然辞职的原因含糊其辞。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hesitant</td><td>/ˈhezɪtənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>犹豫的，迟疑的</td><td>hesitant about the offer<br>对这个提议犹豫</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was hesitant about moving to a new city alone.  他对独自搬到新城市感到犹豫。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 disinclined</td><td>/ˌdɪsɪnˈklaɪnd/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不愿的，不情愿的</td><td>disinclined to help<br>不愿帮忙</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After the argument she was disinclined to help him again.  争吵之后她不愿再帮他。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 indisposed</td><td>/ˌɪndɪˈspoʊzd/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不愿的；身体不适的</td><td>indisposed to talk<br>不愿交谈</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The witness seemed indisposed to talk about that evening.  证人似乎不愿谈那晚的事。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 reluctant</td><td>/rɪˈlʌktənt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不情愿的，勉强的</td><td>reluctant to answer<br>不愿回答</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The witness seemed reluctant to answer any question about that evening.  证人似乎不愿回答关于那天晚上的任何问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 reticent</td><td>/ˈretɪsnt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>沉默不语的</td><td>reticent about his past<br>对往事讳莫如深</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was oddly reticent about the years he spent working abroad.  他对自己在国外工作的那些年出奇地沉默不语。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 disposed</td><td>/dɪˈspoʊzd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>愿意的，有意向的</td><td>disposed to agree<br>倾向于同意</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The judge seemed disposed to grant a shorter sentence.  法官似乎倾向于判处较短刑期。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 inclined</td><td>/ˌɪnˈklaɪnd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>倾向于的，愿意的</td><td>inclined to believe<br>倾向于相信</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 I am inclined to believe his version of the story.  我倾向于相信他讲的那个版本。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>conviction</b></td><td>/kənˈvɪkʃn/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>深信，确信；证明有罪</td><td>speak with conviction<br>满怀信念地说</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She argued her case with a conviction that silenced every critic.  她以坚定的信念陈述自己的主张，令所有批评者沉默。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 assurance</td><td>/əˈʃʊrəns/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>保证；自信，确信</td><td>give an assurance<br>作出保证</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She answered the difficult questions with surprising assurance.  她以惊人的自信回答了那些难题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 certainty</td><td>/ˈsɜːrtənti/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>确定，必然；确信</td><td>with absolute certainty<br>绝对确定地</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 No one can predict the outcome with absolute certainty.  没人能绝对确定地预测结果。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 certitude</td><td>/ˈsɜːrtɪtuːd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>确信无疑</td><td>moral certitude<br>道德上的确信</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He spoke of the plan's success with unshakable certitude.  他以不可动摇的确信谈起这项计划的成功。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sentence</td><td>/ˈsentəns/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>判决，宣判；句子</td><td>a life sentence<br>无期徒刑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The court gave him a five-year sentence for the robbery.  法院因抢劫判他五年刑期。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 dubiety</del></td><td>/duˈbaɪəti/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>怀疑，疑惑</td><td>a note of dubiety<br>一丝怀疑的语气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A trace of dubiety showed in the professor's careful reply.  教授谨慎的回答中透出一丝怀疑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 incredulity</td><td>/ˌɪnkrəˈduːlɪti/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>不相信，怀疑</td><td>stare in incredulity<br>难以置信地盯着</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She listened to his excuse with obvious incredulity.  她带着明显的怀疑听他的借口。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 acquittal</td><td>/əˈkwɪtəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>无罪释放，宣判无罪</td><td>win an acquittal<br>获判无罪</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The defense lawyer won an acquittal after a three-week trial.  经过三周审判，辩护律师赢得了无罪判决。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>convoke</b></td><td>/kənˈvoʊk/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>召集开会</td><td>convoke a special session<br>召集特别会议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The president will convoke an emergency session of the council.  总统将召集理事会举行紧急会议。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 assemble</td><td>/əˈsembəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>聚集，集合；组装</td><td>assemble a crowd<br>聚集起人群</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Villagers assembled in the square to hear the announcement.  村民聚集在广场上听那则公告。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 convene</td><td>/kənˈviːn/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>召开，召集</td><td>convene a meeting<br>召开会议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The board convened an emergency session to discuss the sudden resignation.  董事会召开紧急会议讨论这次突然辞职。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 summon</td><td>/ˈsʌmən/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>召集，召唤</td><td>summon the committee<br>召集委员会</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The general summoned all his officers before launching the attack.  将军在发动进攻前召集了所有的军官。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 muster</td><td>/ˈmʌstər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>集合，召集；一群人</td><td>muster the troops<br>召集部队</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The captain mustered his crew on the upper deck.  船长把船员集合到上层甲板。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 adjourn</td><td>/əˈdʒɜːrn/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>延期，休会</td><td>adjourn the meeting<br>宣布会议休会</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The judge decided to adjourn the trial until Monday morning.  法官决定将审判延至周一上午。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>convoluted</b></td><td>/ˈkɑːnvəluːtɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>复杂的，费解的</td><td>a convoluted argument<br>费解的论证</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The instructions were so convoluted that nobody could assemble the shelf.  说明书太过费解，没人能装好那个架子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 complex</td><td>/ˈkɑːmpleks/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>复杂的；综合体</td><td>a complex argument<br>复杂的论证</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The paper presents a complex argument about ancient trade routes.  这篇论文提出了关于古代商路的复杂论证。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 knotty</td><td>/ˈnɑːti/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>复杂的，困难的</td><td>a knotty question<br>棘手的问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The professor posed a knotty question that nobody could answer confidently.  教授提出了一个没人能自信回答的棘手问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 involved</td><td>/ɪnˈvɑːlvd/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>错综复杂的，难懂的</td><td>an involved argument<br>错综复杂的论证</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The plot is so involved that few readers finish the book.  情节太过错综复杂，很少有读者读完这本书。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sophisticated</td><td>/səˈfɪstɪkeɪtɪd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>复杂精密的；老练的</td><td>a sophisticated system<br>复杂精密的系统</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The lab uses highly sophisticated equipment to detect the particles.  实验室用极为精密的设备探测这些粒子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 twisted</td><td>/ˈtwɪstɪd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>扭曲的；反常的</td><td>a twisted plot<br>曲折离奇的情节</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The novel's twisted plot keeps readers guessing until the last page.  这部小说曲折的情节让读者猜到最后一页。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tangled</td><td>/ˈtæŋɡəld/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>纠缠的，错综复杂的</td><td>a tangled web<br>错综复杂的关系网</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The case involves a tangled web of loans and false names.  此案涉及贷款与假名交织的复杂网络。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 labyrinthine</td><td>/ˌlæbəˈrɪnθaɪn/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>迷宫似的，复杂曲折的</td><td>labyrinthine corridors<br>迷宫般的走廊</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We got lost in the labyrinthine corridors of the old hospital building.  我们在这座老医院大楼迷宫般的走廊里迷了路。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 straightforward</td><td>/ˌstreɪtˈfɔːrwərd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>简单明了的；坦率的</td><td>a straightforward procedure<br>简单直接的流程</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The instructions are straightforward enough for a child to follow.  说明简单明了，连孩子都能照着做。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>convulsion</b></td><td>/kənˈvʌlʃn/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>骚乱</td><td>political convulsions<br>政治动荡</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The revolution threw the entire country into political convulsions for a decade.  这场革命使整个国家陷入长达十年的政治动荡。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 commotion</td><td>/kəˈmoʊʃn/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>骚乱</td><td>cause a commotion<br>引起一阵骚动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The escaped goat caused quite a commotion in the market.  逃跑的山羊在市场上引起一阵不小的骚动。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ferment</td><td>/ˈfɜːrment/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>动乱，骚动；发酵</td><td>in a state of ferment<br>处于动荡之中</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The whole country was in a state of ferment before the election.  大选前全国处于动荡之中。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tumult</td><td>/ˈtuːmʌlt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>骚动，暴动</td><td>the tumult of the crowd<br>人群的骚动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The tumult in the square grew louder as more protesters arrived.  随着更多抗议者到来，广场上的骚动越来越大。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 upheaval</td><td>/əpˈhiːvəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>剧变，动乱</td><td>social upheaval<br>社会剧变</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The war brought decades of social upheaval to the region.  战争给这一地区带来数十年的社会剧变。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 clamor</td><td>/ˈklæmər/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>喧闹，喧哗，噪声</td><td>a clamor of voices<br>一片人声喧哗</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A clamor of voices rose from the crowded courtyard.  拥挤的院子里传出一片人声喧哗。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tempest</td><td>/ˈtempəst/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>风暴；骚动，风波</td><td>a tempest of protest<br>抗议的风暴</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His careless remark raised a tempest of protest online.  他随意的一句话在网上掀起抗议风暴。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 uproar</td><td>/ˈʌˌprɔːr/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>喧嚣，骚动</td><td>cause an uproar<br>引起轩然大波</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new tax plan caused an uproar among small business owners.  新税收方案在小企业主中引起轩然大波。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 serenity</td><td>/səˈrenəti/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>宁静，安详</td><td>a sense of serenity<br>一种宁静感</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The garden offers a rare serenity in the middle of the city.  这座花园在城市中心提供了难得的宁静。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 tranquility</td><td>/træŋˈkwɪləti/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>宁静，淡定</td><td>an air of tranquility<br>宁静的气息</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He enjoyed the tranquility of the snowy fields at dusk.  他享受黄昏时白雪覆盖的田野的静谧。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 8

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>coop</b></td><td>/kuːp/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>监禁，困于…之中</td><td>coop up the chickens<br>把鸡关进笼里</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The heavy rain cooped us up indoors for the whole weekend.  大雨把我们整个周末都困在室内。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 box</td><td>/ˈbɑːks/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>困住，围住；盒子</td><td>box someone in<br>把某人困住</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Two trucks boxed our car in at the narrow intersection.  两辆卡车在狭窄路口把我们的车困住了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cage</td><td>/ˈkeɪdʒ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>关进笼中；笼子</td><td>cage a wild bird<br>把野鸟关进笼子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It seems cruel to cage such a strong wild bird.  把这样一只强健的野鸟关进笼子似乎很残忍。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 corral</td><td>/kəˈræl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>关入围栏；畜栏</td><td>corral the horses<br>把马圈进围栏</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The cowboys corralled the horses before the storm arrived.  牛仔们在暴风雨到来前把马圈进围栏。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 encage</del></td><td>/ɛnˈkeɪdʒ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>关入笼中，禁闭</td><td>encage a wild animal<br>把野兽关入笼中</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The zoo no longer encages its larger animals in narrow pens.  动物园不再把大型动物关入狭窄的笼舍。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 enclose</td><td>/ɪnˈkloʊz/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>围住，圈起；附上</td><td>enclose the garden<br>把花园围起来</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A stone wall encloses the garden on three sides.  一道石墙把花园三面围住。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 envelop</td><td>/ɪnˈveləp/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>包围，笼罩</td><td>envelop the valley in fog<br>使山谷笼罩在雾中</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Thick fog enveloped the valley before dawn.  黎明前浓雾笼罩了山谷。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fence</td><td>/ˈfens/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>栅栏，篱笆；围起</td><td>a wooden fence<br>木栅栏</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They painted the wooden fence around the garden last summer.  去年夏天他们给花园周围的木栅栏刷了漆。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hedge</td><td>/hedʒ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>树篱，篱笆；用篱笆围住</td><td>a neatly trimmed hedge<br>修剪整齐的树篱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A neatly trimmed hedge separated the garden from the road.  一道修剪整齐的树篱把花园与马路隔开。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 immure</del></td><td>/ɪˈmjʊr/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>监禁，禁闭；使闭门不出</td><td>immure himself in study<br>闭门苦读</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The scholar immured himself in the archives for the whole winter.  这位学者整个冬天都闭门待在档案馆里。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 include</td><td>/ɪnˈkluːd/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>包含，包括</td><td>include all the costs<br>包含全部费用</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The price includes all the costs of shipping and insurance.  价格包含全部运费和保险费用。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pen</td><td>/ˈpen/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>把…关进圈栏；笔</td><td>pen up the sheep<br>把羊圈起来</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The farmer penned up the sheep for the cold night.  农民为寒夜把羊圈了起来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 wall</td><td>/ˈwɔːl/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>墙，壁；用墙围住</td><td>a brick wall<br>砖墙</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Photographs of his children covered the wall behind the desk.  书桌后的墙上贴满了他孩子的照片。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 free</td><td>/ˈfriː/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、形容词</td><td>释放，解放；自由的</td><td>free the hostages<br>释放人质</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Negotiators finally persuaded the gunmen to free the hostages.  谈判者最终说服枪手释放了人质。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 liberate</td><td>/ˈlɪbəreɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>解放，释放</td><td>liberate the city<br>解放这座城市</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Allied troops liberated the city in the spring of that year.  盟军在那年春天解放了这座城市。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 release</td><td>/riˈliːs/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>释放，解除束缚；发布</td><td>release the prisoners<br>释放囚犯</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The court ordered officials to release him from custody at once.  法庭命令官员立即将他从拘留中释放。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>copious</b></td><td>/ˈkoʊpiəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>大量的，丰富的</td><td>take copious notes<br>做大量笔记</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She took copious notes during every lecture and reread them each night.  她每堂课都记大量笔记，并在每晚重读。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 plentiful</td><td>/ˈplentəfəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>丰富的，大量的</td><td>a plentiful harvest<br>丰收</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Rain came early, so the harvest was unusually plentiful.  雨来得早，所以收成异常丰富。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 abundant</td><td>/əˈbʌndənt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>大量的，丰富的</td><td>abundant evidence<br>大量的证据</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Water was abundant after the spring rains.  春雨过后水源充足。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ample</td><td>/ˈæmpəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>充足的，富裕的</td><td>ample opportunity<br>充足的机会</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 There is ample room for everyone in the van.  车里有足够的空间容纳所有人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 gushing</td><td>/ˈɡʌʃɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>喷涌的；过分热情的</td><td>a gushing spring<br>喷涌的泉水</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A gushing spring supplies the whole village with water.  一股喷涌的泉水供给全村用水。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 sparse</td><td>/spɑːrs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>稀疏的，稀少的</td><td>sparse vegetation<br>稀疏的植被</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Vegetation grows sparse as the trail climbs above the tree line.  小径爬上林线以上后，植被变得稀疏。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 dearth</td><td>/dɜːrθ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>供应不足</td><td>a dearth of talent<br>人才匮乏</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 There is a real dearth of affordable housing in this district.  这个区确实缺乏买得起的住房。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 scant</td><td>/skænt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不足的，缺乏的</td><td>scant attention<br>很少的关注</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The report paid scant attention to the concerns of local residents.  报告对当地居民的关切几乎没有给予关注。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>coquette</b></td><td>/koʊˈket/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>调情；卖弄风情的女子</td><td>coquette with danger<br>拿危险开玩笑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The young poet coquetted with several admirers but loved none of them.  那位年轻诗人与好几位追慕者调情，却谁也不爱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 trifle</td><td>/ˈtraɪfəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>轻视，玩弄；小事</td><td>trifle with her feelings<br>玩弄她的感情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Do not trifle with her feelings just to amuse yourself.  不要为了取乐而玩弄她的感情。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dally</td><td>/ˈdæli/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>虚度时光，磨蹭</td><td>dally over lunch<br>吃午饭磨蹭半天</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Don't dally on the way home; dinner is almost ready.  回家路上别磨蹭，晚饭快好了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 flirt</td><td>/flɜːrt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>调情</td><td>flirt with the waiter<br>跟服务员调情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The waitress cheerfully flirted with every single man in the restaurant.  女服务员愉快地和餐厅里每个单身男子调情。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mess around</td><td>/mɛs əˈraʊnd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>闲混，不认真对待</td><td>mess around all day<br>整天闲混</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Stop messing around and finish your report before midnight.  别再闲混了，午夜前把报告写完。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del><b>countervail</b></del></td><td>/ˌkaʊntərˈveɪl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>抗衡，抵消</td><td>countervail the loss<br>抵消损失</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Higher tariffs were imposed to countervail the subsidies given to foreign producers.  提高关税以抵消给予外国生产者的补贴。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 compensate</td><td>/ˈkɑːmpənˌseɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>补偿，弥补；赔偿</td><td>compensate for the loss<br>弥补损失</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Extra practice can compensate for a lack of natural talent.  额外的练习能弥补天赋的不足。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 counteract</td><td>/ˌkaʊntərˈækt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>抵抗，抵消，中和</td><td>counteract the poison<br>中和毒素</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Exercise can counteract some effects of a poor diet.  运动可以抵消不良饮食的部分影响。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 counterbalance</td><td>/ˈkaʊntərˌbæləns/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>抵消，使平衡</td><td>counterbalance the risk<br>抵消风险</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Higher wages counterbalance the long hours of this job.  较高的工资抵消了这份工作的长工时。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 counterpoise</del></td><td>/ˈkaʊntərˌpɔɪz/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>使平衡，抗衡；平衡力</td><td>counterpoise two forces<br>使两种力量抗衡</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The senate was designed to counterpoise the power of the president.  参议院的设计意在抗衡总统的权力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 neutralize</td><td>/ˈnuːtrəlaɪz/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>中和；抵消，使无效</td><td>neutralize the acid<br>中和酸性</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A spoonful of baking soda will neutralize the acid spilled on the bench.  一勺小苏打就能中和溅在台面上的酸。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 offset</td><td>/ɔfˈset/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>抵消，弥补；补偿物</td><td>offset the cost<br>抵消成本</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 New tax credits should offset the cost of the equipment.  新的税收抵扣应能抵消设备成本。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>cornucopia</b></td><td>/ˌkɔːrnjuˈkoʊpiə/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>大量</td><td>a cornucopia of choices<br>大量的选择</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The market offered a cornucopia of fresh fruit at surprisingly low prices.  市场上有大量新鲜水果，价格低得惊人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 plentitude</del></td><td>/ˈplɛnɪtud/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>充足，大量</td><td>a plentitude of talent<br>人才济济</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The region enjoys a plentitude of water and fertile soil.  该地区拥有充足的水源和肥沃的土壤。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 plethora</td><td>/ˈpleθərə/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>过量，过剩</td><td>a plethora of options<br>过多的选项</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The menu offers a plethora of options that confuse first time visitors.  菜单提供过多的选项，让初次光顾的客人不知所选。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 wealth</td><td>/ˈwelθ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>大量，丰富；财富</td><td>a wealth of experience<br>丰富的经验</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old editor brought a wealth of experience to the team.  这位老编辑给团队带来了丰富的经验。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 profusion</td><td>/prəˈfjuːʒn/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>丰富，大量</td><td>a profusion of flowers<br>繁花盛开</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A profusion of wildflowers covered the hillside after the spring rains.  春雨过后，山坡上开满了大量野花。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 affluence</td><td>/ˈæfluəns/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>富裕，丰富</td><td>live in affluence<br>生活富裕</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The family had lived in quiet affluence for three generations.  这家人已在平静的富裕中生活了三代。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 lack</td><td>/ˈlæk/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>缺乏，不足</td><td>a lack of funds<br>资金不足</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The project stalled because of a serious lack of funds.  项目因严重缺乏资金而停滞。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 pittance</td><td>/ˈpɪtns/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>少量津贴</td><td>work for a pittance<br>为微薄报酬工作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The apprentices worked long hours for a pittance and no benefits.  学徒们长时间工作，只拿微薄津贴且没有福利。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 deficiency</td><td>/dɪˈfɪʃnsi/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>缺乏，不足</td><td>a vitamin deficiency<br>维生素缺乏</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A serious iron deficiency left her tired and short of breath.  严重缺铁使她疲倦、气短。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>coronation</b></td><td>/ˌkɔːrəˈneɪʃn/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>加冕，加冕礼</td><td>attend the coronation ceremony<br>出席加冕典礼</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Crowds lined the streets to watch the young queen's coronation procession.  人群沿街站立，观看年轻女王的加冕游行。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 enthronement</td><td>/ɛnˈθroʊnmənt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>登基，即位</td><td>the king's enthronement<br>国王的登基</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Crowds filled the capital for the young king's enthronement.  人群涌入首都参加年轻国王的登基。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 crowning</td><td>/ˈkraʊnɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>加冕；最高的，最终的</td><td>the crowning of the queen<br>女王的加冕</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 That discovery was the crowning achievement of her long career.  那项发现是她漫长职业生涯的最高成就。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 abdication</td><td>/ˌæbdɪˈkeɪʃən/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>退位，逊位；放弃</td><td>the emperor's abdication<br>皇帝的退位</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The emperor's abdication shocked the whole court.  皇帝的退位震惊了整个朝廷。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>corporeal</b></td><td>/kɔːrˈpɔːriəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>肉体的；有形的，实体的</td><td>corporeal existence<br>肉体的存在</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The ghost seemed almost corporeal in the flickering candlelight.  在摇曳的烛光中，那鬼影几乎像有实体。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bodily</td><td>/ˈbɑːdəli/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词、副词</td><td>身体的，肉体的</td><td>bodily harm<br>身体伤害</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The law protects citizens from any threat of bodily harm.  法律保护公民免受任何身体伤害的威胁。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 carnal</td><td>/ˈkɑːrnl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>肉体的，物质的；世俗的</td><td>carnal pleasures<br>肉体的享乐</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The monk warned his students against carnal pleasures and idle talk.  僧人告诫学生远离肉体享乐和闲谈。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 corporal</td><td>/ˈkɔːrpərəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>肉体的，身体的；下士</td><td>corporal punishment<br>体罚</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Most countries have banned corporal punishment in their schools.  多数国家已禁止在学校施行体罚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 somatic</td><td>/səˈmætɪk/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>肉体的</td><td>somatic symptoms<br>身体上的症状</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The doctor attributed her somatic symptoms to prolonged emotional stress.  医生把她身体上的症状归因于长期的情绪压力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 physical</td><td>/ˈfɪzɪkəl/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>身体的；物质的</td><td>physical evidence<br>实物证据</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Police found no physical evidence linking him to the crime.  警方没有找到把他与罪案联系起来的实物证据。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fleshly</td><td>/ˈflɛʃli/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>肉体的；世俗的</td><td>fleshly desires<br>肉体的欲望</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The monk warned his students against fleshly temptations.  那位僧人告诫学生要远离肉体的诱惑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 material</td><td>/məˈtɪriəl/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>物质的；重要的；材料</td><td>material comforts<br>物质上的享受</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He cared little for material wealth and lived very simply.  他不太在意物质财富，生活极为简朴。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 substantial</td><td>/səbˈstænʃl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>大量的，可观的；实质的</td><td>a substantial increase<br>大幅的增长</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company reported a substantial increase in profits this quarter.  公司报告本季度利润大幅增长。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sensible</td><td>/ˈsensəbəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>明智的，合理的</td><td>a sensible choice<br>明智的选择</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Wearing boots was a sensible choice in that mud.  在那种泥地里穿靴子是明智的选择。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tangible</td><td>/ˈtændʒəbl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>可感知的</td><td>tangible benefits<br>实实在在的好处</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Voters want tangible benefits, not vague promises about the future.  选民想要实实在在的好处，而不是对未来的空泛承诺。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 spiritual</td><td>/ˈspɪrɪtʃuəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>精神的；灵魂的，宗教的</td><td>spiritual growth<br>精神成长</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She turned to meditation in search of spiritual peace.  她转向冥想，以寻求精神上的平静。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 intangible</td><td>/ɪnˈtændʒəbl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无法感知的，无形的</td><td>intangible assets<br>无形资产</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A company's reputation is an intangible asset that no balance sheet captures.  公司声誉是一项资产负债表无法体现的无形资产。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 disembodied</td><td>/ˌdɪsɪmˈbɑːdid/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无实体的，脱离肉体的</td><td>a disembodied voice<br>不见其人的声音</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A disembodied voice announced the delay over the loudspeaker.  扬声器里传来一个不见其人的声音宣布延误。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 immaterial</td><td>/ˌɪməˈtɪriəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无关的，不重要的</td><td>immaterial to the case<br>与本案无关</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Whether he liked the plan is immaterial to the legal question here.  他是否喜欢这个计划与这里的法律问题无关。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>corroborate</b></td><td>/kəˈrɑːbəreɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>用证据或权威证实；为…提供证据，支持</td><td>corroborate the witness's story<br>证实证人的说法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Two neighbors corroborated the driver's account of the accident that night.  两位邻居证实了司机对那晚事故的说法。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 confirm</td><td>/kənˈfɜːrm/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>证实，确认，进一步确定</td><td>confirm the findings<br>证实这些发现</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Later tests confirmed what the first team had suspected.  后来的检测证实了第一个团队的猜测。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 authenticate</td><td>/ɔˈθentəˌkeɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>证实，鉴定为真</td><td>authenticate the painting<br>鉴定画作真伪</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Experts were called in to authenticate the painting's signature.  专家被请来鉴定这幅画签名的真伪。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 justify</td><td>/ˈdʒʌstɪfaɪ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>证明…的合理性，为…辩解</td><td>justify his decision<br>证明他的决定合理</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Nothing in the report can justify his decision to conceal the results.  报告里没有任何内容能证明他隐瞒结果的决定是合理的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 substantiate</td><td>/səbˈstænʃieɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>证实</td><td>substantiate the claim<br>证实这一说法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 No evidence has emerged to substantiate his serious accusations.  没有证据能证实他严重的指控。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 validate</td><td>/ˈvælədeɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>证实，确认；使有效</td><td>validate the results<br>验证这些结果</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Independent labs validated the results within three months.  独立实验室在三个月内验证了这些结果。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 verify</td><td>/ˈverɪfaɪ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>证实，核实</td><td>verify the data<br>核实这些数据</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Two independent labs will verify the results before publication.  两家独立实验室将在发表前核实这些结果。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 back</td><td>/ˈbæk/</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>支持，资助</td><td>back a candidate<br>支持某位候选人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Several investors agreed to back her new company.  几位投资人同意资助她的新公司。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bolster</td><td>/ˈboʊlstər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>支持，增强</td><td>bolster his confidence<br>增强他的信心</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A word of praise from her would bolster his confidence enormously.  她一句赞扬就会极大地增强他的信心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 buttress</td><td>/ˈbʌtrɪs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>支持，加强（论点）</td><td>buttress an argument<br>为论点提供支撑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Fresh evidence buttressed the prosecutor's case against the two suspects.  新证据支持了检察官针对两名嫌疑人的指控。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 reinforce</td><td>/ˌriːɪnˈfɔːrs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>加强，强化</td><td>reinforce the message<br>强化这一信息</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Repeated praise reinforced the child's interest in reading.  反复的表扬强化了孩子对阅读的兴趣。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 support</td><td>/səˈpɔːrt/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>支持，支撑</td><td>support a proposal<br>支持一项提议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Most members voted to support the new proposal.  大多数成员投票支持这项新提案。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 controvert</del></td><td>/ˈkɑntrəvɜrt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>反驳，否证</td><td>controvert the claim<br>反驳该说法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 New fossil data controvert the long-accepted theory of migration.  新的化石数据反驳了长期公认的迁徙理论。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 contradict</td><td>/ˌkɑːntrəˈdɪkt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>否认，反驳；与…相矛盾</td><td>contradict the evidence<br>与证据相矛盾</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The witness's second statement flatly contradicted everything she had said earlier.  证人的第二份陈述与她此前所说完全矛盾。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 deny</td><td>/dɪˈnaɪ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>否认；拒绝给予</td><td>flatly deny the charge<br>断然否认指控</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 I do not deny that the plan carries some very real risks.  我不否认这个计划确实带有一些实实在在的风险。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>corrosive</b></td><td>/kəˈroʊsɪv/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>腐蚀性的；讽刺性的</td><td>a corrosive chemical<br>腐蚀性化学品</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Salt water is highly corrosive and slowly eats through the ship's hull.  盐水腐蚀性很强，会慢慢蚀穿船体。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 erosive</td><td>/ɪˈroʊsɪv/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>侵蚀性的，腐蚀性的</td><td>the erosive action of water<br>水的侵蚀作用</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The erosive action of the river carved a deep canyon.  河水的侵蚀作用切出一道深峡谷。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 caustic</td><td>/ˈkɔːstɪk/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>挖苦讽刺的，刻薄的</td><td>a caustic remark<br>刻薄的评论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His caustic remarks about the film upset the young director.  他对影片刻薄的评论让年轻导演很难过。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sarcastic</td><td>/sɑːrˈkæstɪk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>讽刺的，尖刻的</td><td>a sarcastic remark<br>讽刺的话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His sarcastic tone made it clear he disagreed with the plan.  他讽刺的语气清楚表明他不同意这个计划。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 barbed</td><td>/ˈbɑːrbd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>尖刻的；有倒刺的</td><td>a barbed comment<br>带刺的评论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her barbed compliment made the whole table fall silent.  她那句带刺的赞美让全桌人都沉默了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 acerbic</td><td>/əˈsɜːrbɪk/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>（语调）尖酸的，刻薄的</td><td>acerbic wit<br>尖刻的机智</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The reviewer is famous for her acerbic comments on new plays.  这位评论家以对新剧的尖酸评论闻名。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 satiric</td><td>/səˈtɪrɪk/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>讽刺的，讥讽的</td><td>a satiric novel<br>一部讽刺小说</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The magazine published a satiric cartoon about the election.  这家杂志刊登了一幅关于选举的讽刺漫画。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 acrid</td><td>/ˈækrɪd/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>刻薄的；辛辣刺鼻的</td><td>acrid smoke<br>刺鼻的烟</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 An acrid smell of burning plastic filled the hallway.  一股塑料烧焦的刺鼻气味充满了走廊。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mordant</td><td>/ˈmɔːrdnt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>尖酸刻薄的</td><td>mordant wit<br>尖刻的机智</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His mordant wit amused readers but cost him several close friendships.  他尖刻的机智取悦了读者，却让他失去几段亲密友谊。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tart</td><td>/ˈtɑːrt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>尖刻的；酸的</td><td>a tart reply<br>尖刻的回答</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her tart reply silenced everyone at the dinner table.  她尖刻的回答让餐桌上所有人都沉默了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 noncorrosive</del></td><td>/ˌnɑnkəˈroʊsɪv/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无腐蚀性的</td><td>a noncorrosive cleaner<br>无腐蚀性的清洁剂</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Use a noncorrosive cleaner on the polished metal surface.  在抛光金属表面上使用无腐蚀性的清洁剂。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>corrugated</b></td><td>/ˈkɔːrəɡeɪtɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>起波纹的，起皱的</td><td>corrugated cardboard<br>瓦楞纸板</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The roof was made of cheap corrugated iron sheets.  屋顶是用便宜的瓦楞铁皮做的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 wrinkled</td><td>/ˈrɪŋkəld/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有皱纹的，起皱的</td><td>a wrinkled shirt<br>起皱的衬衫</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He arrived at the interview in a badly wrinkled shirt.  他穿着一件皱得厉害的衬衫来面试。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 creased</td><td>/kriːst/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有折痕的，起皱的</td><td>a creased photograph<br>有折痕的照片</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She smoothed the creased photograph and placed it in the album.  她抚平那张有折痕的照片，放进相册。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 folded</td><td>/ˈfoʊldəd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>折叠的，对折的</td><td>a folded map<br>折叠的地图</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He pulled a folded map out of his back pocket.  他从后袋里抽出一张折叠的地图。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 smooth</td><td>/ˈsmuːð/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词、动词</td><td>温和的，圆滑的；平滑的</td><td>a smooth talker<br>说话圆滑的人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He is a smooth talker who never offends anyone in negotiations.  他谈判时言辞圆滑，从不得罪任何人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 9

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>cosmopolitan</b></td><td>/ˌkɑːzməˈpɑːlɪtən/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>有世界性眼光的，包容的</td><td>a cosmopolitan city<br>国际化的都市</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Years abroad gave her a cosmopolitan outlook her old classmates clearly lacked.  多年国外生活给了她老同学明显缺乏的世界性眼光。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 universal</td><td>/ˌjuːnəˈvɜːrsəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>普遍的，全体的</td><td>universal human rights<br>普世人权</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Music seems to have a universal appeal across all cultures.  音乐似乎在所有文化中都有普遍的感染力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 catholic</td><td>/ˈkæθlɪk/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>普遍的，包容的</td><td>catholic tastes<br>广泛的爱好</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her catholic tastes in music surprised everyone at the party.  她在音乐上广泛的爱好让聚会上每个人都惊讶。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 global</td><td>/ˈɡloʊbəl/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>全球的；全面的</td><td>the global economy<br>全球经济</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The pandemic reshaped the global economy in only two years.  疫情仅两年就重塑了全球经济。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 worldwide</td><td>/ˈwɜːrldwaɪd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词、副词</td><td>全世界的，遍及全球的</td><td>worldwide recognition<br>世界性的声誉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The film earned worldwide recognition after winning the festival prize.  这部影片获奖后赢得了世界性的声誉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 insular</td><td>/ˈɪnsələr/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>（观念、想法等）孤立狭隘的</td><td>an insular attitude<br>狭隘的态度</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His insular attitude toward foreign customs cost him many friendships abroad.  他对外国习俗的狭隘态度使他在国外失去了许多友谊。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 provincial</td><td>/prəˈvɪnʃl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>狭隘（的）</td><td>provincial tastes<br>狭隘的品味</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His provincial tastes made him suspicious of anything foreign or unfamiliar.  他狭隘的品味使他对一切外来或陌生的事物都怀疑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del><b>cosset</b></del></td><td>/ˈkɑːsɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>宠爱</td><td>cosset an only child<br>宠爱独生子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Grandparents tend to cosset the youngest child in the family.  祖父母往往宠爱家里最小的孩子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 caress</td><td>/kəˈres/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>轻抚，抚摸</td><td>caress the child's hair<br>轻抚孩子的头发</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She caressed the child's hair until he fell asleep.  她轻抚孩子的头发，直到他睡着。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cuddle</td><td>/ˈkʌdəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>拥抱，搂抱</td><td>cuddle the puppy<br>抱着小狗</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The little girl cuddled the puppy on the sofa all evening.  小女孩整晚在沙发上抱着小狗。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dote</td><td>/ˈdoʊt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>溺爱，宠爱</td><td>dote on her grandson<br>溺爱她的孙子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She dotes on her grandson and refuses him nothing.  她溺爱孙子，对他从不拒绝。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pet</td><td>/ˈpet/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>抚摸，宠爱；宠物</td><td>pet the cat gently<br>轻轻抚摸猫</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Visitors are allowed to pet the rabbits at the farm.  参观者可以在农场抚摸兔子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mollycoddle</td><td>/ˈmɑːlikɑːdl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>宠爱，溺爱</td><td>mollycoddle the students<br>溺爱学生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Some parents mollycoddle their children and leave them unprepared for real difficulties.  有些父母溺爱孩子，让他们无法应对真正的困难。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 coddle</td><td>/ˈkɑːdəl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>娇养，溺爱</td><td>coddle a spoiled child<br>娇养被宠坏的孩子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Coaches should not coddle athletes who refuse to train hard.  教练不该娇养那些不肯苦练的运动员。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 slight</td><td>/slaɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词、及物动词</td><td>轻微的，不重要的；轻视，怠慢</td><td>slight her contribution<br>轻视她的贡献</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He felt slighted when nobody mentioned his contribution during the ceremony.  典礼上无人提及他的贡献，他觉得受到了轻视。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>countenance</b></td><td>/ˈkaʊntənəns/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>容忍；赞成，推崇</td><td>countenance such behavior<br>容忍这种行为</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The headmaster would not countenance cheating of any kind in his school.  校长绝不容忍校内任何形式的作弊。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 abide</td><td>/əˈbaɪd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>容忍；遵守；持续</td><td>cannot abide dishonesty<br>不能容忍不诚实</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She cannot abide people who interrupt her while reading.  她无法容忍别人在她读书时打扰。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 endure</td><td>/ɪnˈdʊr/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>忍受；持续存在</td><td>endure great hardship<br>忍受巨大艰苦</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The settlers endured three winters without proper shelter.  移民们在没有像样住所的情况下熬过了三个冬天。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stand</td><td>/ˈstænd/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>容忍，忍受；站立</td><td>cannot stand the noise<br>受不了噪音</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 I cannot stand the noise from the construction site next door.  我受不了隔壁工地的噪音。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stomach</td><td>/ˈstʌmək/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>容忍</td><td>cannot stomach the insult<br>无法忍受这种侮辱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He could not stomach the insult and walked out of the meeting.  他无法忍受这种侮辱，径直退出会议。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 wear</td><td>/ˈwer/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>忍受，容忍；穿戴</td><td>will not wear that excuse<br>不会容忍那种借口</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The manager would not wear such a feeble excuse again.  经理不会再容忍这种苍白的借口。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 accept</td><td>/əkˈsept/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>接受，同意，承认</td><td>accept responsibility<br>承担责任</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She accepted the award on behalf of her entire research team.  她代表整个研究团队接受了这个奖项。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 favor</td><td>/ˈfeɪvər/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>赞同，好评；恩惠</td><td>win public favor<br>赢得公众好评</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The proposal quickly won favor among the younger members.  这项提议很快赢得年轻成员的支持。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 subscribe</td><td>/səbˈskraɪb/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>赞同（观点）；订阅</td><td>subscribe to a view<br>赞同某观点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 I do not subscribe to the view that money buys happiness.  我不赞同金钱能买到幸福的观点。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 disapprove</td><td>/ˌdɪsəˈpruːv/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>不赞成，反对</td><td>disapprove of the plan<br>不赞成该计划</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her parents strongly disapprove of the plan to drop out.  她父母强烈不赞成退学的计划。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 frown</td><td>/ˈfraʊn/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>不悦，不赞成；皱眉</td><td>frown on the practice<br>不赞成这种做法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Most universities frown on students sharing their assignments.  大多数大学不赞成学生互相分享作业。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>counterfeit</b></td><td>/ˈkaʊntərfɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词、及物动词</td><td>仿制的，假冒的；仿制</td><td>counterfeit banknotes<br>伪造的钞票</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Police seized thousands of counterfeit banknotes hidden in the delivery van.  警方缴获了藏在送货车里的数千张假钞。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 forged</td><td>/ˈfɔːrdʒd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>伪造的，假冒的</td><td>a forged signature<br>伪造的签名</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The bank noticed a forged signature on the withdrawal slip.  银行发现取款单上有伪造的签名。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bogus</td><td>/ˈboʊɡəs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>伪造的，假冒的</td><td>a bogus claim<br>虚假的索赔</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Police discovered a stack of bogus passports in the van.  警方在货车里发现一叠伪造护照。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 spurious</td><td>/ˈspjʊriəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>假的，伪造的</td><td>spurious claims<br>虚假的说法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The website spread spurious claims about the new vaccine.  该网站传播关于新疫苗的虚假说法。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pseudo</td><td>/ˈsuːdoʊ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>假的，冒充的</td><td>pseudo science<br>伪科学</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The book dresses up old superstitions as pseudo science.  这本书把旧迷信包装成伪科学。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 feigned</td><td>/ˈfeɪnd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>假装的，伪装的</td><td>feigned surprise<br>假装的惊讶</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He listened with feigned interest while checking his phone.  他一边看手机，一边假装感兴趣地听着。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 artificial</td><td>/ˌɑːrtəˈfɪʃəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>人造的；虚假的</td><td>artificial flowers<br>人造花</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The lobby was decorated with artificial flowers that never wilted.  大厅用永不枯萎的人造花装饰。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 phony</td><td>/ˈfoʊni/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>假的，冒充的；骗子</td><td>a phony accent<br>假装的口音</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He used a phony name when renting the small apartment.  他租那间小公寓时用了假名。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 copy</td><td>/ˈkɑːpi/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>复制品，仿制品；抄写</td><td>a cheap copy<br>廉价仿制品</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The painting on the wall is only a cheap copy.  墙上那幅画只是廉价的仿制品。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 forge</td><td>/fɔːrdʒ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>伪造；锻造，铸就</td><td>forge a passport<br>伪造护照</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was arrested for forging a passport and two visas.  他因伪造一本护照和两个签证而被捕。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 imitate</td><td>/ˈɪməˌteɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>模仿，仿效</td><td>imitate his accent<br>模仿他的口音</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Children naturally imitate the speech of adults around them.  孩子会自然地模仿身边大人的说话方式。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 simulate</td><td>/ˈsɪmjuleɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>模拟，假装</td><td>simulate battle conditions<br>模拟作战环境</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The training program can simulate battle conditions inside a quiet classroom.  该训练项目能在安静的教室里模拟作战环境。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mimic</td><td>/ˈmɪmɪk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词、形容词</td><td>模仿，仿效；仿造的</td><td>mimic human speech<br>模仿人类说话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Certain parrots can mimic human speech well enough to fool a casual listener.  某些鹦鹉能模仿人类说话，足以骗过不留意的听者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 authentic</td><td>/ɔːˈθentɪk/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>真实的，非仿造的</td><td>authentic Italian cuisine<br>正宗的意大利菜</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The museum confirmed the letter was an authentic original.  博物馆确认这封信是真实的原件。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 genuine</td><td>/ˈdʒenjəwən/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>真正的，真实的；真诚的</td><td>genuine leather<br>真皮</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Experts confirmed that the signature on the letter was genuine.  专家确认信上的签名是真的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>countermand</b></td><td>/ˌkaʊntərˈmænd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>取消，撤销</td><td>countermand an order<br>撤销一道命令</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The general countermanded the order before the troops reached the river.  将军在部队抵达河边之前撤销了那道命令。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 annul</td><td>/əˈnʌl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>宣告无效，取消</td><td>annul the marriage<br>宣告婚姻无效</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The court agreed to annul the marriage after only three weeks.  法院同意在仅仅三周之后宣告这段婚姻无效。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 repeal</td><td>/rɪˈpiːl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>撤销，废除（法律等）</td><td>repeal the old law<br>废除旧法律</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Lawmakers voted to repeal the outdated statute that had confused courts for decades.  议员们投票废除了这部困扰法院数十年的过时法规。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rescind</td><td>/rɪˈsɪnd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>废除，取消</td><td>rescind an offer<br>撤回报价</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company rescinded the job offer without explanation.  公司未加解释便撤回了聘用通知。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 revoke</td><td>/rɪˈvoʊk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>撤销，废除；吊销</td><td>revoke a license<br>吊销执照</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The court revoked his driving license after the second offense.  法院在他第二次违法后吊销了他的驾照。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cancel</td><td>/ˈkænsəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>取消，撤销；抵消</td><td>cancel the meeting<br>取消会议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They canceled the outdoor concert because of the heavy rain.  因为大雨，他们取消了露天音乐会。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 approve</td><td>/əˈpruːv/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>批准；赞成</td><td>approve the budget<br>批准预算</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The committee finally approved the plan after three long meetings.  委员会经过三次长会终于批准了该计划。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 permit</td><td>/pərˈmɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>允许，准许；许可证</td><td>permit access<br>允许进入</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The school does not permit phones during classroom hours.  学校不允许上课时间使用手机。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 sanction</td><td>/ˈsæŋkʃn/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>批准，同意，认可</td><td>sanction the new policy<br>批准这项新政策</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The board refused to sanction the plan without a detailed budget.  董事会拒绝在没有详细预算的情况下批准该计划。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>court</b></td><td>/kɔːrt/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>追求，献殷勤；法庭</td><td>court public favor<br>讨好公众</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The candidate spent the summer courting voters in small rural towns.  这位候选人整个夏天都在乡村小镇讨好选民。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 woo</td><td>/ˈwuː/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词、不及物动词</td><td>追求，求爱；争取支持</td><td>woo new voters<br>争取新选民</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He wooed her with flowers and long handwritten letters.  他用鲜花和长长的手写信追求她。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pursue</td><td>/pərˈsuː/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>追求，追寻；追捕</td><td>pursue a career<br>追求事业</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She pursued him for months before he finally agreed to dinner.  她追了他好几个月，他才终于答应共进晚餐。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 invite</td><td>/ɪnˈvaɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>邀请；招致，引来</td><td>invite public criticism<br>招致公众批评</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His careless remarks invited criticism from nearly every member present.  他轻率的言论招致了几乎所有在场成员的批评。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 spurn</td><td>/spɜːrn/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>摈弃，拒绝</td><td>spurn an offer<br>断然拒绝提议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She spurned the generous offer without giving any explanation at all.  她断然拒绝了这个优厚的提议，且不作任何解释。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 snub</td><td>/snʌb/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>轻视，不理睬</td><td>deliberately snub a colleague<br>故意冷落同事</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She deliberately snubbed her colleague by ignoring him at the meeting.  她在会上故意不理睬同事，冷落了他。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>covert</b></td><td>/ˈkoʊvərt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>隐蔽的，秘密的</td><td>covert operations<br>秘密行动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The agency ran covert operations in several neighboring countries.  该机构在数个邻国进行秘密行动。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cloistered</td><td>/ˈklɔɪstərd/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>隐居的，与世隔绝的</td><td>a cloistered life<br>隐居的生活</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He led a cloistered life among books, rarely meeting strangers.  他在书堆中过着与世隔绝的生活，很少见陌生人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hidden</td><td>/ˈhɪdən/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>隐藏的，隐蔽的</td><td>hidden costs<br>隐藏的费用</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The contract contained hidden fees no one had mentioned.  合同里含有没人提过的隐藏费用。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 secret</td><td>/ˈsiːkrət/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>秘密的，隐秘的；秘密</td><td>keep a secret<br>保守秘密</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They held secret meetings in the basement for several months.  他们在地下室秘密开会数月。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sheltered</td><td>/ˈʃeltərd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>受保护的，遮风避雨的</td><td>a sheltered childhood<br>受呵护的童年</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She grew up sheltered and knew little of the world outside.  她在呵护中长大，对外面的世界几乎一无所知。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 open</td><td>/ˈoʊpən/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>公开的，坦率的</td><td>open opposition<br>公开的反对</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 There was open opposition to the plan at the meeting.  会上有人公开反对这项计划。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 overt</td><td>/oʊˈvɜːrt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>明显的，公开的</td><td>overt hostility<br>公开的敌意</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The policy met overt hostility from local business owners.  该政策遭到当地商户的公开敌视。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 aboveboard</td><td>/əˈbʌvbɔːrd/</td><td>☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无欺诈的，光明正大的</td><td>open and aboveboard<br>公开而光明正大</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The whole deal was entirely open and aboveboard.  整笔交易完全是光明正大的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>covetous</b></td><td>/ˈkʌvətəs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>贪婪的，渴求财富的</td><td>covetous of his wealth<br>贪求他的财富</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He cast a covetous eye on his neighbor's newly bought tractor.  他贪婪地盯着邻居新买的拖拉机。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 acquisitive</td><td>/əˈkwɪzətɪv/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>贪婪的</td><td>an acquisitive society<br>物欲横流的社会</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The acquisitive collector bought every painting that came onto the market.  这位贪得无厌的收藏家买下了市面上出现的每一幅画。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 avaricious</td><td>/ˌævəˈrɪʃəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>贪财的，贪婪的</td><td>an avaricious landlord<br>贪财的房东</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The avaricious banker cared for nothing but the size of his bonus.  这个贪财的银行家只在乎奖金的多少。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 avid</td><td>/ˈævɪd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>急切渴望的，热衷的</td><td>an avid reader<br>热衷阅读的人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 My grandmother is an avid gardener even in winter.  我祖母即使在冬天也是个热衷园艺的人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rapacious</td><td>/rəˈpeɪʃəs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>过度贪婪的</td><td>rapacious business practices<br>贪婪的商业行径</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Rapacious business practices drove several small competitors out of the market.  贪婪的商业行径把几家小竞争者挤出了市场。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 grasping</td><td>/ˈɡræspɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>贪婪的，唯利是图的</td><td>a grasping moneylender<br>唯利是图的放贷人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His grasping relatives fought over the estate before the funeral ended.  他贪婪的亲戚在葬礼结束前就争夺遗产。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 easily-satiated</td><td>/ˈiːzəli ˈseɪʃieɪtɪd/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>容易满足的</td><td>an easily-satiated appetite<br>容易满足的胃口</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Unlike his greedy brother, he was easily satiated and asked for little.  与贪心的哥哥不同，他很容易满足，要求很少。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>cow</b></td><td>/kaʊ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>恐吓，威胁</td><td>cow them into obedience<br>威吓他们服从</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The sergeant's shouting failed to cow the new recruits into silence.  中士的吼叫没能把新兵威吓得沉默下来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 intimidate</td><td>/ɪnˈtɪmɪdeɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>威吓</td><td>intimidate the witness<br>威吓证人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The defendant's friends tried to intimidate the witness before the trial began.  被告的朋友试图在审判开始前威吓证人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 browbeat</td><td>/ˈbraʊbiːt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>恐吓</td><td>browbeat the witness<br>恐吓证人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The lawyer tried to browbeat the witness into changing her story.  律师试图恐吓证人改口。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bully</td><td>/ˈbʊli/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>欺凌弱小者，恶霸</td><td>stand up to a bully<br>勇敢面对恶霸</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new teacher quickly stopped the bully who terrorized younger children.  新老师很快制止了那个欺负小同学的恶霸。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
